Knowing the Self, Understanding the World: A Dual-Cognition Benchmark for UAV Spatio-temporal Reasoning with MLLMs
Authors: Like Liu, Zhengzheng Xu, Haitao He, Hongzhe Li, Shuchang Zhang, Dian Shao
First: 2026-07-17T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables. Project website: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models have achieved strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, yet their capabilities in UAV scenarios remain insufficiently explored. Recent UAV-oriented benchmarks have begun to evaluate MLLMs in aerial scenarios, but they typically focus on scene understanding, event recognition, or navigation completion, rather than jointly assessing the dual-cognition capability required for UAV agents: reasoning about both the UAV's own state and the external environment in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. To address this gap, we present UAV-DualCog, a benchmark for aerial multiview spatio-temporal reasoning built on this dual-cognition perspective. UAV-DualCog includes both image and video tasks to jointly evaluate self-state and environment-state reasoning, while requiring spatial or temporal grounding beyond discrete answer prediction. We also develop an automated pipeline that constructs data from scene-level semantic point clouds, yielding a scalable benchmark with diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Extensive evaluations show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable in UAV dual cognition. Self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization are persistent bottlenecks, and additional validation with thinking/frontier models and a human baseline confirms that the benchmark is understandable to humans but challenging for existing models. We further construct UAV-DualCog-Train from disjoint scenes and show through a lightweight optimization probe that it provides useful structured supervision, suggesting its value not only as an evaluation benchmark but also as a data resource for advancing MLLM-based UAV agents. Project website and supplementary materials: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com
中文标题/摘要
标题：知我知世：面向无人机时空推理的双认知能力评估基准
多模态大语言模型在各类视觉-语言任务中表现优异，但在无人机场景中的能力尚未充分探索。近期面向无人机的基准测试虽开始评估大语言模型在空中的表现，但多聚焦于场景理解、事件识别或导航完成，而非联合评估无人机代理所需的双认知能力：在多视角时空上下文中，同时推理自身状态与外部环境状态。为填补这一空白，我们提出UAV-DualCog基准，基于双认知视角构建面向空中多视角时空推理的评估体系。该基准包含图像与视频任务，联合评估自状态与环境状态推理能力，同时要求超越离散答案预测的时空定位。我们还开发自动化数据构建流水线，基于场景级语义点云生成数据，形成涵盖多样场景、数百地标与数千问答样本的可扩展基准。大量评估表明，当前大语言模型在无人机双认知任务中仍远未可靠。自状态推理、视角变换、精确空间定位与时间区间定位仍是持续瓶颈，结合思维/前沿模型与人类基线验证，确认该基准对人类而言可理解，对现有模型而言具挑战性。我们进一步构建UAV-DualCog-Train（基于不相交场景），并通过轻量优化探针验证其提供有效结构化监督，表明其不仅作为评估基准，亦可作为推动基于大语言模型的无人机代理发展的数据资源。项目网站与补充材料：https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in UAV scenarios beyond basic vision-language tasks, this work introduces UAV-DualCog, a benchmark designed to assess dual-cognition reasoning—simultaneously understanding the UAV’s own state and the external environment—in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. The benchmark combines image and video tasks requiring spatial and temporal grounding beyond discrete answers, and is constructed via an automated pipeline from semantic point clouds to yield diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Experimental results show current MLLMs perform poorly on self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization, with human baselines confirming the benchmark’s challenge and understandability. Additionally, UAV-DualCog-Train, built from disjoint scenes, proves useful as structured supervision through lightweight optimization, suggesting dual utility as both evaluation tool and training resource.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在无人机场景中的能力，本文提出UAV-DualCog基准，旨在评估无人机同时理解自身状态与外部环境的双认知推理能力，涵盖多视角时空场景。该基准结合图像与视频任务，要求超越离散答案的时空定位能力，并通过语义点云自动化构建，生成包含数百地标与数千问答样本的可扩展数据集。实验表明，当前MLLMs在自状态推理、视角转换、精确空间定位与时间区间定位上表现不佳，人类评估确认该基准具有挑战性且符合人类理解。此外，对UAV-DualCog-Train的轻量优化探针显示其可作为训练资源，表明此类双认知基准兼具评估与监督价值。
MotionForesight: Re-purposing Video Models for Future 3D Scene-Flow Prediction
Authors: Homanga Bharadhwaj, Yash Jangir
First: 2026-07-17T17:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:59:38+00:00
Comments: Website with visual results motionforesight.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humans can infer how objects are likely to move from passive observation: a cup may be lifted, a drawer may slide, and a lid may rotate shut. Such predictions expose the physical consequences of interaction needed to act in the real world. We study how to learn this anticipation from ordinary monocular videos of human-object interaction. Given a short observed video context, MotionForesight predicts future 3D trajectories for points on the manipulated object. This casts interaction prediction as object-centered 3D motion forecasting without any assumptions on the object properties. Our key insight is that video prediction models already encode rich priors about how objects move during human interactions. We redirect these priors from pixel prediction toward future 3D scene flow. We start from a dense 3D tracker built on a pretrained video model, generate pseudo-ground-truth tracks from complete clips, and train the forecaster using only the observed frames. We replace future RGB and geometry with learned mask latents and train a lightweight adapter to turn the retrospective tracking representation into a forward predictor, while freezing the large video and tracking components. Using just 40k human videos and no auxiliary inputs such as language, MotionForesight generalizes across diverse out-of-distribution objects, environments, viewpoints, and interactions. It also outperforms substantially larger models that use over a million training videos. These results show that we can efficiently re-purpose video priors into explicit geometric forecasts for embodied intelligence. https://motionforesight.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：MotionForesight：重用视频模型进行未来3D场景流预测
人类可以从被动观察中推断物体可能的运动方式：杯子可能被提起，抽屉可能滑动，盖子可能旋转关闭。此类预测揭示了交互所需的物理后果，以在现实世界中行动。我们研究如何从普通单目视频中学习这种预测。给定一段观察到的视频上下文，MotionForesight预测被操作物体上各点的未来3D轨迹。这将交互预测转化为以物体为中心的3D运动预测，无需对物体属性做任何假设。我们的核心见解是：视频预测模型已编码了丰富的关于物体在人类交互中如何运动的先验知识。我们将这些先验知识从像素预测转向未来3D场景流。我们从基于预训练视频模型构建的密集3D跟踪器出发，从完整视频片段生成伪真实轨迹，并仅使用观察帧训练预测器。我们用学习到的掩码潜在变量替代未来的RGB和几何信息，并训练一个轻量级适配器，将回顾性跟踪表示转化为前向预测器，同时冻结大型视频和跟踪组件。仅使用4万段人类视频且无需语言等辅助输入，MotionForesight可泛化至多样化的分布外物体、环境、视角和交互。它还显著优于使用超过百万训练视频的更大模型。这些结果表明，我们可以高效地将视频先验重用于具身智能的显式几何预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict how objects will move during human interactions from passive video observation, MotionForesight re-purposes existing video models to forecast future 3D scene flow without assuming object properties. It begins with a pretrained dense 3D tracker, generates pseudo-ground-truth trajectories from full video clips, and trains a lightweight adapter to convert retrospective tracking into forward motion prediction, while freezing the underlying video model. Evaluated on 40k human-object interaction videos without language or other auxiliary inputs, the model generalizes across unseen objects, environments, viewpoints, and actions, and outperforms larger models trained on over a million videos.
为从普通单目视频中预测人类交互时物体的未来运动，MotionForesight重新利用现有视频模型，无需假设物体属性即可预测未来3D场景流。其方法基于预训练的密集3D跟踪器，从完整视频片段生成伪真实轨迹，并训练一个轻量级适配器将回顾性跟踪转化为前瞻运动预测，同时冻结底层视频模型。在仅使用4万条人类交互视频且无额外输入的情况下，该方法在未见过的物体、环境和交互中表现优异，甚至超越了训练数据量超百万的更大模型。
FVAttn: Adaptive Sparse Attention with Runtime Load Balancing for Video Generation
Authors: Hao Liu, Chenghuan Huang, Ye Huang, Zhiying Wen, Hao Liu, Mohan Zhang, Chen Li, Ziyang Ma, Jing Lyu, Jiangsu Du
First: 2026-07-17T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:59:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video Diffusion Transformers process long spatio-temporal sequences, making self-attention the main bottleneck in high-resolution video generation. Training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, but adaptive Top-$p$ routing creates uneven per-head workloads under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. The resulting workload heterogeneity turns sparse attention into a rank-level straggler problem. We present \method{}, a training-free sparse-attention system that improves the distributed execution efficiency of adaptive sparse attention under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. \method{} uses Top-$p$ routing, a Top-$k$ safety floor, and video-aware block organization as the sparse-routing frontend, then repairs the materialized mask at runtime. Runtime Load Balancing migrates a small number of heavy heads via P2P communication to shorten the current critical path. Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation fills residual non-critical-rank slack with additional high-value blocks, while overlap hides scheduling and migration overhead behind existing computation. On step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V, \method{} reduces average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08 and delivers a $4.41\times$ attention speedup over FlashAttention, while achieving a $2.02$--$2.11\times$ DiT inference speedup with competitive video quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FVAttn：面向视频生成的自适应稀疏注意力与运行时负载均衡
视频扩散Transformer处理长时空序列，使自注意力成为高分辨率视频生成的主要瓶颈。无训练稀疏注意力可降低该成本，但自适应Top-p路由在多GPU序列并行下导致各头负载不均。由此产生的负载异质性使稀疏注意力演变为秩级瓶颈问题。我们提出FVAttn，一种无训练稀疏注意力系统，提升多GPU序列并行下自适应稀疏注意力的分布式执行效率。FVAttn采用Top-p路由、Top-k安全底线及视频感知块组织作为稀疏路由前端，再在运行时修复材料化掩码。运行时负载均衡通过P2P通信迁移少量重负载头以缩短当前关键路径。空闲感知稀疏增强利用非关键秩的空闲资源填充高价值块，同时重叠隐藏调度与迁移开销于现有计算之后。在步进蒸馏的Wan2.2 I2V模型上，FVAttn将平均负载不均衡从1.34降至1.08，相较FlashAttention实现4.41倍注意力加速，同时在视频质量相当前提下，实现2.02至2.11倍DiT推理加速。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational bottleneck of self-attention in high-resolution video generation, FVAttn introduces a training-free sparse attention system optimized for multi-GPU sequence parallelism. It combines Top-p routing with a Top-k safety floor and video-aware block organization to manage attention sparsity, then dynamically repairs masks at runtime. To balance workload heterogeneity, it employs Runtime Load Balancing to migrate heavy attention heads via P2P communication and Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation to utilize idle computation cycles with high-value blocks, hiding overhead through overlap. Experiments on step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V show a reduction in average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08, a 4.41x speedup over FlashAttention, and a 2.02–2.11x inference speedup for DiT with comparable video quality.
为解决高分辨率视频生成中自注意力的计算瓶颈，FVAttn提出一种无需训练的稀疏注意力系统，专为多GPU序列并行优化。该系统结合Top-p路由、Top-k安全底线和视频感知块组织管理稀疏性，并在运行时动态修复掩码。为缓解负载不均，通过P2P通信迁移部分重负载注意力头，并利用空闲计算周期填充高价值块，通过重叠隐藏开销。在步进蒸馏的Wan2.2 I2V上，负载不均从1.34降至1.08，相比FlashAttention实现4.41倍注意力加速，DiT推理速度提升2.02–2.11倍，视频质量保持竞争力。
Searching Videos as Trees: Self-Correcting Agents for Grounded Long Video QA
Authors: Ce Zhang, Ziyang Wang, Yulu Pan, Oluwatumininu Oguntola, Pranav Wagh, Qiyu Wu, Hiromi Wakaki, Mohit Bansal, Gedas Bertasius
First: 2026-07-17T17:59:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:59:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Grounded long-video question answering (Grounded LVQA) requires answering a question about a long video while localizing the short evidence interval that supports the answer. Recent agentic methods frame this task as multi-turn exploration with a single crop_video(start, end) action, which supports coarse-to-fine narrowing but provides no primitive for fine-to-coarse backtracking. As a result, these agents typically converge prematurely and cannot recover from an early mistake. We propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a framework that casts grounded LVQA as iterative self-correcting search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS constructs a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries so that each node corresponds to a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate the tree through four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. These operations expose backtracking and recovery as explicit, learnable primitives rather than implicit behaviors. To train this navigation, we introduce a trajectory synthesis pipeline that produces multi-step paths through the tree, including deliberate detours into incorrect branches followed by recovery. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with grounding and answer-accuracy rewards. On three Grounded LVQA benchmarks (CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D), VTS outperforms the strongest prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU on CG-Bench and +7.4 T-F1 on Haystack-Ego4D. The learned policy also transfers to general long-video QA, surpassing all prior agentic baselines on Video-MME, MLVU, and LVBench by up to +7.1 accuracy points. Ablations confirm that self-correcting hierarchical search is the central mechanism behind these gains: removing either adaptive descent or explicit backtracking substantially degrades performance. Code is available at https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视频搜索如树：用于接地长视频问答的自纠正代理
接地长视频问答（Grounded LVQA）要求在回答长视频问题的同时定位支持答案的短证据区间。近期代理方法将该任务视为多轮探索，仅通过单次crop_video(start, end)操作，支持粗到细的缩小，但缺乏细到粗的回溯机制。因此，这些代理通常过早收敛，无法从早期错误中恢复。我们提出VideoTreeSearch（VTS），将接地LVQA框架化为在自适应时间树上的迭代自纠正搜索。VTS从视觉场景边界构建非均匀树，使每个节点对应语义连贯的片段，并训练代理通过四种离散操作（zoom_in、zoom_out、shift、answer）在树上导航。这些操作将回溯与恢复显式化为可学习的原始操作，而非隐式行为。为训练导航，我们引入轨迹合成管道，生成树中的多步路径，包括故意进入错误分支后恢复的迂回路径。我们使用这些轨迹进行监督微调，随后结合接地与答案准确率奖励进行强化学习。在三个Grounded LVQA基准（CG-Bench、Haystack-LVBench、Haystack-Ego4D）上，VTS在CG-Bench上比最强的先前代理方法高出+12.5 mIoU，在Haystack-Ego4D上高出+7.4 T-F1。所学策略也迁移到通用长视频问答，于Video-MME、MLVU和LVBench上超越所有先前代理基线，最高提升+7.1准确率。消融实验确认自纠正分层搜索是性能提升的核心机制：移除自适应下降或显式回溯会显著降低性能。代码见https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing agentic methods that lack fine-to-coarse backtracking and thus converge prematurely in grounded long-video QA, the authors propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a self-correcting framework that models the task as iterative search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS builds a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries, where each node represents a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate via four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. Training uses a trajectory synthesis pipeline that generates multi-step paths including deliberate detours and recovery, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with grounding and accuracy rewards. Experiments on CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, and Haystack-Ego4D show VTS outperforms prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU and +7.4 T-F1, and transfers to general long-video QA with up to +7.1 accuracy gains, with ablations confirming the critical role of hierarchical self-correction.
为解决现有代理方法因缺乏细粒度回溯能力而容易过早收敛的问题，作者提出VideoTreeSearch（VTS）框架，将接地长视频问答建模为在自适应时间树上的迭代自纠正搜索。VTS根据视觉场景边界构建非均匀树，每个节点对应语义连贯的片段，并训练代理通过四种离散操作（放大、缩小、平移、回答）进行导航。训练采用轨迹合成管道生成包含错误分支探索与恢复的多步路径，结合监督微调与强化学习（奖励为定位准确性和答案正确性）。在三个基准测试中，VTS在CG-Bench上提升12.5 mIoU，在Haystack-Ego4D上提升7.4 T-F1，且在通用长视频问答任务中超越所有现有代理基线最多7.1个准确率点，消融实验证实层级自纠正机制是性能提升的核心。
Handroid: Bridging Dexterous Hand and Humanoid
Authors: Ruogu Li, Chenyang Ma, Sikai Li, Zhenyu Wei, Yunchao Yao, Haochen Shi, C. Karen Liu, Shuran Song, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-07-17T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:59:05+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://handroid.org
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dexterous hands and humanoid robots are typically developed as distinct embodiments: the former enable contact-rich manipulation at the object scale, whereas the latter provide mobility and whole-body interaction in human-centered environments. We introduce \textbf{Handroid}, a desktop-scale dual-embodiment robot that integrates both capabilities within a single reconfigurable platform. Handroid reuses one 27-DoF electromechanical body as either a dexterous hand or a desktop humanoid, measuring 0.33 m in height and 2.05 kg in weight. In the dexterous hand embodiment, 20 DoFs form an anthropomorphic hand closely matching the kinematic structure of the human hand. In the humanoid embodiment, the same articulated modules are reconfigured into a humanoid with a head, arms, and legs, including a 12-DoF lower-limb structure for locomotion and whole-body motion. Handroid further provides a unified control and learning framework supporting hand teleoperation, dexterous grasping, in-hand manipulation, humanoid locomotion, gait generation, and interactive motion authoring. We validate the platform through real-world dexterous manipulation, reinforcement-learning-based locomotion, keyframe motion deployment, and a long-horizon task involving embodiment reconfiguration, locomotion, docking, and dexterous pick-and-place. These results position Handroid as a compact and reproducible platform for advancing morphology-reconfigurable robotics and cross-embodiment robot learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Handroid：连接灵巧手与人形机器人的桥梁
灵巧手和人形机器人通常作为独立的实体开发：前者支持物体尺度上的接触密集型操作，后者则提供人类中心环境中的移动性和全身交互能力。我们提出了\textbf{Handroid}，一种桌面尺度的双体态机器人，将这两种能力整合于单一可重构平台上。Handroid 重用一个27自由度的机电一体身体，可作为灵巧手或桌面人形机器人使用，高度0.33米，重量2.05公斤。在灵巧手形态下，20个自由度构成与人手运动学结构高度相似的仿生手；在人形机器人形态下，相同的关节模块重新配置为人形，包含头部、手臂和腿部，其中下肢为12自由度结构，支持移动与全身运动。Handroid 还提供统一的控制与学习框架，支持手部遥操作、灵巧抓取、手内操作、人形移动、步态生成及交互式运动创作。我们通过真实世界灵巧操作、基于强化学习的移动、关键帧运动部署以及涉及体态重构、移动、对接和灵巧抓取放置的长时任务验证了该平台。这些成果使 Handroid 成为推进形态可重构机器人与跨体态机器人学习的紧凑且可复现平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge the functional gap between dexterous hands and humanoid robots, the authors introduce Handroid, a single reconfigurable platform that can operate as either a 20-DoF dexterous hand or a desktop humanoid with a 12-DoF lower limb. The system uses a shared 27-DoF electromechanical body and a unified control framework to support tasks ranging from teleoperation and in-hand manipulation to locomotion and motion authoring. Experimental results demonstrate its capability in real-world dexterous manipulation, reinforcement learning-based walking, keyframe motion deployment, and a long-horizon task requiring embodiment switching, locomotion, docking, and pick-and-place, validating its potential as a compact, reproducible platform for morphology-reconfigurable robotics and cross-embodiment learning.
为弥合灵巧手与人形机器人功能上的鸿沟，本文提出Handroid，一个可重构的桌面级机器人，通过同一套27自由度机电结构，可切换为20自由度灵巧手或12自由度人形形态。平台支持统一控制与学习框架，可执行遥操作、抓取、手内操作、移动、步态生成及动作创作。实验结果验证其在真实灵巧操作、强化学习驱动的移动、关键帧动作部署及长时任务（需形态切换、移动、对接与灵巧抓取）中的能力，确立其为紧凑、可复现的形态可重构机器人与跨形态学习平台。
PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization
Authors: Yuchen Yang, Yifan Zhao, Anisha Dasgupta, Sasa Misailovic
First: 2026-07-17T17:58:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:58:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PagedWeight：基于动态质量感知权重量化高效MoE大语言模型服务
专家混合（MoE）是流行的大语言模型（LLM）类别，提供高效率和准确性。然而，在KV缓存密集型服务场景中，MoE模型常面临模型权重GPU内存需求与不断增长的KV缓存之间的张力。我们提出PagedWeight，一种新颖的MoE LLM服务管理方法，可在运行时动态量化MoE模型权重，平衡专家权重精度与KV缓存大小。PagedWeight揭示并有效应对模型任务准确性、内存消耗与吞吐量/延迟之间的复杂权衡。在多个内存敏感的MoE服务场景中，PagedWeight在多个现有量化基线方法上改善了质量-内存权衡。PagedWeight在保持FP16等效精度的同时，实现最高72.0%的GPU内存节省和1.94倍吞吐量提升，并在内存预算相似的情况下，通过最多4.1%的吞吐量损失，将质量提升最高39.3%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory pressure in KV-cache-heavy MoE LLM serving, PagedWeight introduces a dynamic, quality-aware weight quantization method that adapts expert weight precision at runtime to balance memory usage and model accuracy. It selectively quantizes weights based on their contribution to task performance, thereby reducing GPU memory consumption without sacrificing throughput or latency. Experimental results show that PagedWeight achieves FP16-level accuracy with up to 72.0% memory savings and 1.94x throughput gain, and can improve model quality by up to 39.3% over existing quantization methods while incurring at most 4.1% throughput loss.
为缓解KV缓存密集型MoE大模型服务中的显存压力，PagedWeight提出一种动态、质量感知的权重量化方法，在运行时根据专家权重对任务性能的影响选择性量化，从而在保持精度的同时降低显存占用。实验表明，该方法可实现与FP16相当的精度，最高节省72.0%显存并提升1.94倍吞吐量，同时在显存预算相近时，相比量化基线方法质量提升最高达39.3%，仅损失最多4.1%吞吐量。
A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing
Authors: Owen Lockwood, Jérémy Béjanin, Joost Bus, Christopher Chamberland, Patrick Huembeli, Frank Schäfer, Guillaume Verdon
First: 2026-07-17T17:57:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:57:49+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于平衡态可微连续变量热力学计算的蓝图
为应对机器学习工作负载日益增长的能量与延迟需求，我们提出了一种高效且快速的热力学计算堆栈蓝图，该堆栈利用物理硬件中的随机模拟过程。本研究聚焦于基于能量的热力学计算，其中随机过程可用具有可调能量势的朗之万动力学良好描述。在物理硬件中实现这些势能，使我们能够生成并采样基础参数化能量模型。我们展示了如何基于这些硬件原生的能量模型，利用概率图模型框架构建和训练流行机器学习模型。我们基于理论分析和数值研究，分析了该热力学范式下不同模型的运行时间和能耗。作为此类硬件的初步实验实现，我们展示了由热噪声驱动的随机模拟超导电路。这些结果共同勾勒出一条面向概率机器学习的节能热力学硬件的发展路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rising energy and latency demands of machine learning, this work proposes a thermodynamic computing framework using stochastic analog processes governed by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials, implemented in physical hardware to enable efficient sampling from energy-based models. The method constructs and trains machine learning models via probabilistic graphical models built on these hardware-native potentials, allowing direct mapping of neural networks to energy landscapes. Experimental results using stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise show that this paradigm achieves energy-efficient computation, with runtime and energy consumption analyzed both theoretically and numerically, demonstrating feasibility for probabilistic machine learning.
为应对机器学习任务日益增长的能耗与延迟需求，本文提出一种基于热力学的计算框架，利用受可控势能函数调控的朗之万动力学随机模拟过程，通过物理硬件实现能量模型的采样。该方法基于概率图模型，将主流机器学习模型构建于硬件原生的能量函数之上，支持通过平衡态微分实现梯度学习。实验采用热噪声驱动的随机模拟超导电路，理论与数值分析表明该范式可实现高效能计算，为概率机器学习提供可行的硬件路径。
Vision-Language Assistant for Emotional Reactions to Risky Driving
Authors: Harine Choi, Eun Hak Lee, Zhengzhong Tu
First: 2026-07-17T17:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:57:27+00:00
Comments: Transportation Research Record. Advance online publication (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study introduces a vision-language pipeline that detects risky driving behaviors and generates emotionally expressive responses to support driver awareness and comfort. Although vision-language models have advanced perception and reasoning in autonomous driving, existing systems rarely consider the emotional dimension or real-world user experience. Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA) detects high-risk driving maneuvers in real time, such as sudden cut-ins. It then produces emotional responses through a large language model tailored to driver preferences. The framework comprises two core modules. The vision module uses YOLOv8 variants to detect nearby vehicles and identify risky behaviors such as sudden cut-ins. Key driving metrics, including relative distance, speed, and projected reach time, are extracted and normalized to produce a structured behavior log. The language module processes this log with user-defined emotional tone settings, such as neutral, humorous, and analytical, and generates verbal reactions using state-of-the-art large language models, including ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, and Copilot. We evaluated the proposed system using dashcam videos containing risky driving behaviors and a user study involving 108 participants. Participants selected preferred response styles, and the large language models were evaluated based on emotional alignment. All models received favorable ratings, although preferences varied across personas. Notably, the combination of YOLOv8s and ChatGPT-4o achieved the highest score of 4.29 out of 5.00. By integrating real-world perception with emotionally adaptive dialogue, KYA introduces a new paradigm for emotionally intelligent in-vehicle artificial intelligence. It offers promising directions for improving safety, trust, and emotional well-being in both conventional and autonomous vehicles.

The vision module extracts key metrics like distance, speed, and projected reach time to construct a structured behavior log, which the language module then processes with user-defined emotional tones—such as neutral, humorous, or analytical—to generate verbal reactions. Cluster-Aware Matching via Laplacian Optimal Transport
Authors: Gabriel Samberg, YoonHaeng Hur, Yuehaw Khoo, Nir Sharon
First: 2026-07-17T17:56:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:56:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In many applications of matching, the point clouds to be matched are not merely unstructured sets of points but rather samples from distributions with an intrinsic cluster structure. In such cases, as individual points are often interchangeable within a coherent region, finding a robust region-to-region alignment is more desirable than establishing a precise point-to-point correspondence. To this end, we propose a novel approach for cluster-aware matching based on Laplacian Optimal Transport (LapOT). The key idea is to regularize the optimal transport problem with quadratic Laplacian terms constructed from similarity graphs of the point clouds, which encourages the optimal coupling to respect the cluster structure of both point sets. We also introduce Refined Simultaneous Clustering (RSC), a method that leverages the cluster-aware coupling obtained from LapOT to produce consistent partitions across the point sets, which can overcome the limitations of independent clustering and yield more stable and interpretable results. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through theoretical analysis and empirical experiments, showing that LapOT indeed produces cluster-aware matching that leads to more consistent and meaningful alignments between point clouds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于拉普拉斯最优传输的聚类感知匹配
在许多匹配应用中，待匹配的点云并非简单的无结构点集，而是来自具有内在聚类结构的概率分布的样本。在这种情况下，由于单个点在连贯区域内往往可互换，寻找稳健的区域到区域对齐比建立精确的点到点对应更可取。为此，我们提出了一种基于拉普拉斯最优传输（LapOT）的聚类感知匹配新方法。其核心思想是通过从点云相似性图构建的二次拉普拉斯项对最优传输问题进行正则化，从而鼓励最优耦合尊重两个点集的聚类结构。我们还引入了精炼同步聚类（RSC）方法，利用LapOT获得的聚类感知耦合，生成点集间一致的划分，从而克服独立聚类的局限性，获得更稳定且可解释的结果。我们通过理论分析和实验证明，LapOT确实能产生聚类感知匹配，实现点云间更一致且有意义的对齐。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to align point clouds that inherently possess cluster structures rather than treating them as random point sets, this work introduces Laplacian Optimal Transport (LapOT) to enforce cluster-aware matching. The method regularizes the optimal transport problem using quadratic Laplacian terms derived from similarity graphs, encouraging the transport plan to preserve regional groupings. It further integrates Refined Simultaneous Clustering (RSC) to generate consistent cluster partitions across point sets, improving upon independent clustering. Experimental results confirm that LapOT yields more stable and semantically meaningful alignments by respecting intrinsic cluster structures, outperforming standard point-to-point matching methods.
为解决点云匹配中固有聚类结构未被充分考虑的问题，本文提出基于拉普拉斯最优传输（LapOT）的聚类感知匹配方法。该方法通过从点云相似性图构建的二次拉普拉斯项对最优传输问题进行正则化，促使传输方案尊重两组点云的聚类结构。进一步引入精炼同步聚类（RSC）方法，利用LapOT获得的聚类感知耦合生成一致划分，克服独立聚类的局限性。实验结果表明，LapOT能有效实现更稳定、更具解释性的点云对齐，凸显其在保留内在聚类结构方面的优势。
Beyond Success Rate: Cost-Aware Evaluation of Offensive and Defensive Security Agents
Authors: Paul Kassianik, Blaine Nelson, Yaron Singer
First: 2026-07-16T17:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:56:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Security-agent evaluations commonly measure peak offensive capability under generous inference budgets, emphasizing vulnerability discovery, exploit development, penetration testing, and CTF completion. Such measurements are useful but incomplete: in operational security, every reasoning step, tool call, telemetry query, and enrichment request consumes budget. We evaluate language-model security agents through this cost-success lens on offensive Cybench challenges and defensive Splunk BOTS v1 investigation challenges. Instead of reporting only best-case success, we compare models at fixed cost levels and decompose performance by inference spend and tool spend. Our results show distinct scalingregimes for red- and blue-team tasks. Offensive CTF performance improves with additional test-time compute, and scaled open-weight models can approach frontier proprietary systems while remaining cost-competitive. Defensive SOC investigation does not scale in the same way: success depends more heavily on disciplined tool use, telemetry navigation, and selective enrichment than on raw reasoning budget alone. We argue that security-agent benchmarks should measure economic efficiency and operational fit alongside task success. Cost-aware, SOC-native evaluations provide a clearer picture of which models are practically useful today and where defensive agents still need to improve. We present an interactive website with our results https://evals.frontier.security.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越成功率：面向攻击与防御安全代理的成本感知评估
安全代理评估通常在宽松的推理预算下测量其峰值攻击能力，强调漏洞发现、漏洞利用开发、渗透测试和CTF完成。此类测量虽有用但不完整：在实际安全运营中，每一步推理、工具调用、遥测查询和丰富请求均消耗预算。我们通过成本-成功率视角，在攻击性Cybench挑战和防御性Splunk BOTS v1调查挑战中评估语言模型安全代理。我们不仅报告最佳情况下的成功率，还比较固定成本水平下的模型表现，并按推理开销和工具开销分解性能。结果表明，红队与蓝队任务具有不同的扩展规律：攻击性CTF表现随测试时计算量增加而提升，扩展后的开源模型可接近前沿专有系统且保持成本竞争力；而防御性SOC调查则不遵循相同规律：其成功更依赖于工具使用纪律、遥测导航和选择性丰富，而非单纯推理预算。我们认为，安全代理基准应同时衡量经济效率与实际运营适配度。成本感知、原生SOC评估能更清晰地揭示当前哪些模型实用，以及防御代理仍需改进之处。我们提供交互式网站展示结果：https://evals.frontier.security。

鉴于现有安全代理评估常忽略实际运行成本，本研究从成本与成功率的双重角度评估攻防安全代理，通过固定推理与工具预算衡量模型表现。在Cybench攻防挑战与Splunk BOTS v1防御调查任务中，作者不仅关注成功率，更分解模型在推理与工具消耗上的效率。结果表明，进攻型代理随计算资源增加而性能提升，开源模型可逼近商业前沿系统且成本可控；而防御型代理则更依赖工具使用策略与日志导航，而非单纯推理预算，凸显当前防御代理在成本敏感场景下仍需改进，呼吁评估体系应纳入经济效率与实战适配性考量。
Physics-enhanced reinforcement learning for real-time optimal control of dynamical systems
Authors: Matteo Tomasetto, Nicolò Botteghi, Gabriele Bruni, Andrea Manzoni
First: 2026-07-17T17:56:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:56:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising feedback control strategy for nonlinear and complex dynamical systems. However, RL algorithms are sample inefficient and require a large number of interaction with the environment to synthesize optimal control strategies. Consequently, applications of RL are typically limited to sparse sensors and actuators due to the curse of dimensionality entailed by the exploration-exploitation dilemma in high-dimensional spaces. In this work, we bridge RL and traditional optimal control for dynamical system with a novel Physics-EnhAnced Reinforcement Learning (PEARL) paradigm tailored to the control of high-dimensional and parametric dynamical systems, exploiting the differentibility of their dynamics. Specifically, PEARL employs an actor-adjoint algorithm that leverages automatic differentiation to compute policy gradients over short horizons and adjoint-based sensitivities of future returns approximated via neural networks, significantly reducing the number of environment interactions, while mitigating long-term gradient instabilities. Through two challenging parametric navigation problems in unsteady flows, we show that PEARL (i) effectively exploits differentiable environments to outperform state-of-the-art RL algorithms, (ii) is sample efficient, thanks to the physics-guided policy learning, (iii) generalizes across multiple scenarios, which is crucial when dealing with parametric systems, and (iv) enables scaling RL to high-dimensional state and action spaces, without requiring low-dimensional state representations or multi-agent strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理增强的强化学习用于动态系统的实时最优控制
强化学习（RL）近期已成为非线性和复杂动态系统的有前景的反馈控制策略。然而，RL算法样本效率低，需要大量与环境交互才能合成最优控制策略。因此，RL的应用通常受限于稀疏传感器和执行器，这是由高维空间中探索-利用困境所导致的维度灾难。本研究通过一种新颖的物理增强强化学习（PEARL）范式，将RL与传统动态系统的最优控制相结合，专门针对高维和参数化动态系统的控制，利用其动力学的可微性。具体而言，PEARL采用一种actor-adjoint算法，利用自动微分计算短时域内的策略梯度，并通过神经网络近似未来回报的伴随敏感度，显著减少环境交互次数，同时缓解长期梯度不稳定性。通过两个在非定常流中的挑战性参数化导航问题，我们证明PEARL：(i) 有效利用可微环境，优于现有最先进的RL算法；(ii) 样本效率高，得益于物理引导的策略学习；(iii) 能够在多个场景中泛化，这对于处理参数化系统至关重要；(iv) 无需低维状态表示或多智能体策略，即可将RL扩展到高维状态和动作空间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sample inefficiency of standard reinforcement learning in high-dimensional dynamical systems, this work introduces Physics-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning (PEARL), a framework that integrates differentiable physics into RL to reduce environmental interactions. PEARL uses an actor-adjoint algorithm combining automatic differentiation for short-horizon policy gradients with neural network approximations of adjoint-based return sensitivities, stabilizing long-term learning. Experiments on parametric navigation in unsteady flows demonstrate that PEARL outperforms state-of-the-art RL methods, achieves superior sample efficiency, generalizes across scenarios, and scales to high-dimensional state-action spaces without relying on low-dimensional representations or multi-agent setups.
为解决传统强化学习在高维动态系统中样本效率低的问题，本文提出物理增强强化学习（PEARL）框架，通过actor-adjoint算法将可微分物理融入强化学习。该方法利用自动微分计算短时策略梯度，并用神经网络近似长期回报敏感度，从而减少环境交互次数并稳定梯度。在非稳态流体中的参数化导航实验中，PEARL表现优于现有方法，具备高样本效率、跨场景泛化能力，且无需低维状态表示或多智能体策略即可扩展至高维状态-动作空间。
Evaluating Open-Weight LLMs for Generating Structured Threat Information for Autonomous Vehicle Vulnerabilities
Authors: Md Erfan, Ahmed Ryan, Md Kamal Hossain Chowdhury, Md Rayhanur Rahman
First: 2026-07-17T17:55:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:55:19+00:00
Comments: 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) rely on interconnected software and hardware components, including sensors, Electronic Control Units, in-vehicle infotainment systems, and telematics units, where vulnerabilities can compromise assets, users, and vehicle operations. These vulnerabilities are commonly documented as plain text in the Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database; however, security practitioners require structured information about affected assets, types of weaknesses, and attack behaviors to effectively mitigate the risks from these vulnerabilities. To this end, we evaluate open-weight Large Language Models (LLMs) for generating Structured Threat Information Expression (STIX), a well-known structured format for representing threat information, for CAV-related CVEs. We construct a dataset called CAV-STIXGen that maps CAV vulnerability descriptions to STIX domain objects (SDO), STIX relationship objects (SRO), Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), and MITRE ATT&CK techniques mappings. Using this dataset, we evaluated 11 open-weight LLMs (4B to 120B parameters) across various prompting strategies and temperatures. Single-model configurations achieve F1 scores of 0.94 for SDO, 0.63 for SRO, and 0.99 for CWE mapping, while complete MITRE ATT&CK mapping remains challenging. In a multi-agent setup, Gemma-4-31B and Codestral-22B achieve F1 scores of 0.91 for SDOs and 0.43 for SROs, respectively. Lastly, we analyze CWE and MITRE ATT&CK co-occurrences to identify recurring threat patterns in the CAV domain, demonstrating how AI-assisted vulnerability-to-STIX translation can automate threat intelligence and prioritize defense in transportation security.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估开放权重大语言模型生成自动驾驶汽车漏洞结构化威胁信息的能力
联网自动驾驶汽车（CAV）依赖相互连接的软硬件组件，包括传感器、电子控制单元、车载信息娱乐系统和车载通信单元，其漏洞可能危及资产、用户和车辆运行。这些漏洞通常以纯文本形式记录在通用漏洞和披露（CVE）数据库中；但安全从业者需要关于受影响资产、弱点类型和攻击行为的结构化信息，以便有效缓解相关风险。为此，我们评估了开放权重大语言模型（LLM）生成结构化威胁信息表达（STIX）的能力，STIX是表示威胁信息的知名结构化格式，用于CAV相关CVE。我们构建了名为CAV-STIXGen的数据集，将CAV漏洞描述映射到STIX领域对象（SDO）、STIX关系对象（SRO）、常见弱点枚举（CWE）和MITRE ATT&CK技术映射。使用该数据集，我们评估了11个开放权重LLM（4B至120B参数）在不同提示策略和温度下的表现。单模型配置在SDO、SRO和CWE映射上的F1分数分别为0.94、0.63和0.99，而完整MITRE ATT&CK映射仍具挑战性。在多智能体设置中，Gemma-4-31B和Codestral-22B分别在SDO和SRO映射上达到0.91和0.43的F1分数。最后，我们分析CWE与MITRE ATT&CK的共现情况，以识别CAV领域中的重复威胁模式，展示AI辅助漏洞到STIX翻译如何自动化威胁情报并优先处理交通领域的防御。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for structured threat intelligence to address vulnerabilities in Connected and Autonomous Vehicles, this study evaluates open-weight LLMs for generating STIX-formatted threat data from plain-text CVE descriptions. Using a custom dataset (CAV-STIXGen) mapping CVEs to STIX objects, CWEs, and MITRE ATT&CK techniques, the researchers tested 11 LLMs ranging from 4B to 120B parameters under varied prompting and temperature settings. Single models achieved high F1 scores—0.94 for STIX Domain Objects, 0.63 for STIX Relationship Objects, and 0.99 for CWE mapping—while MITRE ATT&CK mapping remained difficult. 为应对联网自动驾驶汽车（CAV）中漏洞带来的安全风险，本研究评估了开源大语言模型（LLM）从CVE描述生成结构化威胁情报（STIX格式）的能力。研究人员构建了名为CAV-STIXGen的新数据集，将CAV漏洞映射至STIX域对象、关系对象、CWE及MITRE ATT&CK技术。在11个参数规模从4B至120B的模型中，单模型配置在STIX域对象、关系对象和CWE映射上分别取得0.94、0.63和0.99的F1分数，但MITRE ATT&CK映射仍具挑战性。多智能体配置下，Gemma-4-31B和Codestral-22B在域对象映射上分别达到0.91和0.43。CWE与MITRE ATT&CK共现分析揭示了CAV领域中的重复性威胁模式，表明AI辅助的漏洞到STIX转换可自动化威胁情报并优化交通安全防御优先级。
Vision-Language-Motion Maps: An Open-Vocabulary, Uncertainty-Aware, Queryable Motion Attribute for 3D Scene Maps
Authors: Dibyendu Ghosh, Ayushi Shakya
First: 2026-07-17T17:53:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:53:13+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Submitted to IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-vocabulary 3D maps let robots answer language queries about what and where, but they assume a static world and cannot answer queries about how scene elements behave. We introduce Vision-Language-Motion Maps (VLMM), an open-vocabulary, natural-language-queryable 3D map in which each element carries a fused motion attribute: a VLM/LLM semantic movability prior combined with geometrically observed cross-frame motion, together with a per-element uncertainty. Queries reduce to attribute filters that distinguish what has been seen to move, what could move but has not, and what stays still. On a controlled simulator benchmark with exact ground truth (AI2-THOR, three scene types) we show through ablation that the schema fields are non-substitutable: a semantic-only baseline fails motion queries even with strong features, and neither motion field substitutes for the other (the prior cannot answer "what is moving," observed motion cannot answer "what could move"). On real dynamic RGB-D (TUM and Bonn, six sequences) we show the uncertainty channel-our key difference from prior fused-motion work-consistently improves moving-vs-static average precision and reduces false motion flags, and is robust to estimated (noisy) poses. The raw confidence is not calibrated, but post-hoc isotonic calibration reaches an expected calibration error of 0.10. VLMM is a representation contribution: the closest prior maps each lack at least one of the four properties-open-vocabulary, language-queryable, fused prior-and-observed motion, and per-element uncertainty-that our combination provides.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言-运动地图：一种开放词汇、不确定性感知、可查询的3D场景运动属性
开放词汇3D地图使机器人能够回答关于场景中物体‘是什么’和‘在哪里’的语言查询，但它们假设世界是静态的，无法回答物体‘如何运动’的查询。我们提出了视觉-语言-运动地图（VLMM），这是一种开放词汇、可自然语言查询的3D地图，其中每个元素携带一个融合的运动属性：结合VLM/LLM语义可移动性先验与几何观测的跨帧运动，以及每个元素的不确定性。查询简化为属性过滤，可区分‘已被观测到运动’、‘可能运动但尚未运动’和‘保持静止’的物体。在具有精确真实值的控制模拟器基准（AI2-THOR，三种场景类型）上，通过消融实验表明，该架构的各个字段不可替代：仅语义基线即使具备强特征也无法回答运动查询，且运动字段彼此不可替代（先验无法回答‘什么在运动’，观测运动无法回答‘什么可能运动’）。在真实动态RGB-D数据（TUM和Bonn，六组序列）上，我们展示了不确定性通道——与先前融合运动工作相比的关键差异——持续提升运动与静止物体的平均精度，减少误报运动标志，并对估计（噪声）位姿具有鲁棒性。原始置信度未经校准，但事后等高线校准后预期校准误差降至0.10。VLMM是一种表示贡献：最接近的先前地图至少缺少我们组合提供的四个属性之一——开放词汇、语言可查询、融合先验与观测运动、每个元素的不确定性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to understand dynamic 3D scenes through natural language, this work introduces Vision-Language-Motion Maps (VLMM), a 3D map representation that assigns each scene element a fused motion attribute combining semantic movability priors from VLM/LLM models and geometrically observed cross-frame motion, along with per-element uncertainty. Experiments on AI2-THOR simulator and real-world RGB-D datasets (TUM, Bonn) demonstrate that the fused motion attribute is essential: semantic-only baselines fail motion queries, and neither component alone suffices to answer questions about what moves or could move. The uncertainty channel consistently improves motion detection accuracy and reduces false positives, even under noisy pose estimates, and post-hoc calibration achieves an expected calibration error of 0.10.
为解决现有3D地图虽支持开放词汇语言查询却无法推理场景动态行为的问题，本文提出Vision-Language-Motion Maps（VLMM），为每个场景元素赋予融合语义可移动性先验（来自VLM/LLM）与几何观测跨帧运动的运动属性，并附带每个元素的不确定性。在AI2-THOR仿真基准测试中，实验表明该架构各组件不可替代：仅语义基线无法回答运动问题，且两种运动信息无法互相替代。在真实RGB-D数据集（TUM和Bonn）上，不确定性通道持续提升运动与静止物体的平均精度并减少误报，即使在姿态估计噪声下仍保持鲁棒性，经后处理等距校准后预期校准误差达0.10。
When Does Muon Help Agentic Reinforcement Learning?
Authors: Kai Ruan, Jinghao Lin, Zihe Huang, Ziqi Zhou, Qianshan Wei, Xuan Wang, Hao Sun
First: 2026-07-17T17:49:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:49:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Muon is competitive with AdamW in large-scale pre-training, but its value for reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training remains unclear. We study vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL through matched single-seed comparisons with AdamW on ALFWorld using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weight matrices raises final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%); high-rate AdamW controls retain no post-update success. The effect depends on the advantage estimator and learning rate. At 3e-5, Muon improves GRPO from 0.161 to 0.268, whereas GraphGPO's late-window gap narrows near saturation. At 1e-5, GraphGPO Muon reaches 0.901, raises normalized validation AUC from 0.399 to 0.556, and reaches 0.5 and 0.75 success 30 and 60 updates earlier, respectively. These exploratory results show that Muon can benefit agentic RL and motivate studying the policy optimizer, advantage estimator, and learning rate jointly. Multi-seed and cross-task validation remain open.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Muon在何种情况下有助于代理强化学习？
Muon在大规模预训练中与AdamW相当，但其在强化学习（RL）后训练中的价值尚不明确。我们在ALFWorld上使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct，通过与AdamW的单种子匹配比较，研究了基础版Muon在稀疏奖励代理RL中的表现。在组内组策略优化（GiGPO）下，仅对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon，最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%）；高学习率的AdamW控制组则无后更新成功率。该效果依赖于优势估计器和学习率。在3e-5时，Muon使GRPO从0.161提升至0.268，而GraphGPO的后期窗口差距接近饱和。在1e-5时，GraphGPO Muon达到0.901，将归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，并分别在30和60次更新时提前达到0.5和0.75的成功率。这些探索性结果表明Muon可促进代理RL，激励联合研究策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率。多种子和跨任务验证仍待探索。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unclear utility of Muon in reinforcement learning post-training despite its competitiveness with AdamW in pre-training, this study evaluates vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct on ALFWorld. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weights boosts final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%), while high-rate AdamW controls show no post-update success.
