该研究探讨了Muon优化器（在大规模预训练中与AdamW表现相当）是否能提升稀疏奖励环境下的代理强化学习（RL）性能。在ALFWorld上使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct与Group-in-Group Policy Optimization（GiGPO）时，仅对隐藏权重应用Muon，使最终窗口验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），而AdamW对照组无任何后更新成功率。效果受优势估计器和学习率影响：在3e-5时，Muon使GRPO从0.161提升至0.268；在1e-5时，GraphGPO配合Muon达到0.901成功率，标准化验证AUC从0.399升至0.556，且提前30和60次更新分别达到0.5和0.75成功率。结果表明Muon可提升代理RL性能，但其效果依赖于策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率，多种子和跨任务验证仍待探索。
Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting
Authors: Ramin Soleimani, Andrea Visentin, Dirk Pesch
First: 2026-07-17T17:48:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:48:22+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 45 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于行为条件的神经过程用于自适应住宅短期负荷预测
住宅短期负荷预测（STLF）具有挑战性，因为家庭用电需求具有异质性、时间可变性，并受多种行为模式影响。本研究探讨是否可将推断出的行为结构嵌入基于神经过程的概率模型的预测机制中，而非仅作为外部分组信号，以实现基于上下文的住宅STLF。我们提出一种行为条件的注意力神经过程框架，将每个负荷曲线视为一个预测任务。行为结构通过从可用上下文推断出的离散潜在变量表示，并用于行为条件解码器的条件化；而连续潜在变量则捕捉异质负荷曲线间的共享函数不确定性。为实现无需真实行为标签的条件化，训练阶段利用聚类衍生信息提供弱监督，测试阶段则仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布。在智能电网与智慧城市（SGSC）数据集上的实验采用用户不重叠的训练/验证/测试划分、可变上下文长度和多步预测时域，并与无标签ANP基线及固定窗口确定性STLF基线进行比较。所提变体在所有预测时域和上下文设置下均优于ANP，尤其在上下文受限时提升显著。表现最佳的变体相较ANP平均降低MAE 7.9%、CRPS 6.9%。与固定窗口基线相比，该变体在所有评估时域下均实现更低的RMSE，同时保持竞争力的MAE，表明其在异质消费模式下预测偏差更小。结果支持在异质家庭、上下文和预测时域下使用单一模型进行不确定性感知的预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the heterogeneity and behavioural variability in residential electricity demand, this work proposes a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process to embed inferred behavioural structure directly into the forecasting mechanism rather than treating it as an external grouping signal. The model uses discrete latent variables to represent inferred behavioural classes from context and continuous latent variables to capture shared functional uncertainty across profiles, enabling conditioning without ground-truth labels via clustering-derived weak supervision during training. At test time, it relies solely on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the SGSC dataset with user-disjoint splits and variable context lengths show that the proposed method consistently outperforms a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic baselines, achieving average 7.9% and 6.9% reductions in MAE and CRPS respectively, and lower RMSE across horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, indicating better handling of heterogeneous consumption patterns.
该论文旨在解决住宅短期负荷预测中的异质性挑战，通过将推断出的行为结构直接嵌入神经过程模型，而非仅作为外部分组信号。作者提出一种行为条件化的注意力神经过程框架，利用离散潜在变量表示推断出的用户行为，连续潜在变量捕捉不同负荷曲线间的共享函数不确定性。训练阶段借助聚类衍生的弱监督信号，测试阶段仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布，无需真实标签。在SGSC数据集上使用用户不相交的训练/验证/测试划分和可变上下文长度实验表明，该模型在多个预测时长和上下文条件下均优于无标签ANP基线和固定窗口确定性基线，MAE和CRPS分别平均降低7.9%和6.9%，且RMSE更低，表明其在异质消费模式下更少出现大幅预测偏差。
An Exam for Active Observers
Authors: Jiarui Zhang, Muzi Tao, Shangshang Wang, Ollie Liu, Xuezhe Ma, Willie Neiswanger
First: 2026-07-17T17:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:46:23+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://activevision.dev
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human vision is a closed loop: gaze is continuously redirected by intermediate hypotheses rather than a single snapshot. Decades of psychophysics and cognitive science have argued that this active observation is essential for a wide range of tasks. Whether today's multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exercise active observation is an empirical question that current vision-language benchmarks do not answer. We introduce ActiveVision, a benchmark that makes active observation measurable for MLLMs, comprising 17 tasks across 3 categories. Tasks are designed to force repeated visual perception rather than a single static description. Frontier MLLMs collapse on ActiveVision: the highest-scoring model we evaluate, GPT-5.5 at the highest exposed reasoning-effort tier, solves only 10.6% of items and scores zero on 11 of the 17 tasks, and even Claude Fable 5, despite topping most reasoning and coding leaderboards, solves just 3.5%, far behind three human participants who average 96.1%. Furthermore, much of the gap persists even when models write and run their own vision code: such code is unreliable on realistic imagery, and catching its failures itself requires the active perception the models lack. Together, these results indicate that current MLLMs lack robust active visual observation, motivating architectures and training objectives that close the perception-reasoning loop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向主动观察者的测试
人类视觉是一个闭环系统：注视会持续根据中间假设进行调整，而非单一快照。几十年来，心理物理学和认知科学一直认为，这种主动观察对于多种任务至关重要。当前的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是否具备主动观察能力，是一个当前视觉-语言基准测试无法回答的经验性问题。我们提出了ActiveVision基准测试，旨在使MLLMs的主动观察能力可测量，包含17项任务，分属3类。这些任务旨在强制模型进行重复视觉感知，而非单一静态描述。前沿的MLLMs在ActiveVision上表现不佳：我们评估的最高得分模型GPT-5.5（在最高推理努力层级）仅解决10.6%的题目，17项任务中有11项得分为零；即使在推理和编程排行榜上领先的Claude Fable 5，也仅解决3.5%，远低于三位人类参与者平均96.1%的表现。此外，即使模型自行编写并运行视觉代码，其性能差距仍普遍存在：此类代码在真实图像上不可靠，而检测其失败本身又需要模型所缺乏的主动感知能力。综合来看，这些结果表明当前MLLMs缺乏稳健的主动视觉观察能力，亟需设计能够闭合感知-推理循环的架构与训练目标。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether modern multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit active visual observation, a core human capability where gaze and perception are dynamically adjusted based on hypotheses. To measure this, the authors introduce ActiveVision, a benchmark with 17 tasks across three categories designed to require repeated visual sampling rather than static image description. Results show that even top-tier models like GPT-5.5 and Claude Fable 5 perform poorly—scoring only 10.6% and 3.5% respectively—while human participants achieve 96.1%. Even when models generate and execute their own vision code, performance remains low due to unreliable visual reasoning, highlighting a fundamental gap in perception-reasoning integration.
本文探讨当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是否具备人类视觉中关键的主动观察能力——即根据中间假设动态调整注视点。为此，作者设计了ActiveVision基准测试，包含17个任务，要求模型反复感知而非仅静态描述图像。实验结果显示，即便顶尖模型如GPT-5.5和Claude Fable 5，准确率也仅为10.6%和3.5%，而人类平均准确率达96.1%。即使模型自动生成并执行视觉代码，其可靠性仍低，且检测失败仍需主动感知能力，而模型缺乏。这些结果表明当前MLLMs缺乏稳健的主动视觉观察能力，亟需构建能更好融合感知与推理的架构与训练目标。
A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs
Authors: Rahul Krishnan, Volker Schulz
First: 2026-07-14T09:23:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:45:07+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference: it grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the throughput ceiling. Existing reductions fall into two families. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT (Joint Lagrangian Tucker), applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, then restores the energy that truncation discards with a rotated low-bit residual: a random orthogonal rotation followed by low-bit quantization. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression. Perplexity stays near-lossless on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B), and GSM8K accuracy and needle-in-a-haystack retrieval hold at the uncompressed baseline at 2x on both architectures and through 3x on the GQA model. At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at 1024-token context and matched quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大语言模型的KV缓存近无损压缩方法：基于联合Tucker与JL残差分配
键值（KV）缓存已成为Transformer推理的主要内存开销：其规模随批大小、上下文长度和层数增长，在长上下文场景下，缓存而非模型权重成为吞吐量瓶颈。现有压缩方法分为两类：低秩方法对缓存的二维切片（如每头矩阵或跨层特征块）进行分解，量化方法则降低每个元素的比特宽度。二者均未利用缓存天然为三阶张量的特性——其三个轴（头、令牌、特征）冗余度差异显著。本文直接采用张量视角，提出JoLT（联合拉格朗日Tucker）方法：对令牌与特征轴进行部分Tucker分解，保留头与层轴不变，再通过旋转低比特残差恢复截断损失——即随机正交旋转后低比特量化。单个拉格朗日对偶变量在每层组内联合分配Tucker秩与残差比特宽度，对键与值分别独立优化，总预算为1字节。结果实现近无损2-3倍压缩。在分组查询注意力模型（Mistral-7B-v0.3）与多头注意力模型（LLaMA-2-13B）上，困惑度保持近无损；GSM8K准确率与needle-in-a-haystack检索性能在2倍压缩下保持无损，在3倍压缩下于GQA模型仍维持基线水平。2倍压缩时，JoLT在两种架构上重建缓存的相对Frobenius误差分别为0.009（K）和0.006（V）。其随机SVD变体FlashJoLT在1024令牌上下文下实现5-13倍压缩速度提升，且保持同等质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory cost of KV caches in transformer inference, which scales with batch size, context length, and model depth, this paper proposes JoLT, a tensor-based compression method that treats the cache as a third-order tensor with distinct redundancy across heads, tokens, and features. JoLT applies a partial Tucker decomposition to compress token and feature axes while preserving head and layer dimensions, then restores lost energy via a rotated low-bit residual using a single Lagrangian dual to jointly optimize Tucker ranks and bit-widths under a per-layer byte budget. Experiments show near-lossless compression of 2-3x with minimal performance degradation: perplexity remains stable on both GQA and multi-head models, and GSM8K accuracy and retrieval performance are preserved even at 3x compression on GQA. At 2x compression, reconstruction errors are 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V), and a randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, achieves 5-13x faster compression without quality loss at 1024-token context.
为降低Transformer推理中KV缓存的高内存开销（随批大小、上下文长度和层数增长），本文提出JoLT方法，将缓存视为头、令牌、特征三轴冗余不同的三阶张量。该方法对令牌和特征轴进行部分Tucker分解，保留头和层轴，再通过旋转低比特残差恢复丢失能量。联合拉格朗日优化在每层组内统一分配Tucker秩和残差比特宽度，对键值分别优化，满足单字节预算，实现2-3倍压缩且近无损。实验表明，在GQA和多头模型上，困惑度与任务准确率（GSM8K、检索）均接近无损，重构误差低于0.01。其随机化SVD变体FlashJoLT在1024令牌上下文下压缩速度提升5-13倍，质量不变。
Interaction-Aware Whole-Body Control for Compliant Object Transport
Authors: Hao Zhang, Yves Tseng, Ding Zhao, H. Eric Tseng
First: 2026-03-04T05:50:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cooperative object transport in unstructured environments remains challenging for assistive humanoids because strong, time-varying interaction forces can make tracking-centric whole-body control unreliable, especially in close-contact support tasks. This paper proposes a bio-inspired, interaction-oriented whole-body control (IO-WBC) that functions as an artificial cerebellum - an adaptive motor agent that translates upstream (skill-level) commands into stable, physically consistent whole-body behavior under contact. This work structurally separates upper-body interaction execution from lower-body support control, enabling the robot to maintain balance while shaping force exchange in a tightly coupled robot-object system. A trajectory-optimized reference generator (RG) provides a kinematic prior, while a reinforcement learning (RL) policy governs body responses under heavy-load interactions and disturbances. The policy is trained in simulation with randomized payload mass/inertia and external perturbations, and deployed via asymmetric teacher-student distillation so that the student relies only on proprioceptive histories at runtime. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IO-WBC maintains stable whole-body behavior and physical interaction even when precise velocity tracking becomes infeasible, enabling compliant object transport across a wide range of scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于交互感知的全身协调控制用于柔性物体搬运
在非结构化环境中，协作搬运物体对助人型人形机器人仍具挑战性，因强且时变的交互力会使以轨迹跟踪为中心的全身控制不可靠，尤其在紧密接触支撑任务中。本文提出一种仿生、以交互为导向的全身控制（IO-WBC），其作为人工小脑——一种自适应运动代理，将上游（技能层）指令转化为接触下的稳定、物理一致的全身行为。该方法结构上分离上身交互执行与下身支撑控制，使机器人在紧密耦合的机器人-物体系统中保持平衡并塑造力交换。轨迹优化的参考生成器（RG）提供运动学先验，而强化学习（RL）策略则调控机器人在重载交互与扰动下的身体响应。该策略在仿真中通过随机负载质量和外部扰动训练，并通过非对称教师-学生蒸馏部署，使学生在运行时仅依赖本体感觉历史。大量实验表明，IO-WBC即使在精确速度跟踪不可行时，仍能维持稳定全身行为与物理交互，实现广泛场景下的柔性物体搬运。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of maintaining stable whole-body control during cooperative object transport in unstructured environments due to unpredictable interaction forces, this paper introduces Interaction-Aware Whole-Body Control (IO-WBC), a bio-inspired control framework modeled after an artificial cerebellum. It decouples upper-body interaction execution from lower-body balance control, using a trajectory-optimized reference generator for kinematic guidance and a reinforcement learning policy trained in simulation with variable payloads and disturbances. The policy is distilled to a lightweight student model that relies only on proprioceptive data at runtime. Experimental results show that IO-WBC sustains stable physical interaction and whole-body behavior even when precise velocity tracking fails, enabling reliable compliant transport across diverse scenarios.
为了解决在非结构化环境中进行协作物体搬运时，因交互力强且时变导致的全身控制不稳定问题，本文提出了一种受生物启发的交互感知全身控制（IO-WBC）系统，模拟小脑功能。该方法将上半身交互执行与下半身平衡控制解耦，结合轨迹优化参考生成器提供运动先验，并用强化学习策略处理重载交互与扰动。该策略在仿真中训练，支持随机负载与外部干扰，并通过非对称教师-学生蒸馏部署，运行时仅依赖本体感觉数据。实验表明，即使无法精确跟踪速度，IO-WBC仍能保持全身稳定与物理交互一致性，实现多种场景下的可靠柔性物体搬运。
Agent Step Value: Auditing Evaluator-Channel Reversals in Black-Box Agent Traces
Authors: Andrew Zhang, Chengzhan Li
First: 2026-07-05T17:27:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:39:00+00:00
Comments: adds source-contract intervention and two-wave retry study; re-acquires cube and extends bridge cohort to n=98 to 100
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pooling, substituting, or reusing evaluator-derived step rewards assumes that their direction survives a change of evaluation channel. The same frozen transition can violate that assumption. Process rewards vary agent states, while evaluator audits vary scoring configurations; neither first difference isolates their interaction. We define Agent Step Value (ASV) as a channel-indexed target-margin gain and identify the state-by-channel interaction on complete matched faces. Across frozen PubMed question-answering transitions, direct scoring yields a positive mean ASV, while the generated-view channel yields a negative mean. Two matched replay waves reproduce this reversal, and cross-channel sign disagreement exceeds same-channel retry disagreement by 48.0 percentage points. Matched retrieval faces localize the reversal to the generated-view coordinate and trace its direction across a readout-and-stack bridge. A source-only generation contract restores the positive mean direction on artifact-bearing retrievals and removes parser-detected substantive support claims from artifact-free before-state views. ASV turns channel sensitivity into an identified measurement problem that can be localized and tested by intervention before step rewards are reused.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理步值：审计黑盒代理轨迹中评估器-通道反转
假设评估器派生的步奖励方向在评估通道变化后仍保持不变，从而允许聚合、替换或重用这些奖励。但同一冻结的转移可能违反该假设。过程奖励改变代理状态，而评估器审计则改变评分配置；两者的第一阶差值均无法隔离其相互作用。我们将代理步值（ASV）定义为按通道索引的目标-边际增益，并在完全匹配的样本上识别状态-通道交互。在冻结的PubMed问答转移中，直接评分产生正均值ASV，而生成视图通道则产生负均值。两轮匹配重试再现了该反转，且跨通道符号不一致比同通道重试不一致高出48.0个百分点。匹配检索样本将反转定位到生成视图坐标，并通过读出-堆叠桥追踪其方向。仅源生成契约在含伪影的检索中恢复正均值方向，并从无伪影的前状态视图中移除解析器检测到的实质性支持声明。ASV将通道敏感性转化为可定位和通过干预测试的测量问题，从而在重用步奖励前即可解决。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the problem that step rewards derived from evaluators may reverse direction when evaluated through different channels, undermining their reliability for reuse. The authors introduce Agent Step Value (ASV), a channel-indexed metric measuring target-margin gain, to isolate state-channel interactions in agent traces. Using matched transitions from frozen PubMed QA tasks, they find that direct scoring yields positive ASV while the generated-view channel yields negative ASV, a reversal confirmed across two replay waves. Cross-channel sign disagreement is notably higher than same-channel retry disagreement, and matched retrieval cases pinpoint the reversal to the generated-view coordinate. Interventions like source-only generation contracts restore positive ASV on artifact-bearing retrievals and eliminate misleading support claims from artifact-free before-states, demonstrating ASV’s utility in diagnosing and correcting channel-specific reward artifacts before reuse.
为解决黑盒代理轨迹中评估器衍生步骤奖励在不同评估通道间方向一致性问题，本文提出Agent Step Value（ASV）作为通道索引的目标边际收益，用于量化状态-通道交互。在冻结的PubMed问答过渡中，通过匹配检索面实验发现：直接评分产生正ASV，而生成视图通道产生负ASV，该反转在两次重试波中重复出现，且跨通道符号分歧显著高于同通道重试分歧（48.0个百分点）。反转定位在生成视图坐标，并通过读取-堆栈桥追踪其方向；引入仅源生成契约干预后，含伪影的检索面恢复正ASV，且移除无伪影前状态视图中的实质性支持声明，证明ASV能有效识别并测试通道敏感性问题，避免奖励重用前的错误方向。
PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment
Authors: Tam Bang, Hussam Abubakr, Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal, Truc Phuong Nguyen, Austin Harris, Toru Hirano, Mina Sartipi, Yunfei Xu, Hoang H. Nguyen
First: 2026-07-17T17:35:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:35:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at the 2026 IEEE 29th International Conference on Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITSC 2026). 8 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRISA：用于交叉口安全评估的主动基础设施激光雷达框架
城市交叉口是道路网络中最危险的区域之一，对车辆和脆弱道路使用者（如行人和骑行者）构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性要求能够提前预测冲突、防止事故升级的持续实时监测系统。本文提出PRISA，一种模块化的基础设施激光雷达框架，利用隐私保护、低光鲁棒的路边传感器，实现长期交通观测和边缘端实时风险检测。该框架包含两个核心组件：感知层和即插即用的风险评估模块。后者可自动从累积的感知输出中整理特定地点的训练数据，无需人工标注即可训练轨迹预测模型，并部署该模型进行连续运动预测和双重代理安全评估：纵向冲突使用碰撞时间（TTC），交叉及涉及VRU的交互使用预测侵入后时间（PPET）。PRISA在公开的R-LiViT数据集上进行评估，并部署于田纳西州查塔努加市一个实际信号交叉口的NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor平台上。基于PPET的评估端到端延迟为194~ms，预测时域为2.4秒；基于TTC的检测与感知仍保持实时性，证明了该框架在主动多智能体交叉口安全监测中的实际可行性。
Summary / 总结
Urban intersections pose high safety risks due to complex multi-agent interactions, necessitating real-time conflict prediction systems. PRISA introduces a modular LiDAR framework using privacy-preserving roadside sensors to enable long-term traffic observation and edge-based risk detection. Its architecture includes a perception layer and a plug-and-play risk module that automatically generates training data from perception outputs to train trajectory prediction models without manual annotation. The system then forecasts motion and evaluates safety using Time-to-Collision for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time for VRU interactions. Evaluated on the R-LiViT dataset and deployed at a real intersection, PRISA achieves 194ms end-to-end latency for PPET-based assessment over a 2.4s horizon, with TTC-based components remaining real-time, proving its practical viability for proactive safety monitoring.
鉴于城市交叉口因多主体交互复杂而事故风险高，PRISA提出一套模块化LiDAR框架，利用隐私保护的路边传感器实现实时安全监控。该框架结合感知层与可插拔风险评估模块，后者自动从历史感知输出中生成训练数据以训练轨迹预测模型，无需人工标注。系统随后进行运动预测并采用纵向冲突的Time-to-Collision和涉及行人/骑行者的Predicted Post-Encroachment Time评估安全，实测端到端延迟194毫秒（预测时长2.4秒），感知与检测保持实时性，已在R-LiViT数据集上验证并部署于真实交叉口。
CLIFE: Camera-LiDAR Fusion Framework for Edge-Deployable Roadside VRU Perception
Authors: Tam Bang, Hoang H. Nguyen, Lei Cheng, Lihao Guo, Siyang Cao, Hussam Abubakr, Tianya Zhang, Austin Harris, Mina Sartipi
First: 2026-07-17T17:35:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:35:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at the 2026 IEEE 29th International Conference on Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITSC 2026). 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable roadside perception of vulnerable road users (VRUs) remains challenging under occlusions, variable lighting, and diverse weather conditions, particularly under strict edge-computing and latency constraints. Existing multi-sensor fusion systems rely on cloud or server-grade infrastructure, creating a deployment gap at real-world intersections. We present CLIFE, an edge-native camera-LiDAR fusion framework that integrates targetless online calibration and lightweight late-fusion tracking entirely on a single embedded device, without cloud offloading. CLIFE adaptively refines camera-LiDAR alignment on demand and performs multi-sensor fusion and track association with O(N log N) per-frame cost. We deploy CLIFE across 12 signalized intersections in Chattanooga and conduct an in-depth evaluation at a representative intersection using synchronized camera-LiDAR data that spans diverse daytime, nighttime, and weather conditions. Our experiments demonstrate that the fusion architecture substantially enhances the perceptual range and robustness of the individual sensors under varied environmental and traffic conditions. The late-fusion core operates at 53.2 FPS on the Jetson AGX Thor, ensuring high throughput for real-time intersection-scale applications. By centering perception at the edge, CLIFE provides a deployable foundation for downstream safety applications, while reducing bandwidth and calibration overhead for agencies operating multi-intersection corridors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLIFE：面向边缘部署的路侧行人感知相机-激光雷达融合框架
在遮挡、光照变化和多变天气条件下，可靠地感知脆弱道路使用者（VRU）仍具挑战性，尤其受限于严格的边缘计算和延迟约束。现有多传感器融合系统依赖云端或服务器级基础设施，导致在真实路口部署存在差距。我们提出CLIFE，一种原生支持边缘设备的相机-激光雷达融合框架，通过无目标在线标定与轻量级后融合跟踪，完全在单个嵌入式设备上运行，无需云端卸载。CLIFE按需自适应优化相机-激光雷达对齐，并以每帧O(N log N)复杂度执行多传感器融合与轨迹关联。我们在田纳西州查塔努加市12个信号交叉口部署CLIFE，并在代表性路口使用同步相机-激光雷达数据（涵盖多样日间、夜间及天气条件）进行深入评估。实验表明，融合架构显著提升了单个传感器在不同环境与交通条件下的感知范围与鲁棒性。后融合核心在Jetson AGX Thor上运行达53.2 FPS，确保实时路口级应用的高吞吐量。通过将感知置于边缘，CLIFE为下游安全应用提供可部署基础，同时降低多路口走廊运营机构的带宽与标定开销。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliably detecting vulnerable road users under occlusions, variable lighting, and weather while meeting edge-computing constraints, CLIFE introduces a camera-LiDAR fusion framework designed for deployment on a single embedded device without cloud reliance. It employs targetless online calibration and lightweight late-fusion tracking with O(N log N) per-frame complexity, enabling adaptive alignment and efficient multi-sensor fusion. Evaluated across 12 intersections in Chattanooga using synchronized data across diverse conditions, CLIFE significantly improves perceptual range and robustness compared to individual sensors, achieving 53.2 FPS on Jetson AGX Thor—making it suitable for real-time, edge-deployable safety applications.
为解决在遮挡、光照和天气变化环境下可靠检测弱势道路使用者且满足边缘计算低延迟需求的挑战，CLIFE提出一种边缘原生的相机与激光雷达融合框架，可在单个嵌入式设备上完成无目标在线标定和轻量级后融合跟踪，无需云端支持。系统通过自适应调整传感器对齐并以O(N log N)时间复杂度实现多传感器融合，可在Jetson AGX Thor上达到53.2 FPS实时运行。在田纳西州查塔努加市12个交叉口进行评估，涵盖多种光照与天气条件，实验表明CLIFE显著提升单传感器的感知范围与鲁棒性，为边缘部署的交通安全应用提供低带宽、可扩展的基础方案。
VTLoc: Learning-based Tactile Contact Localization in Visual Point Clouds
Authors: Zhiyuan Wu, Zhuo Chen, Shan Luo
First: 2026-07-17T17:27:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:27:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision and touch are complementary modalities essential for robotic perception and manipulation. While vision provides global object context, touch offers precise local information at contact points. Integrating these modalities for contact localization, i.e., predicting the location of touch on an object's surface, poses significant challenges due to the need for accurate spatial alignment between tactile data and visual geometry. To address this challenge, we propose VTLoc, a novel visual-tactile framework that localizes contact points from tactile readings using a 3D point cloud as visual input. VTLoc introduces two key components: a geometric multi-modal alignment module, which reconstructs a pseudo-point cloud from fused visual-tactile features and aligns it with the visual point cloud to enforce spatial consistencies across modalities; and an iterative localizing updater, which iteratively refines the predicted contact location using fused visual-tactile features. Evaluated on a new benchmark of 100 real-world objects, VTLoc improves single-touch contact localization by reducing local-to-global correspondence ambiguity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VTLoc：基于视觉点云的触觉接触点定位
视觉与触觉是机器人感知与操作中互补的重要模态。视觉提供全局物体上下文，而触觉则在接触点提供精确的局部信息。将这两种模态集成用于接触点定位（即预测触觉在物体表面的位置）面临挑战，因为需要精确的空间对齐触觉数据与视觉几何结构。为解决此问题，我们提出VTLoc，一种新颖的视觉-触觉框架，利用3D点云作为视觉输入，从触觉读数中定位接触点。VTLoc包含两个关键组件：几何多模态对齐模块，通过融合视觉-触觉特征重建伪点云，并将其与视觉点云对齐以强制模态间空间一致性；以及迭代定位更新器，利用融合的视觉-触觉特征迭代优化预测的接触位置。在包含100个真实物体的新基准上评估，VTLoc通过减少局部到全局对应模糊性，提升了单次触觉接触点定位性能。
Summary / 总结
VTLoc addresses the challenge of localizing tactile contact points on objects using visual point clouds by integrating vision and touch modalities. It employs a geometric multi-modal alignment module to fuse visual and tactile features into a pseudo-point cloud, aligning it with the visual input to enforce spatial consistency. An iterative localizing updater then refines contact predictions using fused features. Evaluated on a 100-object real-world benchmark, VTLoc significantly reduces local-to-global correspondence ambiguity, improving single-touch contact localization accuracy.
VTLoc 旨在通过视觉点云实现触觉接触点定位，解决视觉与触觉模态融合中的空间对齐难题。其方法包含几何多模态对齐模块，将视觉与触觉特征融合生成伪点云并与其对齐以确保空间一致性；同时引入迭代定位更新器，利用融合特征逐步优化接触点预测。在包含100个真实物体的新基准测试中，VTLoc 显著降低了局部到全局对应模糊性，提升了单点接触定位精度。
ABot-AgentOS: A General Robotic Agent OS with Lifelong Multi-modal Memory
Authors: Jiayi Tian, Shiao Liu, Yuting Xu, Jia Lu, Zihao Guan, Honglin Han, Di Yang, Minqi Gu, Yifei Qian, Tianlin Zhang, Yanqing Zhu, Zeqian Ye, Menglin Yang, Fei Wang, Xu Hu, Xiuxian Li, Wei Zhang, Shihui Su, Yiyan Ji, Jingbo Wang, Ziteng Feng, Jiaheng Liu, Zhaoxiang Zhang, Xiaolong Wu, Zixiao Tang, Zhining Gu, Yang Cai, Linbo Zheng, Jingjing Ma, Mingyang Yin, Zedong Chu, Wenbin Tang, Mu Xu
First: 2026-07-11T15:24:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:27:03+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/amap-cvlab/ABot-AgentOS Project page: https://amap-cvlab.github.io/ABot-AgentOS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Recent VLM and VLA systems have improved robotic perception and action prediction, yet long-horizon embodied agents still require a general runtime layer for reasoning, memory, tool use, verification, and cross-embodiment execution. We present ABot-AgentOS, a general robotic Agent Operating System that sits above low-level controllers and provides a deliberative agent layer for scene-conditioned planning, context-isolated skill execution, multi-stage verification, multi-modal memory, and edge-cloud collaboration. To evaluate such systems, we introduce EmbodiedWorldBench, an executable benchmark with 16 indoor, outdoor, and hybrid scenes, four difficulty levels, and over 200 tasks involving navigation, object search, NPC dialogue, dynamic events, and trace-grounded scoring. ABot-AgentOS further introduces Universal Multi-modal Graph Memory, a persistent source-grounded substrate that converts dialogue, visual observations, spatial context, temporal relations, and task traces into typed nodes and edges. A failure-driven self-evolution loop converts diagnosed memory failures into gated runtime evo-assets that are promoted only to later evaluation splits, preventing current-split ground-truth leakage while enabling continual improvement. On an initial EmbodiedWorldBench subset, ABot-AgentOS improves over a single-controller baseline in both task success and goal completion. Across memory benchmarks, ABot-AgentOS Static achieves 87.5 on LoCoMo, 59.9 on OpenEQA EM-EQA, 88.6 on Mem-Gallery, and 76.5 Acc@All on NExT-QA; self-evolution further improves LoCoMo to 88.7, OpenEQA to 60.4, and Mem-Gallery to 89.0. These results suggest that a general Agent OS layer can improve long-horizon embodied execution while providing persistent, auditable memory for continual interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ABot-AgentOS：具备终身多模态记忆的通用机器人代理操作系统
近期的视觉语言模型（VLM）和视觉语言行动器（VLA）系统已提升机器人感知与动作预测能力，但长时程具身智能体仍需通用运行时层以支持推理、记忆、工具使用、验证及跨具身执行。我们提出ABot-AgentOS，一种通用机器人代理操作系统，位于底层控制器之上，提供基于场景的规划、上下文隔离技能执行、多阶段验证、多模态记忆及边缘-云协作的审慎代理层。为评估此类系统，我们引入EmbodiedWorldBench，一个可执行基准，包含16个室内、室外及混合场景，四个难度等级，超过200项任务，涵盖导航、物体搜索、NPC对话、动态事件及轨迹锚定评分。ABot-AgentOS进一步引入通用多模态图记忆，一种持久的源锚定基底，将对话、视觉观测、空间上下文、时间关系及任务轨迹转化为类型化节点与边。基于失败驱动的自演化循环，将诊断出的记忆失败转化为门控运行时演化资产，仅在后续评估分割中推广，避免当前分割真实值泄露，同时支持持续改进。在EmbodiedWorldBench初始子集上，ABot-AgentOS在任务成功率与目标完成率上均优于单一控制器基线。在各类记忆基准测试中，ABot-AgentOS Static在LoCoMo上达87.5，在OpenEQA EM-EQA上达59.9，在Mem-Gallery上达88.6，在NExT-QA上达76.5 Acc@All；自演化进一步提升LoCoMo至88.7、OpenEQA至60.4、Mem-Gallery至89.0。这些结果表明，通用代理操作系统层可提升长时程具身执行能力，同时提供持久、可审计的记忆以支持持续交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a general runtime layer to support long-horizon robotic agents, the authors introduce ABot-AgentOS, a robotic Agent Operating System that enables scene-conditioned planning, skill execution, verification, and multi-modal memory. It integrates a Universal Multi-modal Graph Memory to persistently store and link dialogue, visual data, spatial context, and task traces into a structured graph. A failure-driven self-evolution mechanism promotes runtime improvements only to future evaluation splits, avoiding ground-truth leakage. Evaluated on EmbodiedWorldBench with 200+ tasks across 16 scenes, ABot-AgentOS outperforms a single-controller baseline in task success and goal completion, achieving top scores on memory benchmarks including 87.5 on LoCoMo and 89.0 on Mem-Gallery after self-evolution.
为支持长时程机器人执行，作者提出ABot-AgentOS，一个提供场景规划、技能执行、验证及多模态记忆的通用机器人操作系统。系统引入通用多模态图记忆结构，将对话、视觉观测、空间上下文与任务轨迹转化为结构化图节点与边，并通过失败驱动的自进化机制将记忆错误转化为受控的运行时资产，提升性能同时避免真实标签泄露。在包含200+任务的EmbodiedWorldBench基准上，ABot-AgentOS显著优于单一控制器基线，在LoCoMo、Mem-Gallery等记忆评测中分别达到87.5和89.0的高分，自进化后进一步提升表现。
Learning Standard Model structure from LHC data with Riemannian flow matching
Authors: Midori Kato, Kevin A. Urquía-Calderón, Inar Timiryasov, Oleg Ruchayskiy
First: 2026-07-17T17:23:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:23:22+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 25 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work we demonstrate that a single transformer-based generative model can capture Standard Model structure spanning five decades of invariant mass, from the sub-GeV regime to the TeV continuum, a range that no single Monte Carlo sample covers. To achieve this we design \textsc{ShellFlow}, a Riemannian conditional flow matching model that, given the recorded event composition, generates each particle on its on-shell manifold. Its only physics priors are the on-shell condition and the invariant-mass formula. The model is trained on $\sim 10^{9}$ real $pp$ collision events from the ATLAS Open Data 13~TeV release and told nothing else. From a single training run, the model learns to reproduce all of the following: intra-particle kinematics, the dilepton resonances ($J/ψ$, $Υ$, $Z$) at their PDG positions, the leptonic Weinberg angle, the $W$ and top-quark masses, and inter-particle correlations that enter no training objective. A substantial fraction of the Standard Model is thus learnable directly from recorded collision data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用黎曼流匹配从LHC数据中学习标准模型结构
本工作中我们证明，一个基于Transformer的生成模型能够捕捉跨越五个数量级不变质量的标准模型结构，从亚GeV区域到TeV连续谱，这一范围单个蒙特卡洛样本无法覆盖。为实现此目标，我们设计了\textsc{ShellFlow}，一种黎曼条件流匹配模型，该模型根据记录的事件组成，为每个粒子生成其在本征流形上的分布。其唯一的物理先验是本征条件和不变质量公式。该模型在ATLAS公开数据13 TeV版本中约$\sim 10^{9}$个真实$pp$碰撞事件上训练，未提供其他信息。从单次训练中，模型学会重现以下所有内容：粒子内动量学、双轻子共振（$J/ψ$、$Υ$、$Z$）在PDG位置、轻子韦恩角、$W$和顶夸克质量，以及未出现在训练目标中的粒子间关联。因此，标准模型的相当一部分可直接从记录的碰撞数据中学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning Standard Model physics directly from collider data without relying on Monte Carlo simulations, this work introduces ShellFlow, a Riemannian conditional flow matching model that generates particles conditioned on event composition while enforcing on-shell constraints and invariant-mass formulas. Trained on approximately 10^9 real ATLAS 13 TeV proton-proton collision events with no additional physics priors, the model learns to reproduce intra-particle kinematics, precise dilepton resonance positions (J/ψ, Υ, Z), the leptonic Weinberg angle, W and top-quark masses, and inter-particle correlations—all without explicit supervision on these quantities. The results demonstrate that a single generative model can capture a broad range of Standard Model structure spanning five decades of invariant mass, from sub-GeV to TeV scales, suggesting that much of the Standard Model is directly learnable from recorded collision data.
为了解决从高能碰撞数据中直接学习标准模型物理而不依赖先验知识的挑战，本研究提出了ShellFlow，一种基于黎曼流匹配的条件生成模型，它仅使用不变质量公式和粒子在壳条件作为物理先验，生成粒子时强制其位于各自的在壳流形上。该模型在约10^9个真实ATLAS 13 TeV质子-质子碰撞事件上训练，无需额外物理输入，即可学习到包括粒子内动量关系、J/ψ、Υ、Z共振峰位置、轻子韦恩角、W和顶夸克质量，以及粒子间关联等关键标准模型特征。实验结果表明，单个生成模型即可覆盖从亚GeV到TeV量级的五十年跨度不变质量范围，说明标准模型的大量结构可直接从原始事件数据中学习得到。
A New Implementation of NeoSLAM and a Comparative Evaluation with RatSLAM
Authors: Joao Victor T. Borges, Fabio Coelho, Paulo Padrao, Jose Fuentes, Ramon R. Costa, Liu Hsu, Leonardo Bobadilla
First: 2026-07-17T17:23:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:23:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a new implementation of the NeoSLAM algorithm. The proposed version is a complete rewrite of NeoSLAM into a modular architecture using modern frameworks that, together, enable real-time execution with minimal discarding of input data. This work also provides a comparative evaluation between NeoSLAM and RatSLAM across three datasets under varying environmental conditions. The experimental results highlight differences in mapping consistency and trajectory reconstruction, demonstrating the effectiveness and practical applicability of the proposed ROS2-based implementation. The results indicate that the new NeoSLAM outperforms the original in terms of processing throughput for real-time applications and achieves comparable performance to RatSLAM in terms of map reconstruction across the evaluated datasets.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, modular, and efficient SLAM systems, this paper introduces a complete rewrite of NeoSLAM using modern frameworks to enable real-time execution without discarding input data. The authors compare this new implementation against RatSLAM across three datasets under varying environmental conditions, finding that the updated NeoSLAM achieves higher processing throughput suitable for real-time applications while maintaining map reconstruction quality comparable to RatSLAM, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.
为满足实时、模块化和高效SLAM系统的需求，本文对NeoSLAM进行了全面重写，采用现代框架实现无数据丢弃的实时运行。在三个不同环境条件下的数据集上与RatSLAM对比实验表明，新版本NeoSLAM在处理吞吐量上优于原版，同时在地图重建质量上与RatSLAM相当，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。
Improving Improved Kernel PLS
Authors: Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm
First: 2026-07-17T17:19:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:19:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：改进的改进型核偏最小二乘法
改进型核偏最小二乘法（IKPLS）算法1和2是目前最快的PLS校准算法。本文聚焦于两个共同步骤——X矩阵旋转R和Y负载Q的计算，并加速这两个步骤。对于R，采用直接计算策略替代逐项累加，虽乘法次数相同，但在现代硬件上并行性更好。对于Q，我首次（据我所知）识别出等价关系：每个Y负载可由同一迭代中先前已计算的量（乘以显式导出的常数）获得，并在IKPLS中利用该关系，当M=1或2≤M<K时（K为预测变量数，即X矩阵列数；M为响应变量数，即Y矩阵列数），将每个负载的计算成本从Θ(KM)降低至Θ(M)。两项改进均能保证与原始算法产生完全相同的W、P、Q、R和T。使用NumPy（CPU）和JAX（GPU）的基准测试显示，独立步骤加速可达两个数量级，完整拟合加速约为CPU端2倍、GPU端6倍。两项改进均已实现于免费开源的Python包ikpls中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate the computation of kernel partial least squares (IKPLS) algorithms, this work targets two key computational bottlenecks: the calculation of X rotations (R) and Y loadings (Q). For R, the authors replace term-by-term accumulation with a direct evaluation strategy that maintains the same computational cost but improves parallelization on modern hardware. For Q, they introduce novel mathematical equivalences showing that each loading can be derived from previously computed quantities, reducing its complexity from Θ(KM) to Θ(M) when M=1 or 2≤M<K. These optimizations preserve all output components (W, P, Q, R, T) identically to the original algorithms. Experimental benchmarks using NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) demonstrate speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for individual steps and overall fits of 2× (CPU) and 6× (GPU).
为加速改进型核偏最小二乘（IKPLS）算法，本文聚焦于两个关键步骤：X旋转矩阵（R）和Y载荷（Q）的计算。针对R，采用直接计算策略替代逐项累加，保持运算量不变但提升现代硬件并行效率；针对Q，首次提出数学等价关系，表明每个载荷可由迭代中已计算的量推导得出，从而在M=1或2≤M<K条件下将计算复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)。这些改进保证输出矩阵（W、P、Q、R、T）与原算法完全一致。实验在NumPy（CPU）和JAX（GPU）上验证，单步加速最高达两个数量级，完整拟合加速分别为CPU端2倍、GPU端6倍。
FAIR_XAI: Improving Multimodal Foundation Model Fairness via Explainability for Wellbeing Assessment
Authors: Sophie Chiang, Tom Brennan, Fethiye Irmak Dogan, Jiaee Cheong, Hatice Gunes
First: 2026-04-26T16:22:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:17:29+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, the integration of multimodal machine learning in wellbeing assessment has offered transformative potential for monitoring mental health. However, with the rapid advancement of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), their deployment in clinical settings has raised concerns due to their lack of transparency and potential for bias. While previous research has explored the intersection of fairness and Explainable AI (XAI), its application to VLMs for wellbeing assessment and depression prediction remains under-explored. This work investigates VLM performance across laboratory (AFAR-BSFT) and naturalistic (E-DAIC) datasets, focusing on diagnostic reliability and demographic fairness. Performance varied substantially across environments and architectures; Phi3.5-Vision achieved 80.4% accuracy on E-DAIC, while Qwen2-VL struggled at 33.9%. Additionally, both models demonstrated a tendency to over-predict depression on AFAR-BSFT. Although bias existed across both architectures, Qwen2-VL showed higher gender disparities, while Phi-3.5-Vision exhibited more racial bias. Our XAI intervention framework yielded mixed results; fairness prompting achieved perfect equal opportunity for Qwen2-VL at a severe accuracy cost on E-DAIC. On AFAR-BSFT, explainability-based interventions improved procedural consistency but did not guarantee outcome fairness, sometimes amplifying racial bias. These results highlight a persistent gap between procedural transparency and equitable outcomes. We analyse these findings and consolidate concrete recommendations for addressing them, emphasising that future fairness interventions must jointly optimise predictive accuracy, demographic parity, and cross-domain generalisation.

标题：FAIR_XAI：通过可解释性提升多模态基础模型在福祉评估中的公平性
近年来，多模态机器学习在福祉评估中的整合为心理健康监测提供了变革性潜力。然而，随着视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的快速发展，其在临床环境中的部署引发了对其透明度不足和潜在偏见的担忧。尽管先前研究已探讨公平性与可解释人工智能（XAI）的交叉领域，但将其应用于福祉评估和抑郁预测的VLMs仍属探索不足。本研究考察了VLMs在实验室（AFAR-BSFT）和自然场景（E-DAIC）数据集上的表现，重点关注诊断可靠性和人口公平性。不同环境与架构下性能差异显著：Phi3.5-Vision在E-DAIC上准确率达80.4%，而Qwen2-VL仅33.9%。两模型均倾向于在AFAR-BSFT上高估抑郁。尽管两种架构均存在偏见，Qwen2-VL性别差异更显著，而Phi-3.5-Vision种族偏见更突出。我们的XAI干预框架效果参差：公平性提示使Qwen2-VL在E-DAIC上实现完全机会平等，但以严重牺牲准确率为代价；在AFAR-BSFT上，基于可解释性的干预提升了程序一致性，但未保证结果公平，有时反而加剧种族偏见。这些结果揭示了程序透明性与公平结果之间持续存在的差距。我们分析了这些发现，并提出具体建议，强调未来公平性干预必须同时优化预测准确性、人口平等性和跨域泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure fairness in multimodal foundation models used for wellbeing assessment, this study evaluates Vision-Language Models (VLMs) across clinical and naturalistic datasets, revealing significant performance and bias disparities. The authors compare Phi3.5-Vision and Qwen2-VL, finding Phi3.5-Vision achieved 80.4% accuracy on E-DAIC while Qwen2-VL scored only 33.9%, with both models over-predicting depression. Boosted Enhanced Quantile Regression Neural Networks with Spatiotemporal Permutation Entropy for Complex System Prognostics
Authors: David J Poland
First: 2025-07-14T08:19:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:14:20+00:00
Comments: Preliminary version of a predictive maintenance framework using spiking neural networks and entropy-based analysis. To be expanded in future publications with hardware implementations and real-time drift detection modules. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2501.05087
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an integrative prognostic framework that combines Spatiotemporal Permutation Entropy (STPE), Boosted Enhanced Quantile Regression Neural Networks (B-EQRNNs), Gated Temporal Attention, a Spiking Neural Network (SNN) refinement stage, and a Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) classifier. The motivation is long-horizon fault prediction in distributed industrial electronic systems, where single-sensor or point-estimate models can miss weak spatially propagating degradation signatures and provide limited uncertainty information. The proposed pipeline first converts 70-channel sensor streams into multiscale STPE descriptors, then learns conditional quantile representations and attention-weighted temporal context before final Normal/Abnormal classification. Evaluation is reported on a nine-system industrial electronic-sensor dataset with 48-, 90-, and 168-hour prediction horizons. The comparison includes a tree-based LightGBM baseline and modern sequence baselines available under the same preprocessing protocol, including LSTM, Autoformer, and TCN models. The full pipeline reaches 81.17% accuracy at the 168-hour horizon and is evaluated with component ablations, computational-cost analysis, and an explicit reproducibility protocol. The contribution is therefore framed as a validated hybrid architecture for uncertainty-aware spatiotemporal prognostics rather than as a new standalone learning theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于时空排列熵的增强分位数回归神经网络用于复杂系统预测
本文提出了一种集成预测框架，结合时空排列熵（STPE）、增强分位数回归神经网络（B-EQRNNs）、门控时间注意力机制、脉冲神经网络（SNN）优化阶段和时间融合变换器（TFT）分类器。该框架旨在解决分布式工业电子系统中长期故障预测问题，单传感器或点估计模型易遗漏空间传播的弱退化特征且不确定性信息有限。该流程首先将70通道传感器数据转化为多尺度STPE描述符，随后学习条件分位数表示并加权时间上下文，最终进行正常/异常分类。评估基于包含48小时、90小时和168小时预测时域的九个工业电子传感器数据集。对比基线包括树模型LightGBM及在相同预处理协议下可用的现代序列模型（LSTM、Autoformer、TCN）。完整流程在168小时预测时域下达到81.17%准确率，并通过组件消融、计算成本分析及明确可复现协议进行评估。本文贡献定位为一种经验证的混合架构，用于不确定性感知的时空预测，而非新的独立学习理论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate long-horizon fault prediction in distributed industrial systems, this work proposes a hybrid prognostic framework combining Spatiotemporal Permutation Entropy for feature extraction, Boosted Enhanced Quantile Regression Neural Networks for uncertainty-aware modeling, Gated Temporal Attention for context weighting, a Spiking Neural Network for refinement, and a Temporal Fusion Transformer for classification. Evaluated on a nine-system sensor dataset across 48-, 90-, and 168-hour horizons, the full pipeline achieved 81.17% accuracy at the longest horizon, outperforming baselines including LightGBM, LSTM, Autoformer, and TCN under identical preprocessing. The study includes ablation analysis and computational profiling, positioning the method as a validated architecture for spatiotemporal prognostics rather than a novel theoretical contribution.
为解决分布式工业系统中长期故障预测的挑战，该研究提出一种融合时空排列熵、增强分位数回归神经网络、门控时间注意力机制、脉冲神经网络和时序融合变换器的混合预测框架。在包含九个系统的传感器数据集上，针对48、90和168小时预测窗口进行评估，该完整流程在168小时窗口下达到81.17%的准确率，优于LightGBM、LSTM、Autoformer和TCN等基线模型。通过模块消融和计算成本分析，验证了该架构在时空预测中对不确定性建模的有效性，其贡献聚焦于可验证的混合架构，而非新的学习理论。
When Do Multi-Agent Systems Help? An Information Bottleneck Perspective
Authors: Wendi Yu, Lianhao Zhou, Xiangjue Dong, Sai Sudarshan Barath, Declan Staunton, Byung-Jun Yoon, Xiaoning Qian, James Caverlee, Shuiwang Ji
First: 2026-07-17T17:13:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:13:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM powered multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex tasks. However, their advantages over single-agent systems (SAS) remain unclear, with performance varying inconsistently across settings. Here, we provide an information bottleneck perspective on elucidating the differences between MAS and SAS. Specifically, our key observation is that a SAS accumulates its full reasoning trace in one shared context, while a MAS uses isolated local contexts connected by bounded relay messages. We show that, under infinite relay bandwidth, any SAS can be simulated by a MAS that transmits the full upstream context. Thus, the nontrivial advantage of MAS arises under bounded relays, where compression introduces a fundamental trade-off: reducing redundant context can improve efficiency, but may also incur loss of task-relevant information. We formalize this trade-off as an information bottleneck controlled by an effective parameter $β$, which captures how the balance shifts with model capability, and shows that MAS gains arise when context reduction outweighs relay information loss. We conduct 18 controlled experiments across five benchmarks and three model scales to validate our theoretical studies. We observe that MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models. In contrast, MAS gains shrink or reverse when relays incur information loss, especially for stronger models that can already extract useful information from redundant context and thus gain little from compression. Our study shows that multi-agent design is fundamentally an information-bottleneck optimization problem. This perspective explains when bounded inter-agent communication helps or hurts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体系统何时有益？信息瓶颈视角
基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为处理复杂任务的有前景范式。然而，其相对于单智能体系统（SAS）的优势尚不明确，且性能在不同设置下表现不一。本文从信息瓶颈视角阐明MAS与SAS的差异。关键观察是：SAS在单一共享上下文中累积完整推理轨迹，而MAS则通过有界中继消息连接独立局部上下文。我们证明，在无限中继带宽下，任何SAS均可被MAS模拟，后者传输完整上游上下文。因此，MAS的非平凡优势源于有界中继，此时压缩引入根本性权衡：减少冗余上下文可提升效率，但可能损失任务相关信息。我们形式化该权衡为由有效参数β控制的信息瓶颈，β反映模型能力下权衡的转移，且表明当上下文压缩收益超过中继信息损失时，MAS获益。我们在五个基准、三种模型规模上开展18项对照实验验证理论。观察到：当中继近似充足时，MAS尤其对弱模型持续有益；相反，当中继导致信息损失时，MAS收益缩小或逆转，尤其强模型已能从冗余上下文提取有用信息，压缩收益微弱。本研究揭示多智能体设计本质上是信息瓶颈优化问题，该视角解释有界智能体间通信何时有益或有害。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when multi-agent systems (MAS) outperform single-agent systems (SAS) by framing the problem through an information bottleneck lens. The authors argue that MAS gain advantages only under bounded communication, where compressing redundant context improves efficiency but risks losing task-relevant information, formalized by a parameter β that balances compression and relay fidelity. Experiments across 18 settings with three model scales and five benchmarks show MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models, but performance degrades or reverses when relays incur information loss, particularly for stronger models that already benefit from redundant context.
本文从信息瓶颈视角探讨多智能体系统（MAS）何时优于单智能体系统（SAS）。作者指出，SAS在单一共享上下文中累积完整推理轨迹，而MAS通过受限的中继消息压缩上下文，形成效率与信息保留之间的权衡。他们用参数β形式化这一权衡，反映模型能力如何影响压缩与信息损失的平衡，预测当压缩收益超过中继损失时MAS更具优势。实验结果表明，在中继带宽接近充足时，MAS对弱模型表现更优；而强模型因能从冗余上下文中获益，当中继导致信息损失时，MAS优势反而减弱或消失。
ToolSciVer: Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification with Visual Tool Augmented Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Binglin Zhou, Peng Shi, Ryo Kamoi, Nan Zhang, Rui Zhang
First: 2026-07-17T17:11:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:11:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification (MSCV) requires models to verify scientific claims using visually grounded evidence from papers, including figures, tables, charts, and textual context. However, existing methods often fail because they struggle to locate decisive visual evidence, accurately read structured scientific visuals, and integrate multimodal observations into reliable reasoning. We introduce ToolSciVer, the first tool-augmented framework for MSCV to our knowledge. ToolSciVer equips a VLM with three type-aware visual tools, table row/column focus, chart-to-structure parsing, and high-resolution region zoom, which convert dense scientific visuals into explicit, claim-facing evidence, and trains the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) under a composite reward of answer correctness, format validity, length control, tool-use efficiency, and tool-validity penalties. Experiments on SciVer and MuSciClaims datasets on five VLMs from three model families (Qwen, InternVL, Gemma) demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance compared to four competitive baselines including prompting-based and RL-based tool-use methods, highlighting the effectiveness of learned, type-aware tool use for scientific claim verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ToolSciVer：基于视觉工具增强强化学习的多模态科学声明验证
多模态科学声明验证（MSCV）要求模型利用论文中的视觉锚定证据（包括图表、表格、图形和文本上下文）验证科学声明。然而现有方法常因难以定位决定性视觉证据、准确读取结构化科学视觉内容，以及整合多模态观察进行可靠推理而失效。我们提出ToolSciVer，这是目前已知首个工具增强框架用于MSCV。ToolSciVer为视觉语言模型配备三种类型感知视觉工具：表格行列聚焦、图表结构解析与高分辨率区域缩放，将密集科学视觉内容转化为明确、面向声明的证据，并采用组合奖励（答案正确性、格式有效性、长度控制、工具使用效率及工具有效性惩罚）下的组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练策略。在SciVer与MuSciClaims数据集上，针对三类模型家族（Qwen、InternVL、Gemma）的五种视觉语言模型的实验表明，我们的方法相较包括基于提示与基于强化学习的工具使用方法在内的四个竞争基线方法表现更优，凸显了学习型、类型感知工具使用在科学声明验证中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of verifying scientific claims using multimodal evidence from research papers, ToolSciVer introduces a novel framework that augments vision-language models with three type-aware visual tools—table row/column focus, chart-to-structure parsing, and high-resolution region zoom—to extract precise, claim-relevant visual evidence. The method trains a reinforcement learning policy using Group Relative Policy Optimization, optimizing for answer correctness, format validity, length control, tool efficiency, and tool validity. Experimental results on SciVer and MuSciClaims datasets across five VLMs from three model families show ToolSciVer outperforms four baseline methods, including prompting and RL-based tool-use approaches, demonstrating the effectiveness of learned, task-specific tool usage in scientific claim verification.
为解决科学论文中多模态证据（如图表、表格和文本）难以定位与整合的问题，ToolSciVer首次提出一种工具增强框架，通过三种类型感知的视觉工具（表格行列聚焦、图表结构解析、高分辨率区域缩放）将复杂科学视觉转化为明确的、面向主张的证据。模型采用组相对策略优化方法，在综合奖励机制下训练，奖励涵盖答案正确性、格式有效性、长度控制、工具使用效率及工具有效性惩罚。在两个数据集上对五种视觉语言模型的实验表明，ToolSciVer显著优于四类基线方法（含提示与强化学习方法），验证了学习型、类型感知工具在科学主张验证中的有效性。
A Methodology for Auditable Trustworthiness Levels in AI Lifecycle Governance
Authors: Andrea Ferrario
First: 2026-07-17T17:11:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:11:22+00:00
Comments: 32 pages; 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI governance increasingly requires judgments about whether an AI system remains adequately trustworthy over time, whether observed changes are tolerable, and how such judgments should be documented in a transparent and contestable way. Yet existing work on AI trustworthiness remains either too high-level to support lifecycle monitoring and reassessment or too narrowly metric-driven to connect with governance needs. We therefore propose a lightweight methodology for auditable trustworthiness levels in AI governance. The methodology has two components: a formal framework for representing and learning trustworthiness levels, and a lightweight AI lifecycle governance procedure for documenting, monitoring, and reassessing them over time. The formal framework models governance-relative trustworthiness through a context-sensitive protocol of measurable dimensions and learns trustworthiness levels as interpretable rules over trustworthiness profiles. Using decision trees as an interpretable proof-of-concept model class, the methodology yields explicit trustworthiness plateaus, readable level transitions, and two simple lifecycle diagnostics: boundary margins and profile drift. The governance procedure embeds these formal objects in a conformity-oriented workflow for design-time labeling, post-deployment monitoring, reassessment, and reporting. It also assigns human responsibilities and control gates for protocol design, validation, monitoring, and reassessment. We illustrate the methodology on synthetic AI lifecycle traces involving degradation, shocks, updates, heterogeneous monitoring cadences, and system comparison. Our methodology does not replace legal or other expert judgment: it supports conformity documentation and lifecycle monitoring by providing an evidential basis for documenting and tracking AI governance-relevant changes over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能生命周期治理中可审计可信度等级的方法论
人工智能治理日益需要判断AI系统在时间维度上是否保持足够可信、观察到的变化是否可接受，以及如何以透明且可争议的方式记录此类判断。然而，现有AI可信度研究要么过于宏观，无法支持生命周期监控与再评估，要么过于狭隘地依赖指标，难以对接治理需求。因此，我们提出一种轻量级的、可审计的AI治理可信度等级方法论。该方法论包含两个部分：一是形式化框架，用于表示和学习可信度等级；二是轻量级AI生命周期治理程序，用于记录、监控和随时间再评估可信度等级。形式化框架通过情境敏感的可测量维度协议建模治理相对可信度，并将可信度等级学习为可信度剖面的可解释规则。采用决策树作为可解释的原型模型类，该方法论可生成明确的可信度平台、可读的等级转换，以及两个简单的生命周期诊断指标：边界裕度与剖面漂移。治理程序将这些形式化对象嵌入以合规为导向的工作流程中，涵盖设计时标注、部署后监控、再评估与报告。同时，该程序还分配人类责任与控制门限，用于协议设计、验证、监控与再评估。我们通过合成AI生命周期轨迹（涉及退化、冲击、更新、异质监控周期及系统比较）演示该方法论。本方法论不替代法律或其他专家判断，而是通过提供证据基础，支持合规文档化与生命周期监控，以记录和追踪AI治理相关变化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for transparent, auditable trustworthiness assessments throughout an AI system's lifecycle, this paper proposes a lightweight methodology that combines a formal framework for modeling governance-relative trustworthiness with a procedural workflow for monitoring and reassessing it over time. The framework uses context-sensitive, measurable dimensions and learns interpretable trustworthiness rules via decision trees, enabling explicit trustworthiness plateaus and readable transitions, along with two diagnostics: boundary margins and profile drift. The governance procedure embeds these formal elements into a conformity-oriented workflow that includes human responsibilities and control gates for design, validation, monitoring, and reassessment. Experimental results on synthetic AI lifecycle traces demonstrate the method’s ability to detect degradation, shocks, updates, and heterogeneous monitoring patterns, supporting evidence-based documentation of governance-relevant changes without replacing expert judgment.
为支持AI系统在生命周期内透明、可争议地监控和记录其可信度，本文提出一种轻量级方法，结合形式化框架与治理流程。框架通过上下文敏感的可测量维度建模治理相关可信度，利用可解释决策树定义可信度等级与转换，输出明确的可信度平台、可读的等级变化及两个诊断指标：边界裕度与轮廓漂移。治理流程将这些形式化对象嵌入到设计时标注、部署后监控、再评估与报告的工作流中，并分配人类责任与控制门。在合成AI生命周期轨迹实验中，该方法有效追踪了退化、冲击、更新及异构监控等场景，支持基于证据的治理，不替代专家判断。
Connectivity Maintenance and Recovery for Multi-Robot Motion Planning
Authors: Yutong Wang, Lishuo Pan, Yichun Qu, Tengxiang Wang, Nora Ayanian
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2025-10-03T20:32:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:08:18+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures. To appear in the Proceedings of the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026), Pittsburgh, PA, USA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Connectivity is crucial in many multi-robot applications, yet balancing connectivity maintenance and fleet traversability in obstacle-rich environments remains challenging. Reactive controllers based on control barrier functions can preserve connectivity when it is initially satisfied, but often struggle with deadlocks in cluttered environments. We propose a real-time Bézier-based constrained motion planning algorithm, namely MPC--CLF--CBF, that produces trajectories and control inputs concurrently, subject to high-order control barrier function and control Lyapunov function constraints. Our motion planner supports connectivity-aware navigation in cluttered workspaces and recovers connectivity from initially disconnected configurations and after temporary obstacle-induced separation; it also provides analytic continuous-time derivatives, facilitating its application to agile differentially flat systems such as quadrotors. In simulations with $4$--$12$ robots, it maintains $95.8$--$100\%$ graph-connected time at $20\%$ obstacle density, compared with $48.9$--$61.3\%$ for MPC--CBF, with no observed collisions. We further validate the planner in a physical experiment with $8$ Crazyflie nano-quadrotors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多机器人运动规划中的连通性维护与恢复
连通性在许多多机器人应用中至关重要，但在障碍物密集环境中平衡连通性维护与编队可通行性仍具挑战。基于控制屏障函数的反应式控制器可在连通性初始满足时保持连通，但在复杂环境中常陷入死锁。本文提出一种实时贝塞尔约束运动规划算法MPC--CLF--CBF，同时生成轨迹与控制输入，满足高阶控制屏障函数与控制李雅普诺夫函数约束。该运动规划器支持在复杂工作空间中实现连通性感知导航，可从初始断开配置或因障碍物临时分离后恢复连通性；同时提供解析连续时间导数，便于应用于敏捷微分平坦系统（如四旋翼）。在4至12机器人仿真中，其在20%障碍密度下保持95.8%至100%图连通时间，相较MPC--CBF的48.9%至61.3%显著提升，且未观察到碰撞。进一步在8架Crazyflie纳米四旋翼的物理实验中验证了该规划器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to maintain robot connectivity in cluttered environments while enabling efficient motion, the paper introduces a real-time motion planner called MPC--CLF--CBF that integrates control barrier functions and Lyapunov functions to enforce connectivity and stability simultaneously. The planner generates continuous trajectories and control inputs using Bézier curves, enabling recovery from initial disconnection and obstacle-induced separation. In simulations with 4–12 robots at 20% obstacle density, it achieved 95.8–100% graph-connected time, significantly outperforming MPC--CBF (48.9–61.3%) without collisions, and was validated in a physical experiment with 8 quadrotors.
为在障碍物密集环境中维持多机器人连通性并实现高效运动，本文提出一种名为MPC--CLF--CBF的实时运动规划器，通过结合控制屏障函数与控制李雅普诺夫函数，同步满足连通性与稳定性约束。该规划器利用贝塞尔曲线生成连续轨迹与控制输入，支持从初始断开或障碍物导致的分离中恢复连通性。在4至12个机器人、20%障碍密度的仿真中，其连通时间占比达95.8%至100%，显著优于MPC--CBF的48.9%至61.3%，且无碰撞发生。该方法已在8个微型四旋翼飞行器的物理实验中验证。
Toward Semantic Communication for Real-time Mobile 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Fangzhou Zhao, Yao Sun, Xuesong Liu, Runze Cheng, Shang Kai, Yi Sun
First: 2026-07-17T17:07:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:07:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time mobile 3D reconstruction is fundamental to many emerging applications such as autonomous navigation and digital twin construction, where a moving platform continuously captures an image stream and transmit to a computing server for scene understanding. Unlike offline reconstruction, camera poses and scene geometry are estimated on-the-fly during acquisition, making multi-view consistency a real-time requirement and rendering geometric estimation highly sensitive to communication-induced distortions. Semantic communication (SemCom) transmits compact semantic information, offering a promising way to preserve task-critical data over unreliable links. However, existing designs are optimized at the image or single-view level and without providing explicit reliability information for geometric estimation, limiting their applicability to real-time mobile 3D reconstruction. In this context, we propose a SemCom framework for real-time mobile 3D reconstruction. The framework includes a semantic transceiver that outputs a reconstructed image alongside a pixel-wise confidence map, quantifying the reliability of each region. We further introduce a confidence-guided geometric estimation method, incorporating confidence into RANSAC-based pose initialization and bundle adjustment to reduce the influence of unreliable regions and enhance robustness under noisy channels. Simulations show that, compared to existing SemCom and traditional seperate source and channel coding, our framework maintains high image quality while significantly improving pose estimation accuracy and 3D structural consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向实时移动3D重建的语义通信
实时移动3D重建是自动驾驶导航和数字孪生构建等新兴应用的基础，其中移动平台持续捕获图像流并传输至计算服务器以理解场景。与离线重建不同，相机姿态和场景几何结构在采集过程中实时估计，使多视角一致性成为实时要求，几何估计对通信引起的失真高度敏感。语义通信（SemCom）传输紧凑的语义信息，为在不可靠链路上保留任务关键数据提供了有前景的方案。然而，现有设计仅针对图像或单视角优化，且未为几何估计提供显式可靠性信息，限制其在实时移动3D重建中的适用性。为此，我们提出了一种面向实时移动3D重建的语义通信框架。该框架包含一个语义收发器，输出重建图像及像素级置信度图，量化各区域的可靠性。我们进一步引入置信度引导的几何估计方法，将置信度融入基于RANSAC的姿态初始化和束调整中，以降低不可靠区域的影响，提升在噪声信道下的鲁棒性。仿真表明，与现有语义通信及传统分离信源信道编码相比，我们的框架在保持高图像质量的同时，显著提升了姿态估计精度和3D结构一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time 3D reconstruction on mobile platforms where communication delays and noise degrade geometric accuracy, this paper proposes a semantic communication framework that transmits pixel-wise confidence maps alongside reconstructed images to guide geometric estimation. The method integrates confidence-aware RANSAC pose initialization and bundle adjustment to mitigate the impact of unreliable regions, enhancing robustness under noisy channels. Experimental results demonstrate that compared to existing semantic communication and traditional separate source-channel coding approaches, the proposed framework preserves high image fidelity while significantly improving pose estimation accuracy and 3D structural consistency.
为满足移动设备实时三维重建在自动驾驶导航等场景中的需求，本文针对通信信道不可靠导致几何估计精度下降的问题，提出一种语义通信框架。该框架包含语义收发器，输出重建图像及像素级置信度图，用于量化各区域可靠性；并引入置信度引导的几何估计方法，将置信度融入RANSAC姿态初始化与光束法平差，降低不可靠区域影响。实验表明，该方法在保持图像质量的同时，显著提升姿态估计精度和三维结构一致性，优于现有语义通信及传统分离信源信道编码方案。
RESOURCE2SKILL: Distilling Executable Agent Skills from Human-Created Multimodal Resources
Authors: Yijia Fan, Zonglin Di, Zimo Wen, Yifan Yang, Mingxi Cheng, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yue Dong, Ji Li, Chong Luo
First: 2026-06-28T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:01:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills are a useful abstraction for software agents, turning human and agent experience into reusable procedural knowledge. Yet existing skill libraries are mostly hand-written, text-centric, or derived from agent traces, leaving tutorial videos and other multimodal human resources largely underused. We present RESOURCE2SKILL, a framework that distills multimodal resources, including tutorial videos, repositories, articles, and reference artifacts, into executable skills for software agents. RESOURCE2SKILL organizes these skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, where each entry combines structured text, code, visual examples, metadata, and provenance. This design preserves complementary signals from different resources: videos capture temporal operations and visual effects, code captures executable tool patterns, and articles or artifacts provide conceptual and stylistic grounding. At inference time, agents retrieve and compose relevant skills from the wiki; when coverage is insufficient, the same construction operator can acquire new skills online. Across seven practical authoring domains, RESOURCE2SKILL improves average overall score by +11.9 percentage points over no-skill agents and outperforms strong harness baselines in 26 of 28 main-aggregate model-domain cells. Ablations confirm the value of multimodal skill format, hierarchical organization, source diversity, selection strategy, and online acquisition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RESOURCE2SKILL：从人类创建的多模态资源中提炼可执行的代理技能
技能是对软件代理有用的抽象，能将人类和代理的经验转化为可复用的程序性知识。然而，现有技能库大多由人工编写、以文本为中心，或源自代理轨迹，导致教程视频等多模态人类资源被大量闲置。我们提出RESOURCE2SKILL框架，将教程视频、仓库、文章和参考制品等多模态资源提炼为软件代理的可执行技能。RESOURCE2SKILL将这些技能组织为分层多模态技能维基，每个条目结合结构化文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据和溯源信息。该设计保留了不同资源的互补信号：视频捕捉时序操作与视觉效果，代码捕捉可执行工具模式，文章或制品提供概念与风格基础。推理时，代理从维基中检索并组合相关技能；当覆盖不足时，同一构建操作符可在线获取新技能。在七个实际创作领域中，RESOURCE2SKILL相较无技能代理平均整体得分提升11.9个百分点，并在28个主模型-领域单元中的26个中优于强基线方法。消融实验验证了多模态技能格式、分层组织、源多样性、选择策略和在线获取的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of multimodal human resources like tutorial videos and code repositories in agent skill development, RESOURCE2SKILL proposes a framework that distills these diverse sources into executable agent skills. It structures skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, integrating text, code, visuals, metadata, and provenance to preserve complementary signals from each resource type. Experimental results across seven authoring domains show that the framework improves average performance by +11.9 percentage points over agents with no skills and outperforms strong baseline methods in 26 of 28 model-domain combinations, with ablation studies confirming the importance of multimodal format, hierarchy, source diversity, selection strategy, and online skill acquisition.
为解决教程视频和代码仓库等多模态人类资源在智能体技能构建中的利用率低问题，RESOURCE2SKILL提出将这些资源提炼为可执行技能，并以分层多模态技能维基的形式组织，融合文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据和溯源信息，保留不同模态的互补信号。在七个实际创作领域中，该框架使智能体平均性能提升11.9个百分点，优于无技能基线，并在28个模型-领域组合中的26个中超越强基线，消融实验验证了多模态格式、层级结构、资源多样性、选择策略和在线获取能力的重要性。
BayesContact: Uncertain Pose Estimation via Visuo-Tactile Proposals and Simulation-based Inference
Authors: Aditya Kamireddypalli, Matias Mattamala, Joao Moura, Russell Buchanan, Sethu Vijayakumar, Subramanian Ramamoorthy
First: 2026-07-17T17:00:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:00:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contact-rich manipulation requires pose estimates that are often more accurate than what depth-only sensing provides. Existing methods, relying on vision and contact, employ costly offline training procedures that need to be retrained for new environments and geometries. We propose BayesContact, a Simulation-Based Inference framework for visuo-tactile pose estimation in peg-in-hole insertion. BayesContact maintains a particle belief over object pose and fuses depth observations with force/torque-derived contact evidence. We employ simulation based forward models to approximate these observation likelihoods. For each pose hypothesis, a renderer predicts depth measurements and a physics simulator predicts contact outcomes under guarded probing actions; both are scored against real observations to update the belief. The resulting multimodal belief also enables information-gain-based probing for active disambiguation. Across simulated geometries and real-robot experiments, BayesContact improves pose observability and insertion success over vision-only inference by 30%
中文标题/摘要
标题：BayesContact：通过视觉-触觉提案与基于模拟的推断实现不确定姿态估计
接触密集型操作需要比仅深度感知更精确的姿态估计。现有方法依赖视觉和接触信息，但需昂贵的离线训练过程，且需针对新环境和几何形状重新训练。我们提出BayesContact，一种面向销插入孔任务的视觉-触觉姿态估计的基于模拟的推断框架。BayesContact维护对象姿态的粒子信念，并融合深度观测与由力/力矩推导的接触证据。我们采用基于模拟的前向模型近似这些观测似然。对每个姿态假设，渲染器预测深度测量值，物理模拟器预测受保护探针动作下的接触结果；两者均与真实观测对比以更新信念。所得多模态信念还可支持基于信息增益的主动探针，用于姿态歧义消解。在模拟几何与真实机器人实验中，BayesContact相比仅视觉推断，姿态可观察性与插入成功率提升30%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate pose estimation in contact-rich manipulation tasks where depth sensing alone is insufficient, BayesContact introduces a simulation-based inference framework that fuses visual and tactile data. It maintains a particle-based belief over object pose and uses rendered depth and simulated contact outcomes to score each hypothesis against real measurements, updating the belief iteratively. Experimental results across simulated and real robotic environments show that BayesContact improves pose observability and insertion success by 30% compared to vision-only methods.
为解决接触密集操作中深度感知精度不足的问题，BayesContact提出一种基于仿真的推理框架，融合视觉与触觉信息。该方法通过粒子信念表示物体位姿，并利用渲染器预测深度观测、物理仿真预测接触结果，对每个位姿假设进行评分并更新信念。实验表明，在模拟与真实机器人环境中，BayesContact相比纯视觉方法，使位姿可观察性与插入成功率提升30%。
CRAFT: Clustering Rubrics to Diagnose Weak LLM Capabilities and Generate Targeted Fine-Tuning Data
Authors: Vipul Gupta, Zihao Wang, Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru, MohammadHossein Rezaei, Aakash Sabharwal, Yunzhong He
First: 2026-07-17T17:00:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T17:00:38+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 3 Tables, 2 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluations should do more than measure a models current performance. They should tell us what to fix for the next model iteration and provide a way to generate targeted post training data. Most evaluation pipelines identify weak examples, topics, or categories, but they leave the underlying capability failure implicit: they say where a model fails, not why. We introduce CRAFT, a method that converts any rubric based evaluation dataset into a model specific diagnosis of weak capabilities. CRAFT treats each grading criterion as a capability probe: it extracts a capability description from every prompt rubric pair, clusters these descriptions into a hierarchical capability tree, scores the target model at every node, and selects low performing nodes dynamically across tree levels, at the granularity where each failure is clearest. The selected weak capabilities then direct the generation of targeted supervised finetuning data. Holding the data generation, finetuning, and evaluation setup fixed, we compare CRAFT against prompt level EvalTree clustering and untargeted random generation on four open source models, two professional domains (finance and legal), and 13 held out benchmarks disjoint from the diagnostic data. CRAFT achieves the strongest finance domain average for all four models under repeated temperature decoding; on legal domain, it is strongest for three of four models and remains within the decoding variance bands of the best baseline on the fourth. Diagnosing weaknesses at the level of rubric criteria, rather than prompts or categories, thus yields both a sharper picture of what a model cannot do and measurably better models after finetuning on that diagnosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRAFT：通过聚类评分标准诊断大语言模型能力缺陷并生成定向微调数据
评估不应仅测量模型当前性能，还应指导下一版本模型的改进方向，并提供生成定向后训练数据的方法。大多数评估流程能识别薄弱示例、主题或类别，但隐含了能力缺陷的本质：它们说明模型在何处失败，而非为何失败。我们提出CRAFT方法，可将任何基于评分标准的评估数据集转化为针对特定模型的能力缺陷诊断。CRAFT将每个评分标准视为能力探测器：从每个提示-评分标准对中提取能力描述，将这些描述聚类为分层能力树，对目标模型在每个节点进行评分，并在树的不同层级动态选择表现较差的节点，以最清晰呈现每个失败点的粒度。选定的薄弱能力随后指导定向监督微调数据的生成。在固定数据生成、微调和评估设置的前提下，我们在四个开源模型、两个专业领域（金融与法律）及13个与诊断数据不重叠的基准测试中，将CRAFT与提示级EvalTree聚类和无定向随机生成方法进行比较。CRAFT在重复温度解码下，对所有四个模型在金融领域均取得最强平均表现；在法律领域，其在四个模型中三个表现最佳，且在第四个模型上仍处于最佳基线的解码方差范围内。因此，以评分标准而非提示或类别为单位诊断弱点，不仅更清晰地揭示模型无法完成的任务，还能显著提升微调后的模型性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to diagnose specific capability failures in LLMs beyond surface-level performance metrics, CRAFT transforms rubric-based evaluation datasets into hierarchical capability trees by treating each rubric criterion as a probe. It clusters capability descriptions derived from prompt-rubric pairs, scores the target model at each node, and selects underperforming nodes dynamically to guide targeted supervised fine-tuning data generation. Experiments across four open-source models and two domains (finance and legal) show CRAFT outperforms baseline methods, achieving the best average performance in finance for all models and leading in legal for three, with consistent gains across benchmarks.
为推动模型迭代优化，CRAFT提出一种基于评分标准的弱能力诊断方法，将评分项描述聚类构建能力树，对模型在各节点进行评分并动态选取低分节点生成定向微调数据。在金融与法律两个领域对四款开源模型的实验表明，CRAFT在金融领域全面优于基线，在法律领域三款模型领先，一款保持竞争力。该方法通过评分标准粒度诊断能力缺陷，不仅更清晰揭示模型短板，也显著提升微调后模型性能。
Minimax and Bayes Optimal Best-Arm Identification
Authors: Masahiro Kato
First: 2025-06-30T16:11:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:56:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates minimax and Bayes optimal strategies for fixed-budget best-arm identification. We consider an adaptive procedure consisting of a sampling phase followed by a recommendation phase, and we design an adaptive experiment within this framework to efficiently identify the best arm, defined as the one with the highest expected outcome. In our proposed strategy, the sampling phase consists of two stages. The first stage is a pilot phase, in which we allocate samples uniformly across arms to eliminate clearly suboptimal arms and to estimate outcome variances. Before entering the second stage, we solve a Gaussian minimax game, which yields a sampling policy and a decision rule. In the second stage, samples are allocated according to this policy. After the sampling phase, the procedure enters the recommendation phase, where we select an arm using the decision rule. We prove that this single strategy is simultaneously asymptotically minimax and Bayes optimal for the simple regret, and we establish upper bounds that coincide exactly with our lower bounds, including the constant terms. The lower bounds hold against every adaptive experiment and for every fixed number of arms, and the strategy attains them without knowing the outcome distributions or the prior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最小最大与贝叶斯最优最佳臂识别
本研究探讨了固定预算下最佳臂识别的最小最大与贝叶斯最优策略。我们考虑一种包含采样阶段和推荐阶段的自适应程序，并在此框架内设计自适应实验以高效识别最佳臂（即期望收益最高的臂）。所提策略的采样阶段包含两个阶段：第一阶段为试点阶段，均匀分配样本至各臂以剔除明显次优臂并估计收益方差；进入第二阶段前，我们求解高斯最小最大博弈，得到采样策略与决策规则；第二阶段按该策略分配样本。采样阶段结束后，进入推荐阶段，依据决策规则选择臂。我们证明该单一策略对简单后悔值同时渐近最小最大与贝叶斯最优，并建立上界与下界完全一致（含常数项）；下界对所有自适应实验及任意固定臂数均成立，且策略无需已知收益分布或先验即可达到下界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently identify the best arm under fixed budgets, this study proposes an adaptive two-stage procedure combining a pilot sampling phase and a Gaussian minimax game-driven sampling phase, followed by a decision rule-based recommendation phase. The pilot phase uniformly samples arms to eliminate poor candidates and estimate variances, while the second phase allocates samples according to a minimax-optimal policy derived from solving a Gaussian game. The method achieves asymptotic minimax and Bayes optimality for simple regret, with tight upper and lower bounds that match exactly, including constant terms, without requiring knowledge of outcome distributions or priors.
为在固定预算下高效识别最优臂，本研究提出一种两阶段自适应方法：先通过均匀采样进行初步筛选剔除明显劣质臂，再基于高斯最小最大博弈确定采样策略与决策规则，最后根据决策规则推荐最优臂。实验表明，该策略在简单后悔度上同时达到渐近最小最大与贝叶斯最优，其上界与已知下界完全一致（含常数项），适用于所有自适应实验和固定臂数，且无需事先知晓结果分布或先验信息。
Rate-Utility Frontiers for Language Encodings: Comparing Tokens, Bytes, and Pixels Under Controlled Linguistic Content
Authors: Ingo Ziegler, Martin Krebs, Desmond Elliott
First: 2026-07-17T16:55:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:55:27+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Code available at https://github.com/ziegler-ingo/rate-utility-frontiers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Language models encode text as subword tokens, raw bytes, or rendered pixels, but these encodings are usually compared under modeling constraints that expose different amounts of linguistic content to models across different languages. We instead ask what each encoding preserves when both the content and the downstream capacity are controlled. Using verified parallel sentences across thirteen languages and five scripts, we compare tokens, bytes, and pixels through a shared bottleneck whose width is swept to trace rate-utility frontiers. This separates three quantities that are often conflated: the number of input positions an encoding creates, the latent capacity available after encoding, and the task-relevant information that survives compression. We evaluate three utilities: surface form preservation, cross-lingual sentence alignment, and topic classification. No encoding dominates across tasks or capacity regimes. Pixels preserve surface form best, bytes preserve cross-lingual alignment best, especially in same-script multilingual settings, and tokens support topic prediction best. These performances are not explained by sequence length alone. Short inputs can discard useful meaning, while long inputs can preserve information that compresses well. Choosing an encoding is therefore not a fixed preference for tokens, bytes, or pixels, but a rate-utility tradeoff that depends on the task, language mix, capacity regime, and compute budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言编码的速率-效用前沿：在受控语言内容下比较词元、字节与像素
语言模型将文本编码为子词词元、原始字节或渲染像素，但这些编码通常在建模约束下进行比较，不同语言暴露给模型的语言内容量不同。我们转而探讨在内容和下游容量均受控的情况下，每种编码所保留的信息。通过十三种语言和五种文字系统的已验证平行句对，我们通过一个宽度可变的共享瓶颈比较词元、字节和像素，以绘制速率-效用前沿。这区分了常被混淆的三个量：编码所创建的输入位置数、编码后可用的潜在容量，以及压缩后保留的任务相关信息。我们评估三种效用：表层形式保留、跨语言句子对齐和主题分类。无一种编码在所有任务或容量范围内占优。像素在表层形式保留方面表现最佳，字节在跨语言对齐方面表现最佳，尤其在同文字系统多语言场景下，词元在主题预测方面表现最佳。这些性能不能仅由序列长度解释。短输入可能丢弃有用语义，而长输入可能保留压缩效果良好的信息。因此，选择编码并非固定偏好词元、字节或像素，而是取决于任务、语言混合、容量范围和计算预算的速率-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how different text encodings—tokens, bytes, and pixels—perform under controlled linguistic content and downstream capacity constraints, aiming to disentangle their trade-offs in information preservation. Using verified parallel sentences across thirteen languages and five scripts, the authors construct rate-utility frontiers by varying a shared bottleneck width, isolating three key factors: input position count, latent capacity, and task-relevant information retention. They evaluate three utilities: surface form preservation, cross-lingual alignment, and topic classification. Results show no single encoding universally outperforms others; pixels excel in surface form, bytes in cross-lingual alignment especially within same-script multilingual settings, and tokens in topic prediction. Performance cannot be explained by sequence length alone, as short inputs may lose meaning while long ones preserve compressible information, indicating encoding choice must be tailored to task, language mix, capacity, and compute constraints.
该研究在控制语言内容与下游容量的前提下，比较了文本编码方式（词元、字节、像素）在信息保留方面的表现，旨在区分它们的权衡关系。研究使用十三种语言和五种文字系统的验证平行句，通过调节共享瓶颈宽度构建速率-效用前沿，分离出三个关键因素：输入位置数、编码后潜在容量和任务相关信息保留量。评估三项效用：表层形式保留、跨语言句子对齐和主题分类。结果表明无单一编码方式全面占优：像素在表层形式保留上表现最佳，字节在跨语言对齐上表现最优——尤其在同文字系统多语言场景下，词元在主题预测上表现最佳。性能并非由序列长度决定，短输入可能丢失语义，长输入则可能保留可压缩信息，因此编码选择需根据任务、语言组合、容量和计算预算动态调整。
Student Evaluation of Repeated AI Feedback Across a Semester of Writing
Authors: Andres Karjus, Janika Leoste, Tiia Õun
First: 2026-07-17T16:53:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:53:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI is increasingly used for feedback in higher education, but evidence from repeated classroom use remains limited. This short paper analyses 2988 reflective essay-feedback-appraisal instances from 283 Estonian bachelor students across one semester. Students obtained and assessed feedback from a self-selected AI tool using a uniform prompt.
