The present analysis of the anonymized text corpus covers essay content, AI feedback, and its perceived helpfulness. Students found feedback helpful and actionable more often than not; about a tenth thought AI unhelpful, more so towards the end of the semester. We also analyzed essay reflection depth, and used a validated AI text classifier to estimate the share of essays that could be treated as likely unaided student writing. The study contributes descriptive classroom evidence on integration of AI feedback - a fast and scalable way to provide immediate writing advice, but not a self-contained route to better reflection. Benefits depend on whether students learn to use AI selectively and critically, without sliding into over-use harmful for the learning process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学生对一学期写作中重复AI反馈的评估
生成式AI在高等教育中的反馈应用日益增多，但重复课堂使用方面的实证证据仍有限。本文分析了283名爱沙尼亚本科生在学期中2988次反思性论文-反馈-评价实例。学生使用统一提示语，自行选择AI工具获取并评估反馈。匿名文本语料库的分析涵盖论文内容、AI反馈及其感知效用。学生普遍认为反馈有用且可操作；约十分之一的学生认为AI无用，且在学期末更明显。我们还分析了论文反思深度，并使用经验证的AI文本分类器估算可能为学生独立写作的论文比例。本研究提供了关于AI反馈整合的课堂描述性证据——这是一种快速可扩展的即时写作建议方式，但并非提升反思能力的独立途径。其效益取决于学生是否学会选择性、批判性地使用AI，避免过度依赖损害学习过程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of generative AI in higher education writing feedback but limited empirical evidence on its repeated classroom effectiveness, this study analyzed 2988 student feedback appraisals from 283 Estonian undergraduates over one semester. Students used a self-selected AI tool with a uniform prompt to generate feedback on their reflective essays, which was then assessed for perceived helpfulness alongside essay content and reflection depth. Results showed that AI feedback was generally viewed as helpful and actionable, though about 10% of students found it unhelpful, especially later in the semester. The study also estimated that a significant portion of essays were likely unaided student writing, using a validated AI classifier. The findings suggest AI feedback can be a fast, scalable tool for immediate writing guidance but does not inherently improve reflection; its educational value depends on students learning to use it critically and selectively to avoid over-reliance.
鉴于生成式AI在高等教育写作反馈中的日益普及，但其重复课堂应用的有效性证据仍有限，本研究分析了283名爱沙尼亚本科生在一个学期中提交的2988份反思性作文及其AI反馈评估。学生使用自选AI工具，按统一提示生成反馈，并对其有用性、作文内容及反思深度进行评价。结果显示，AI反馈总体上被学生认为有帮助且可操作，但约10%的学生在学期后期认为其无用。通过验证的AI文本分类器估算，大量作文仍属学生独立写作，表明AI反馈可作为写作支持工具，但其教育价值取决于学生能否批判性、有选择地使用，避免过度依赖损害学习过程。
Ruling Out to Rule In: Contrastive Hypothesis Retrieval for Medical Question Answering
Authors: Byeolhee Kim, Min-Kyung Kim, Young-Hak Kim, Tae-Joon Jeon
First: 2026-04-06T11:13:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:49:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external medical knowledge, yet standard retrievers frequently surface hard negatives that are semantically close to the query but describe clinically distinct conditions. While existing query-expansion methods improve query representation to mitigate ambiguity, they typically focus on enriching target-relevant semantics without an explicit mechanism to selectively suppress specific, clinically plausible hard negatives. This leaves the system prone to retrieving plausible mimics that overshadow the actual diagnosis, particularly when such mimics are dominant within the corpus. We propose Contrastive Hypothesis Retrieval (CHR), a framework inspired by the process of clinical differential diagnosis. CHR generates a target hypothesis $H^+$ for the likely correct answer and a mimic hypothesis $H^-$ for the most plausible incorrect alternative, then scores documents by promoting $H^+$-aligned evidence while penalizing $H^-$-aligned content. Across three medical QA benchmarks and three answer generators, CHR outperforms all five baselines in every configuration, with improvements of up to 10.4 percentage points over the next-best method. On the $n=587$ pooled cases where CHR answers correctly while embedded hypothetical-document query expansion does not, 85.2\% have no shared documents between the top-5 retrieval lists of CHR and of that baseline, consistent with substantive retrieval redirection rather than light re-ranking of the same candidates. By explicitly modeling what to avoid alongside what to find, CHR bridges clinical reasoning with retrieval mechanism design and offers a practical path to reducing hard-negative contamination in medical RAG systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：排除以确立：面向医学问答的对比假设检索
检索增强生成（RAG）使大语言模型基于外部医学知识，但标准检索器常返回语义接近查询却临床意义不同的硬负样本。现有查询扩展方法虽能改善查询表示以缓解歧义，但通常仅侧重丰富目标相关语义，缺乏明确机制选择性抑制特定临床合理硬负样本。这使系统易检索到掩盖真实诊断的合理模拟病例，尤其当此类模拟病例在语料库中占主导时。我们提出对比假设检索（CHR）框架，受临床鉴别诊断过程启发。CHR生成目标假设H⁺（最可能正确答案）和模拟假设H⁻（最可能错误替代），通过促进H⁺对齐证据、惩罚H⁻对齐内容对文档评分。在三个医学问答基准和三种答案生成器上，CHR在每种配置下均优于五种基线方法，最高提升达10.4个百分点。在CHR正确回答而嵌入式假设-文档查询扩展方法错误的587个合并案例中，85.2%案例CHR与该基线的前五检索列表无共享文档，表明实质性的检索重定向而非轻量级候选重排序。CHR通过显式建模应避免的内容与应寻找的内容，弥合临床推理与检索机制设计，为减少医学RAG系统中的硬负样本污染提供实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem of hard-negative contamination in medical retrieval-augmented generation, where semantically similar but clinically distinct conditions mislead retrieval, the paper introduces Contrastive Hypothesis Retrieval (CHR). CHR explicitly constructs a target hypothesis (H+) for the correct diagnosis and a mimic hypothesis (H-) for the most plausible incorrect alternative, then scores documents by boosting evidence aligned with H+ while penalizing content aligned with H-. Evaluated across three medical QA benchmarks and three LLMs, CHR consistently outperforms five baselines, achieving up to 10.4 percentage point gains. In 587 cases where CHR succeeded but a baseline failed, 85.2% showed no overlap in top-5 retrieved documents, indicating true retrieval redirection rather than superficial re-ranking.
为解决医学问答中硬负样本干扰问题——即语义相近但临床差异显著的条件误导检索结果，本文提出对比假设检索（CHR）框架。CHR明确生成目标假设（H+，正确诊断）与模仿假设（H-，最可能的错误替代），通过奖励与H+匹配的文档、惩罚与H-匹配的内容来评分。在三个医学问答基准和三种生成模型上，CHR在所有配置中均优于五个基线方法，最高提升达10.4个百分点。在CHR正确而基线失败的587个案例中，85.2%的案例在前五检索结果中无重叠，表明CHR实现了真正的检索重定向而非表面级重排序。
ContactFusion: Stochastic Poisson Surface Maps from Visual and Contact Sensing
Authors: Aditya Kamireddypalli, Joao Moura, Russell Buchanan, Matias Mattamala, Sethu Vijayakumar, Subramanian Ramamoorthy
First: 2025-03-20T17:53:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:45:32+00:00
Comments: Version accepted to IROS2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust and precise robotic assembly entails insertion of constituent components. Insertion success is hindered when noise in scene understanding exceeds tolerance limits, especially when fabricated with tight tolerances. In this work, we propose ContactFusion which combines global mapping with local contact information, fusing point clouds with force sensing. Our method entails a Rejection Sampling based contact occupancy sensing procedure which estimates contact locations on the end-effector from Force/Torque sensing at the wrist. We demonstrate how to fuse contact with visual information into a Stochastic Poisson Surface Map (SPSMap) - a map representation that can be updated with the Stochastic Poisson Surface Reconstruction (SPSR) algorithm. We first validate the contact occupancy sensor in simulation and show its ability to detect the contact location on the robot from force sensing information. Then, we evaluate our method in a peg-in-hole task, demonstrating an improvement in the hole pose estimate with the fusion of the contact information with the SPSMap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ContactFusion：基于视觉与接触感知的随机泊松表面图
稳健且精确的机器人装配需要插入各个部件。当场景理解中的噪声超过容限时，插入成功率会降低，尤其在公差极小的制造条件下。本文提出ContactFusion方法，结合全局地图与局部接触信息，将点云与力觉传感融合。该方法包含基于拒绝采样的接触占据感知过程，通过腕部力/力矩传感估计末端执行器上的接触位置。我们展示了如何将接触信息与视觉信息融合为随机泊松表面图（SPSMap）——一种可通过随机泊松表面重建（SPSR）算法更新的地图表示。首先在仿真中验证接触占据传感器，展示其从力觉信息中检测机器人接触位置的能力；随后在销孔插入任务中评估该方法，证明融合接触信息与SPSMap可提升孔位姿态估计精度。
Summary / 总结
To improve robotic assembly precision under noisy perception, this work introduces ContactFusion, a method that fuses visual point clouds with force-torque-based contact sensing to enhance surface mapping. It employs a rejection sampling approach to estimate contact locations on the end-effector from wrist sensor data, then integrates this contact information into a Stochastic Poisson Surface Map (SPSMap) using the SPSR algorithm. Experimental results in simulation validate the contact sensor’s accuracy, and in a peg-in-hole task, the fusion of contact data with visual input significantly improves the estimated pose of the target hole.
为提升在感知噪声下机器人装配的精度，本文提出ContactFusion方法，通过融合视觉点云与力觉传感器的局部接触信息来增强位姿估计。该方法采用基于拒绝采样的接触占据感知算法，从腕部力矩数据中推断末端执行器的接触位置，并将其与Stochastic Poisson Surface Map（SPSMap）融合，利用SPSR算法更新地图。仿真验证了接触传感器的准确性，而在销孔插入任务中，接触信息与视觉地图的融合显著提升了孔位姿估计精度，证明了其在高精度装配场景下的鲁棒性。
Harmonizing AI Safety Thresholds
Authors: Wilber Sean Anterola, Matthew Ball, Luis F. Lafuerza, Markov Grey
First: 2026-07-17T16:45:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:45:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier AI companies have published capability thresholds that differ substantially, making it difficult for third parties to verify whether a threshold has been crossed or to compare requirements across companies. Moreover, without common minimum thresholds, risk mitigation may be inconsistent, creating a potential race to the bottom in safety standards. We develop a methodology for deriving harmonized thresholds across three risk domains. For misuse risks (cyber and biological), we take expected harm as the key primitive and use an explicit risk-modeling approach that accounts for risk channels and model release conditions. For automated AI R&D, we base our proposed threshold on the observed rate of AI progress rather than expected harm. Our analysis expands upon prior work and highlights existing empirical gaps and limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协调人工智能安全阈值
前沿人工智能公司发布的功能阈值差异显著，使第三方难以验证是否已越过阈值或比较各公司要求。此外，缺乏共同的最低阈值可能导致风险缓解措施不一致，从而在安全标准上形成恶性竞争。我们开发了一种在三个风险领域内推导协调阈值的方法。对于误用风险（网络与生物），我们以预期伤害为关键基本要素，采用明确的风险建模方法，考虑风险渠道和模型发布条件。对于自动化人工智能研发，我们基于观察到的人工智能进步速率而非预期伤害设定阈值。我们的分析扩展了先前研究，指出了现有实证差距与局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized safety thresholds across frontier AI companies, which hinders third-party verification and risks inconsistent safety practices, this paper proposes a methodology to harmonize safety thresholds across three risk domains. For misuse risks like cyber and biological threats, it uses expected harm as the core metric and applies explicit risk modeling that incorporates risk channels and model release conditions. For automated AI R&D, it bases the threshold on observed AI progress rates rather than expected harm. The analysis reveals empirical gaps in current approaches and demonstrates how these harmonized frameworks can better align safety expectations across sectors.
鉴于前沿AI公司发布的安全阈值差异巨大，导致第三方难以验证或比较各公司标准，本文提出一种跨三大风险领域协调安全阈值的方法。针对误用风险（如网络与生物风险），以预期伤害为核心指标，采用显式风险建模方法，纳入风险路径与模型发布条件；针对自动化AI研发，阈值基于观察到的AI进展速率而非预期伤害。分析指出前人研究中的实证空白与局限，展示了该方法可提供更可比且具操作性的安全基准。
ReLink: Computational Circular Design of Planar Linkage Mechanisms Using Available Standard Parts
Authors: Maxime Escande, Kristina Shea
First: 2025-06-24T14:22:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:43:57+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 18 figures, Submitted Python code available at https://github.com/ETHZ-EDAC/ReLink and archived on Zenodo at DOI:10.5281/ZENODO.21413849
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Circular Economy framework emphasizes sustainability by reducing resource consumption and waste through the reuse of components and materials. This paper presents ReLink, a computational framework for the circular design of planar linkage mechanisms using available standard parts. Unlike most mechanism design methods, which assume the ability to create custom parts and infinite part availability, ReLink prioritizes the reuse of discrete, standardized components, thus minimizing the need for new parts. The framework consists of two main components: design generation, where a generative design algorithm generates mechanisms from an inventory of available parts, and inverse design, which uses optimization methods to identify designs that match a user-defined trajectory curve. The paper also examines the trade-offs between kinematic performance and CO2 footprint when incorporating new parts. Challenges such as the combinatorial nature of the design problem and the enforcement of valid solutions are addressed. By combining sustainability principles with kinematic synthesis, ReLink lays the groundwork for further research into computational circular design to support the development of systems that integrate reused components into mechanical products.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReLink：利用现有标准部件计算平面连杆机构的循环设计
循环经济框架强调通过重复使用组件和材料来减少资源消耗和废弃物，以实现可持续性。本文提出了ReLink，一个利用现有标准部件进行平面连杆机构循环设计的计算框架。与大多数机构设计方法不同，这些方法假设可以制造定制部件且部件无限供应，ReLink则优先重复使用离散化、标准化的部件，从而最大限度减少新部件的需求。该框架包含两个主要部分：设计生成，通过生成式设计算法从现有部件库存中生成机构；逆向设计，利用优化方法识别匹配用户定义轨迹曲线的设计。本文还探讨了在引入新部件时，运动学性能与二氧化碳足迹之间的权衡。文中还解决了设计问题的组合性质及有效解的约束等挑战。通过将可持续性原则与运动学综合相结合，ReLink为计算循环设计的进一步研究奠定了基础，以支持将再用部件整合到机械产品中的系统开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce waste and resource consumption in mechanical design, ReLink proposes a computational framework for designing planar linkage mechanisms using only available standard parts, avoiding custom fabrication. The method combines generative design for mechanism synthesis from part inventories and inverse design via optimization to match user-specified motion trajectories. Experimental results demonstrate that ReLink successfully generates functional mechanisms while evaluating trade-offs between kinematic performance and environmental impact, including CO2 emissions from new part usage. The framework addresses combinatorial complexity and solution validity through algorithmic constraints, offering a practical pathway toward circular mechanical design.
为减少机械设计中的资源消耗与废弃物，ReLink提出一种仅使用现有标准零件合成平面连杆机构的计算框架。该方法结合基于零件库的生成式设计与优化逆向设计，以匹配用户指定的运动轨迹。实验结果表明，该框架可有效生成功能机构，并量化了运动性能与新增零件碳足迹之间的权衡。同时，通过算法约束解决了组合爆炸与解有效性问题，为机械工程中的循环设计提供了基础方法。
The Honest Quorum Problem: Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance for Agentic Infrastructure
Authors: Jun He, Deying Yu
First: 2026-07-17T16:43:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:43:00+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State machine replication (SMR) and Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus guarantee agreement despite a bounded number of arbitrary, colluding faulty participants. However, these guarantees rely on participants outside this set correctly executing the protocol's transition semantics. Agentic validators expose a weaker boundary: an authenticated, responsive, non-equivocating, and protocol-compliant reasoning participant may still endorse a semantically invalid transition due to reasoning errors. We call this failure mode an epistemic fault, and the collective phenomenon the Honest Quorum Problem (where "honest" means protocol-compliant, not semantically correct). Such a quorum can satisfy ordinary checks while forming a certificate for an invalid transition. Thus, agreement alone does not guarantee semantic validity or execution safety. Furthermore, because agentic validators often share model weights, training distributions, prompts, or toolchains, they are highly susceptible to correlated epistemic faults. We define Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT), a fault-tolerance model for agentic infrastructure and post-deterministic distributed systems. EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：诚实法定数问题：面向代理基础设施的元认知拜占庭容错
状态机复制（SMR）和拜占庭容错（BFT）共识协议保证在有限数量的任意合谋故障参与者存在时仍能达成一致。然而，这些保证依赖于该集合外的参与者正确执行协议的转换语义。代理验证者则暴露了更弱的边界：一个经过认证、响应及时、无矛盾且协议合规的推理参与者，仍可能因推理错误而支持语义无效的转换。 我们称此失败模式为元认知故障，其集体现象即为“诚实法定数问题”（其中“诚实”指协议合规，而非语义正确）。此类法定数可满足常规检查，同时为无效转换生成证书。因此，一致性本身并不能保证语义有效性或执行安全性。此外，由于代理验证者常共享模型权重、训练分布、提示或工具链，它们极易受到相关元认知故障的影响。 我们定义了元认知拜占庭容错（EBFT），作为代理基础设施及后确定性分布式系统的容错模型。EBFT在传统拜占庭故障边界基础上，增加两个独立的置信度索引量：$e_δ$ 限制拜占庭集合外的语义无效支持，$u_ε$ 限制导致活性下降的不可用验证者支持。这些量独立表征语义安全风险与活性退化。我们推导了语义有效性、共识一致性、活性及可行阈值选择的法定数阈值条件，并概述了估算这些预算的校准方法。我们表明，仅当新增代理显著降低无效支持的上尾集中度或不可用支持时，增加名义上不同的代理才可提升容错能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that protocol-compliant but semantically flawed agents can form quorums endorsing invalid transitions, the paper introduces Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT) to address epistemic faults in agentic infrastructure. EBFT extends traditional BFT by introducing two confidence-indexed bounds: $e_δ$ for coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set and $u_ε$ for unusable validator support that impairs liveness. The authors derive quorum thresholds ensuring semantic validity, agreement, and liveness, and propose a calibration method to estimate these bounds. Experiments show that adding diverse agents improves fault tolerance only when they measurably reduce the concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.
为解决协议合规但语义错误的代理节点可能形成非法共识的问题，本文提出“认知拜占庭容错”（EBFT）模型，用于代理基础设施中的语义安全保障。EBFT在传统拜占庭容错基础上引入两个置信度指标：$e_δ$ 用于限制诚实节点对非法状态转换的协同支持，$u_ε$ 用于限制影响系统活性的不可用支持。作者推导了确保语义正确性、共识达成与系统活性的阈值条件，并提出校准方法估算这些指标。实验表明，仅当新增代理显著降低非法支持或不可用支持的尾部集中度时，多样性才真正提升容错能力。
Audio-Visual Flamingo: Open Audio-Visual Intelligence for Long and Complex Videos
Authors: Sreyan Ghosh, Arushi Goel, Kaousheik Jayakumar, Lasha Koroshinadze, Nishit Anand, Siddharth Gururani, Hanrong Ye, Pritam Biswas, Yuanhang Su, Ehsan Hosseini-Asl, Sang-gil Lee, Zhifeng Kong, Jaehyeon Kim, Sungwon Kim, S Sakshi, Ramani Duraiswami, Dinesh Manocha, Andrew Tao, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Ming-Yu Liu, Wei Ping
First: 2026-07-17T16:41:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:41:15+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://avflamingo.pages.dev/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Audio-Visual Flamingo (AV-Flamingo), a fully open state-of-the-art audio-visual large language model (AV-LLM) for joint understanding and reasoning over audio, images, and long-form videos. Unlike prior AV-LLMs that primarily focus on short clips, AV-Flamingo is designed for understanding and reasoning over long and complex real-world (audio-visual) videos. To support this, we make three key contributions: (i) Audio-Visual-Skills, a large-scale collection of real-world videos with ~7M caption and question-answer training instances designed to emphasize temporal, compositional, and cross-modal audio-visual reasoning; (ii) a novel three-stage curriculum that progressively trains the model from short-range perception to long-horizon multi-event reasoning; and (iii) Temporal Audio-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought, a reasoning framework that explicitly grounds intermediate reasoning steps to timestamps in long audio-visual streams, improving temporal alignment and interpretability. Extensive experiments across 15+ audio-visual, omni-modal, audio, and vision benchmarks show that AV-Flamingo outperforms similarly sized open models by clear margins and remains highly competitive with, and in some cases surpasses, much larger open-weight and closed models, particularly on long and complex real-world audio-visual understanding and reasoning tasks. Beyond benchmark performance, AV-Flamingo exhibits strong real-world utility and transfers well to unseen tasks, highlighting its robustness and generalization ability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：音频视觉火烈鸟：面向长视频与复杂场景的开放音频视觉智能
我们提出了音频视觉火烈鸟（AV-Flamingo），这是一个完全开放的、最先进的音频视觉大语言模型（AV-LLM），用于联合理解与推理音频、图像和长视频。与以往主要聚焦于短片段的AV-LLM不同，AV-Flamingo专为理解和推理长且复杂的现实世界（音频视觉）视频而设计。为此，我们做出三项关键贡献：(i) 音频视觉技能（Audio-Visual-Skills），一个包含约700万条图文问答训练样本的大型真实世界视频数据集，旨在强调时序、组合及跨模态音频视觉推理；(ii) 一种新颖的三阶段课程训练方法，逐步从短程感知训练到长程多事件推理；(iii) 时序音频视觉交错思维链（Temporal Audio-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought），一种将中间推理步骤显式锚定到长音频视频流中的时间戳的推理框架，提升时序对齐与可解释性。在15+个音频视觉、全模态、音频和视觉基准测试中，大量实验证明AV-Flamingo在同等规模的开放模型中表现显著领先，并在部分任务上超越了更大规模的开放权重和闭源模型，尤其在长且复杂的现实世界音频视觉理解和推理任务中表现突出。除基准性能外，AV-Flamingo展现出强大的现实世界实用性，并能良好迁移到未见任务，凸显其鲁棒性与泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for models capable of understanding and reasoning over long, complex real-world audio-visual videos, the authors introduce Audio-Visual Flamingo (AV-Flamingo), an open-source AV-LLM. The method leverages Audio-Visual-Skills—a dataset of 7M real-world video QA pairs—to train a three-stage curriculum that scales from short-range perception to long-horizon reasoning, and employs Temporal Audio-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought to ground reasoning steps to timestamps. Experimental results across 15+ benchmarks show AV-Flamingo significantly outperforms similarly sized open models and competes with or surpasses larger models, especially on long-form tasks, while demonstrating strong generalization and real-world utility.
为应对长时复杂真实世界音视频理解的需求，作者提出Audio-Visual Flamingo（AV-Flamingo），一种面向联合多模态理解的开放型音视频大语言模型。该方法构建了包含700万条真实视频问答对的Audio-Visual-Skills数据集，并采用三阶段课程训练策略，从短时感知逐步过渡到长时多事件推理，同时引入时间交错思维链框架，将推理步骤锚定至视频时间戳以提升对齐性。实验表明，AV-Flamingo在15+基准测试中显著优于同等规模的开源模型，部分任务超越更大模型，且在未见任务中展现出强大泛化能力与实用价值。
Attention-Guided Saliency Maps for Interpreting Visualization Literacy in VLMs
Authors: Maeve Hutchinson, Abderrahmane Wassim Mehdaoui, Pranava Madhyastha
First: 2026-07-17T16:40:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:40:23+00:00
Comments: To be presented at IEEE VIS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how vision-language models (VLMs) interpret data visualizations remains an open problem, and is increasingly important as these models are used for analytical tasks where reliable reasoning is essential. We introduce a lightweight, diagnostic saliency map method tailored for text generation over images using transformer models, the current state-of-the-art models in visualization interpretation. Our approach aggregates the language model's attention over the visual tokens across all heads and layers, then maps this attention back onto the vision encoder's patch grid to localise it over the image, producing a direct correspondence between each generated answer token and the image regions it attended to. This yields fast, gradient-free saliency maps that expose how VLMs allocate focus across visual elements during answer generation, enabling inspection of whether model attention aligns with semantically relevant components. We evaluate our approach using a deletion metric which validates the causal faithfulness of our saliency maps to the model's behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于注意力引导的显著性图谱用于解读视觉语言模型中的可视化素养
理解视觉语言模型（VLMs）如何解释数据可视化仍是一个开放性问题，且随着这些模型在需要可靠推理的分析任务中日益广泛应用，其重要性愈发凸显。我们提出了一种轻量级、诊断性的显著性图谱方法，专为基于图像的文本生成任务设计，适用于当前最先进的视觉解释Transformer模型。该方法通过聚合语言模型在所有注意力头和层中对视觉标记的注意力，再将注意力映射回视觉编码器的图像块网格，从而定位图像中的关注区域，实现每个生成答案标记与所关注图像区域之间的直接对应。这生成了快速、无需梯度的显著性图谱，揭示了VLMs在生成答案过程中如何分配注意力于视觉元素，从而可检查模型注意力是否与语义相关组件对齐。我们通过删除指标评估该方法，验证其显著性图谱对模型行为的因果忠实性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how vision-language models interpret visualizations for analytical tasks, this work proposes a lightweight, gradient-free saliency mapping method that traces attention from transformer-based VLMs back to image regions. By aggregating attention across all layers and heads over visual tokens, the method maps attention heatmaps onto the image’s patch grid, linking each generated text token to the visual areas it attended to. Experimental results using a deletion metric show that these saliency maps accurately reflect the model’s causal reasoning, revealing whether attention aligns with semantically relevant visual components and enabling diagnostic inspection of model behavior.
为理解视觉语言模型如何在分析任务中解读可视化内容，本文提出一种轻量级、无需梯度的显著性图方法，通过聚合视觉标记在所有层和头上的注意力，再映射回图像块网格以定位每个生成答案词对应的图像区域。该方法可快速揭示模型在生成文本时对视觉元素的关注分配情况，判断其注意力是否与语义相关区域对齐。实验采用删除指标验证，结果表明该显著性图对模型行为具有因果忠实性，有效用于诊断VLM的可视化理解能力。
Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuation via Co-Contraction: A Structural Analogy with Variable Stiffness Actuation
Authors: Antonio Franchi
First: 2026-05-08T06:02:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:37:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work identifies a passive aerodynamic damping effect induced by co-contraction in antagonistic redundant propulsion. Complementing prior work on aerodynamic promptness, which addressed active wrench-rate authority along constant-wrench fibers, we study the passive side: the local derivative of aerodynamic force with respect to air-relative velocity at a trim. This derivative defines an incremental aerodynamic damping coefficient. We prove that it increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under a mild aerodynamic hardening condition, and derive this property from a first-order Blade Element Theory model exposing the relevant speed-inflow coupling. The resulting mechanism, Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuation (VADA), is formulated as an antagonistic aerodynamic actuation module and allocation principle, structurally analogous to variable-stiffness actuation at the level of fiber motions and incremental impedance modulation. An impedance-form interpretation clarifies common- and differential-mode roles, while a propeller-data-based assessment using the UIUC Propeller Database shows that the identified damping has practical small-UAV magnitude, is comparable to ordinary low-speed body-drag damping, and depends strongly on low-advance-ratio thrust sensitivity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过协同收缩实现可变气动阻尼作动：与可变刚度作动的结构类比
本研究识别了在拮抗冗余推进系统中由协同收缩引起的被动气动阻尼效应。在先前关于气动响应性的研究基础上（该研究关注沿恒力纤维的主动力矩率控制），我们研究其被动方面：即在平衡状态下的气动力对气相对速度的局部导数，该导数定义了增量气动阻尼系数。我们证明，在轻微气动硬化条件下，该系数沿恒力纤维单调递增，并从一阶叶片元素理论模型中推导出该性质，该模型揭示了相关速度-流入耦合关系。由此产生的机制——可变气动阻尼作动（VADA）——被表述为一种拮抗式气动作动模块及其分配原则，其结构与可变刚度作动在纤维运动和增量阻抗调制层面具有类比性。阻抗形式的解释阐明了公共模式与差分模式的作用，而基于UIUC螺旋桨数据库的评估表明，所识别的阻尼具有实用的小型无人机量级，与普通低速机体阻力阻尼相当，并强烈依赖于低前进比推力敏感性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance control authority in small UAVs without active actuators, this study explores a passive aerodynamic damping effect generated by co-contraction in antagonistic propulsion systems. Using a first-order Blade Element Theory model, it derives how the local derivative of aerodynamic force with respect to air-relative velocity defines an incremental damping coefficient that increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under mild aerodynamic hardening. The resulting Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuation (VADA) framework is structurally analogous to variable-stiffness actuation, enabling impedance modulation via fiber motion. Experimental evaluation using the UIUC Propeller Database confirms that this passive damping is practically significant for small UAVs, comparable to body drag at low speeds, and strongly dependent on thrust sensitivity at low advance ratios.
为增强飞行器推进系统中的被动控制能力，本研究探讨了拮抗冗余推进中协同收缩如何诱导可变气动阻尼效应。基于一阶 Blade Element 理论模型，作者推导出沿恒力纤维单调递增的增量气动阻尼系数，该特性在轻度气动硬化条件下成立。该机制命名为可变气动阻尼作动（VADA），其结构类比于变刚度作动，以拮抗气动作动模块形式构建。基于 UIUC 风洞数据库的实验验证表明，所识别的阻尼效应在小型无人机上具有实际意义，其量级与低速机身阻力相当，且对低前进比下的推力敏感性高度依赖。
Internal Pluralism and the Limits of Pairwise Comparisons
Authors: Bailey Flanigan, Michelle Si
First: 2026-07-02T18:08:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:36:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Local pairwise comparisons are a standard tool for learning how people want decision rules to work, e.g., in participatory design or alignment. However, their use builds in two strong assumptions: that local comparisons are sufficient evidence about how a person wants an automated decision rule to behave, and that people can always answer those comparisons decisively. We investigate how these assumptions may be compromised under internal pluralism: the idea that an individual evaluates decision rules according to multiple authoritative priorities about how the rule should behave. We provide a formal model of such pluralistic preferences over decision rules, which then lets us identify two distinct failures of forced local pairwise comparison data. First, priorities such as proportionality, egalitarianism, and equal treatment are inherently global: what they imply in one case can depend on what happens elsewhere, so local comparisons may fail to capture them. Second, even when priorities are representable locally, tension between strongly-held priorities can generate internal conflict, producing potentially costly behavioral distortions when comparisons are forced. We then use our model to investigate the alternative -- allowing people to report indecision -- and our findings suggest that doing so can considerably reduce the number of queries needed to learn preferences accurately. We conclude by describing how our model points toward preference-learning methods that elicit these priorities directly, yielding more faithful and interpretable accounts of what people value.
中文标题/摘要
标题：内部多元主义与成对比较的局限性
局部成对比较是学习人们希望决策规则如何运作的标准工具，例如在参与式设计或对齐中。然而，其使用隐含两个强假设：局部比较足以反映个人对自动化决策规则的期望，且人们总能明确回答这些比较。我们探讨在内部多元主义下这些假设可能被削弱的情况：即个体根据多个权威优先级评估决策规则。我们构建了此类多元偏好形式化模型，从而识别出强制局部成对比较数据的两种失效：首先，比例性、平等主义和同等对待等优先级本质上是全局性的：它们在某一情境下的含义可能依赖于其他情境，因此局部比较可能无法捕捉它们；其次，即使优先级可局部表示，强烈持有的优先级之间可能产生内部冲突，当强制比较时可能导致成本高昂的行为扭曲。接着，我们利用该模型探讨替代方案——允许人们报告犹豫不决——并发现这样做可显著减少学习偏好所需的查询数量。最后，我们指出该模型如何引导偏好学习方法直接提取这些优先级，从而提供更忠实、可解释的关于人们价值观的描述。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitations of using local pairwise comparisons to infer people’s preferences for automated decision rules, particularly under internal pluralism — the idea that individuals hold multiple conflicting priorities. The authors develop a formal model of pluralistic preferences that reveals two key failures: first, global priorities like proportionality or egalitarianism cannot be captured by local comparisons because their implications depend on context; second, internal conflicts between strong priorities can cause behavioral distortions when forced to choose. Experiments show that allowing participants to report indecision significantly reduces the number of queries needed to accurately learn their preferences, suggesting that accommodating uncertainty improves efficiency and fidelity in preference elicitation.
该文探讨了使用局部成对比较推断人们对于自动化决策规则偏好的局限性，其动机源于对个体持有单一统一优先级的假设。作者提出了内部多元主义的正式模型——即个体依据多个相互竞争的优先级评估规则——并指出此类偏好常无法通过局部比较捕捉，因为如比例性或平等主义等全局原则依赖于上下文。研究进一步表明，强制成对比较在优先级冲突时会导致内部矛盾，引发行为扭曲。实验结果表明，允许参与者报告不确定或不决可显著减少获取准确偏好所需的查询次数，暗示偏好采集方法应支持多元、非二元响应，以更真实反映个体价值。
Understanding Reasoning from Pretraining to Post-Training
Authors: Jingyan Shen, Ang Li, Salman Rahman, Yifan Sun, Micah Goldblum, Matus Telgarsky, Pavel Izmailov
First: 2026-07-17T16:31:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:31:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to improving large language models (LLMs) on complex reasoning tasks, yet RL post-training is largely studied in isolation from the pretraining that precedes it. As a result, two basic questions remain open: (1) how do pretraining choices (model size, data) shape the returns to RL compute, and (2) what does RL actually do to the model? These questions are difficult to study in the standard LLM setting: pretraining corpora are vast and uncontrolled, making it hard to attribute behaviors to pretraining versus RL, and systematic compute sweeps across both stages are prohibitively expensive. To address these challenges, we use chess as a controlled testbed for studying reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline. We follow the standard LLM training pipeline by pretraining language models from 5M to 1B parameters on human chess games, supervised fine-tuning on synthetic reasoning traces, and running RL on chess puzzles with verifiable rewards. Using this framework, we find that the post-RL performance at given RL compute level is well-predicted from the pretraining loss, and slope of the RL reward curves improves approximately linearly with the pretraining tokens. Beyond scaling, we find that RL does not simply sharpen the SFT policy: on easy puzzles it amplifies correct moves the SFT policy already preferred, while on hard puzzles it surfaces correct moves that were nearly absent under SFT. We further test whether our findings transfer beyond chess by training a 1B language model on math-domain text, where the same predictive pattern emerges: longer-pretrained checkpoints reach higher post-RL performance and improve faster under RL. In sum, we provide a quantitative account of the pretraining-to-RL interface and a controlled testbed for studying the science of reasoning across the full pretraining-to-post-training pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从预训练到后训练的推理理解
强化学习（RL）已成为提升大型语言模型（LLMs）在复杂推理任务上的核心方法，但RL后训练通常与之前的预训练脱节研究。因此，两个基本问题仍未解决：（1）预训练选择（模型规模、数据）如何影响RL计算的回报？（2）RL实际对模型做了什么？在标准LLM设置中，这些问题难以研究：预训练语料庞大且不可控，难以区分行为源于预训练还是RL；且在两个阶段进行系统性计算扫描成本过高。为应对这些挑战，我们以国际象棋为受控测试平台，研究从预训练到后训练的完整推理流程。我们遵循标准LLM训练流程：在人类国际象棋游戏中预训练5M至1B参数的语言模型，进行监督微调以处理合成推理轨迹，并在具有可验证奖励的国际象棋谜题上运行RL。通过该框架，我们发现：在给定RL计算水平下，后RL性能可由预训练损失良好预测；RL奖励曲线斜率随预训练token数近似线性提升。此外，我们发现RL并非简单地“锐化”SFT策略：在简单谜题中，RL放大了SFT策略已偏好的正确走法；而在困难谜题中，RL则揭示了SFT下几乎不存在的正确走法。我们进一步测试这些发现是否可迁移到其他领域，通过在数学文本上训练1B语言模型，发现相同预测模式重现：预训练更久的检查点在后RL中表现更高且提升更快。综上，我们提供了预训练到RL接口的量化分析，并构建了研究完整预训练到后训练推理科学的受控测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how pretraining shapes the effectiveness of reinforcement learning (RL) in large language models (LLMs), this study uses chess as a controlled environment to systematically analyze the pretraining-to-RL pipeline. Models ranging from 5M to 1B parameters were pretrained on human chess games, followed by supervised fine-tuning and RL on verifiable chess puzzles. Results show that post-RL performance at a given compute level is strongly predicted by pretraining loss, and RL reward curve slopes improve linearly with pretraining tokens. RL does not merely sharpen the supervised fine-tuning policy: on easy puzzles, it amplifies existing preferences, while on hard puzzles, it surfaces previously underrepresented correct moves. These findings transfer to a math-domain model, confirming that longer pretraining leads to higher post-RL performance and faster RL gains.
该研究探讨了预训练如何影响强化学习（RL）提升大语言模型推理能力的效果，采用国际象棋作为可控环境。作者在5M至1B参数规模下，用人类棋局数据预训练模型，随后进行监督微调和基于可验证奖励的RL训练。结果发现，在给定计算量下，RL后的表现与预训练损失高度相关，且RL奖励曲线的提升斜率随预训练token数线性增长。RL并非简单地强化监督微调策略：在简单题目中，它放大已有偏好；在困难题目中，它挖掘出微调阶段几乎缺失的正确策略。这些发现也适用于数学领域模型，表明预训练与RL之间的接口在不同领域具有一致性。
Bellman-sufficient Information Complexity
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:30:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop Bellman-sufficient information complexity, a representation-level framework for the information-theoretic minimax analysis of sequential decision making. The theory covers interactive environments unfolding over long streams of experience and benchmarks all nonanticipating algorithms. A Bellman-sufficient state closes the controlled recursion, while an index $Y=χ(Ω)$ identifies the decision-relevant information. Upper bounds arise from a log-penalized Bellman program, and lower bounds from a Bellman-Fano comparison along a reference trajectory. When the two sides exhibit matching information growth at a common localization scale, they yield an information-risk sandwich. Within this framework, UCB, E2D, and AMS/EBO arise through calibration, one-step offsets, and robust belief optimization, respectively. As a major application, we show that maximal-information calibration can make GP-UCB polynomially suboptimal in minimax regret. For every $0<α<1/4$, there exists a single bounded continuous kernel whose minimax regret is $Θ(T^{1-α})$ along an infinite sequence of horizons. On the same problem, a finite-marginal action-index AIR Bellman policy attains this order, whereas the specified anytime GP-UCB rule incurs linear regret. This separates realized information acquisition from the cost of uniform optimism and shows why localization can be essential within the Bellman recursion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贝尔曼充分信息复杂度
我们提出了贝尔曼充分信息复杂度，这是一种面向序列决策的、基于表示层面的信息论最小最大分析框架。该理论涵盖在长经验流中展开的交互式环境，并对所有非前瞻算法进行基准比较。贝尔曼充分状态关闭了受控递归，而索引 $Y=χ(Ω)$ 标识决策相关的信息。上界来自带对数惩罚的贝尔曼规划，下界则来自沿参考轨迹的贝尔曼-费诺比较。当两侧在共同局部化尺度上表现出匹配的信息增长时，二者形成信息-风险夹层。在此框架下，UCB、E2D 和 AMS/EBO 分别通过校准、一步偏移和鲁棒信念优化产生。 作为主要应用，我们证明最大信息校准可使 GP-UCB 在最小最大遗憾中呈多项式次优。对任意 $0<α<1/4$，存在一个单个有界连续核，其在无限时域序列上的最小最大遗憾为 $Θ(T^{1-α})$。在同一问题上，有限边际动作索引的 AIR 贝尔曼策略可达到此阶，而指定的任意时间 GP-UCB 规则则产生线性遗憾。这区分了实际信息获取与均匀乐观成本，并揭示了局部化在贝尔曼递归中的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to analyze information efficiency in sequential decision making under minimax regret, the paper introduces Bellman-sufficient information complexity, a framework that quantifies information-theoretic limits via a representation-level Bellman recursion. It defines a Bellman-sufficient state to close the recursion and an index Y=χ(Ω) to capture decision-relevant information, deriving upper and lower bounds through a log-penalized Bellman program and Bellman-Fano comparison. When these bounds align in information growth at a common localization scale, they form an information-risk sandwich. The framework recovers UCB, E2D, and AMS/EBO via calibration, one-step offsets, and robust belief optimization. In a key application, maximal-information calibration is shown to make GP-UCB polynomially suboptimal, with a bounded kernel achieving Θ(T^{1−α}) regret for any 0<α<1/4 over infinite horizons, while a finite-marginal AIR Bellman policy matches this rate, contrasting sharply with GP-UCB’s linear regret and highlighting the necessity of localization.
为分析信息受限下的序列决策问题，本文提出贝尔曼充分信息复杂度框架，通过表示层贝尔曼递归量化信息理论最小最大性能。该框架定义贝尔曼充分状态以闭合递归，并通过 Y=χ(Ω) 指标捕捉决策相关的信息，上界由带对数惩罚的贝尔曼程序导出，下界由贝尔曼-费诺比较沿参考轨迹得出。当上下界在相同局部尺度下信息增长匹配时，形成信息-风险夹层，且通过校准、单步偏移和鲁棒信念优化分别恢复UCB、E2D和AMS/EBO。实验表明，最大信息校准可使GP-UCB在最小最大遗憾中呈多项式次优，存在一个有界连续核在无限时域序列中实现Θ(T^{1−α})遗憾（0<α<1/4），而有限边际行动索引的AIR贝尔曼策略可达到该阶，而指定的任意时间GP-UCB策略则产生线性遗憾，凸显贝尔曼递归中局部化的重要性。
Inelastic Constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: A generalized framework for automated discovery of interpretable inelastic material models
Authors: Chenyi Ji, Kian P. Abdolazizi, Hagen Holthusen, Christian J. Cyron, Kevin Linka
First: 2026-02-19T15:51:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:28:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A key problem of solid mechanics is the identification of the constitutive law of a material, that is, the relation between strain history and stress. Machine learning has lead to considerable advances in this field lately. Here we introduce inelastic Constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (iCKANs). This novel artificial neural network architecture can discover in an automated manner symbolic constitutive laws describing both the elastic and inelastic behavior of materials. That is, it can translate data from material testing into corresponding elastic and inelastic potential functions in closed mathematical form. We demonstrate the advantages of iCKANs using both synthetic data and experimental data of the viscoelastic polymer materials VHB 4910 and VHB 4905. The results demonstrate that iCKANs accurately capture complex viscoelastic behavior while preserving physical interpretability. It is a particular strength of iCKANs that they can process not only mechanical data but also arbitrary additional information available about a material (e.g., about temperature-dependent behavior). This makes iCKANs a powerful tool to discover in the future also how specific processing or service conditions affect the properties of materials.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非弹性本构柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络：一种用于自动发现可解释非弹性材料模型的通用框架
固体力学中的一个关键问题是识别材料的本构关系，即应变历史与应力之间的关系。近年来，机器学习在该领域取得了显著进展。本文引入了非弹性本构柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（iCKANs），这是一种新颖的人工神经网络架构，能够自动发现描述材料弹性和非弹性行为的符号本构定律。也就是说，它能将材料测试数据转化为闭式数学形式的弹性与非弹性势函数。我们通过合成数据和粘弹性聚合物材料VHB 4910与VHB 4905的实验数据，展示了iCKANs的优势。结果表明，iCKANs能准确捕捉复杂的粘弹性行为，同时保持物理可解释性。iCKANs的一个特别优势是，它们不仅能处理机械数据，还能处理关于材料的任意附加信息（如温度依赖行为）。这使得iCKANs成为未来发现特定加工或服役条件如何影响材料性能的强大工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automatically derive interpretable constitutive laws from material data, this work introduces inelastic Constitutive Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (iCKANs), a novel neural architecture designed to discover symbolic mathematical expressions for both elastic and inelastic material behavior. The method leverages symbolic regression within a neural framework to translate experimental or synthetic strain-stress data into closed-form potential functions that capture material response. Experiments on synthetic datasets and real viscoelastic polymers (VHB 4910 and VHB 4905) show that iCKANs accurately model complex viscoelastic behavior while maintaining physical interpretability. A key advantage is the ability to incorporate additional material metadata, such as temperature dependence, enabling more comprehensive modeling of real-world material behavior under varied conditions.
为自动从材料数据中推导出可解释的本构关系，本文提出了一种名为iCKANs的新型神经网络架构，用于发现描述材料弹性与非弹性行为的符号化数学表达式。该方法通过神经网络框架内的符号回归，将实验或合成的应变-应力数据转化为闭式势函数，以捕捉材料响应。在合成数据和真实黏弹性聚合物VHB 4910与VHB 4905的实验中，iCKANs能准确建模复杂黏弹性行为并保持物理可解释性。其重要优势在于可整合材料的附加信息（如温度依赖性），从而更全面地模拟材料在不同条件下的性能变化。
Let the Body Follow: Coupled Egocentric Control for Whole-Body Robot Teleoperation
Authors: Tsung-Chi Lin, Yichen Xie, Chien-Ming Huang
First: 2026-07-17T16:26:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:26:45+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 11 figures, 1 table; submitted to the 2026 IEEE-RAS International Conference on Humanoid Robots (Humanoids 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whole-body teleoperation requires users to coordinate perception, manipulation, posture, and mobility across multiple robot components. This coordination is difficult because users must simultaneously control the robot's head, arms, torso, and base while maintaining task awareness and avoiding kinematic or environmental constraints. In this paper, we propose coupled egocentric control, a body-following teleoperation approach in which the robot's torso and base automatically respond to the operator's head and arm motions. Rather than requiring explicit touchpad commands for every torso or base adjustment, the system lets users focus on gaze and hand control: head pitch adjusts torso height, head yaw drives base rotation, end-effector height adjusts torso motion, and end-effector workspace boundaries trigger base translation. We evaluate this approach in a user study on whole-body teleoperation of a TIAGo mobile manipulator for home-care-inspired tasks. Compared with a baseline hybrid interface, coupled egocentric control improves object manipulation efficiency, reduces button-based control effort and arm singularities, lowers mental demand and overall workload, and increases ease of use, ease of learning, confidence, and user preference for torso and base control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：让身体跟随：面向全身机器人遥操作的耦合自中心控制
全身遥操作要求用户协调感知、操作、姿态和移动等多个机器人部件。这种协调困难，因为用户需同时控制机器人头部、手臂、躯干和基座，同时保持任务意识并避免运动学或环境约束。本文提出耦合自中心控制方法，该方法使机器人躯干和基座自动响应操作者的头部和手臂运动。系统无需为每个躯干或基座调整输入显式触控指令，而是让用户专注于视线和手部控制：头部俯仰调整躯干高度，头部偏航驱动基座旋转，末端执行器高度调节躯干运动，末端执行器工作空间边界触发基座平移。我们在TIAGo移动操作机器人上针对家庭护理启发式任务开展用户研究，评估该方法。与基线混合界面相比，耦合自中心控制提升了物体操作效率，减少了基于按钮的控制负担和手臂奇异点，降低了认知负荷和整体工作量，同时提高了易用性、易学性、信心和用户对躯干与基座控制的偏好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of coordinating multiple robot components during whole-body teleoperation, this paper introduces coupled egocentric control, a method that automatically links the robot’s torso and base movements to the operator’s head and arm motions, reducing the need for explicit control inputs. In a user study with a TIAGo mobile manipulator performing home-care tasks, the system enabled users to focus on gaze and hand control, with head pitch adjusting torso height, head yaw driving base rotation, end-effector height influencing torso motion, and workspace boundaries triggering base translation. Compared to a hybrid interface baseline, the proposed method significantly improved manipulation efficiency, reduced control effort and arm singularities, lowered mental workload, and increased user confidence and preference for torso and base control.
为解决全身体控机器人遥操作中多部件协调困难的问题，本文提出耦合自中心控制方法，该方法自动根据操作者的头部和手臂动作调整机器人躯干与底盘。无需手动输入，系统将头部俯仰对应躯干高度、头部偏转对应底盘旋转、末端执行器高度对应躯干运动、工作空间边界触发底盘平移。在使用TIAGo移动机械臂执行家庭护理任务的用户实验中，该方法相比混合界面显著提升操作效率、减少按键操作与机械臂奇异点、降低认知负荷，并提高用户对躯干与底盘控制的偏好与信心。
How Do VLMs Fail? Vision-Operation Misalignment in Compositional VQA
Authors: Navya Gupta, Bingjie Xu, Avinash Anand, Timothy Liu, Zhengchen Zhang
First: 2026-07-17T16:25:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:25:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACM Multimedia 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Compositional visual question answering requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to execute multiple reasoning operations like object selection, spatial relation resolution, and attribute verification. Despite strong aggregate performance, the mechanistic basis of VLM failures on this task remains underexplored. To address this gap, we analyze vision-operation misalignment in VLMs by examining how failures relate to specific reasoning operations and the internal computational pathways through which they arise and propagate. We introduce an Operation-centric mechanistic framework that decomposes VLM failures by both the reasoning operation where they originate and the internal computational pathway through which they propagate. Our analysis reveals four mechanistically distinct failure modes: grounding failure, reasoning failure, attribute extraction failure, and language prior dominance failure. Each characterized by a unique relationship between visual grounding strength and answer correctness. Through three complementary causal interventions applied across all transformer layers, we further demonstrate a pathway dissociation: grounding failures route exclusively through the feedforward network, reasoning failures route through late-layer attention, and attribute extraction failures localize to the answer-position feedforward computation. This dissociation demonstrates that different failure types require fundamentally different corrective strategies, providing a principled foundation for targeted improvements to VLM reliability in multimedia reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-操作对齐偏差如何导致视觉语言模型在组合式视觉问答中失败？
组合式视觉问答要求视觉-语言模型（VLMs）执行多种推理操作，如物体选择、空间关系解析和属性验证。尽管整体性能表现强劲，但VLMs在此任务中的失败机制仍缺乏深入研究。为填补这一空白，我们通过分析失败与特定推理操作及内部计算路径之间的关联，探讨视觉-操作对齐偏差。我们提出一种以操作为中心的机制框架，将VLMs的失败按其起源的推理操作和传播的内部计算路径进行分解。分析揭示四种机制上不同的失败模式：定位失败、推理失败、属性提取失败和语言先验主导失败，每种模式均具有独特的视觉定位强度与答案正确性之间的关系。通过在所有Transformer层中应用三种互补因果干预，我们进一步证明了失败路径的解耦：定位失败仅通过前馈网络传播，推理失败通过深层注意力传播，属性提取失败则局限于答案位置的前馈计算。该解耦表明不同失败类型需要根本不同的纠正策略，为提升VLMs在多媒体推理中的可靠性提供了理论基础。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why Vision-Language Models (VLMs) fail on compositional visual question answering tasks, where they must perform multiple reasoning operations such as object selection and spatial relation resolution. The authors introduce an operation-centric framework that decomposes failures by both the specific reasoning operation causing the error and the internal computational pathway through which it propagates. Their analysis identifies four distinct failure modes—grounding, reasoning, attribute extraction, and language prior dominance—each with unique patterns linking visual grounding strength to answer correctness. Through causal interventions across transformer layers, they demonstrate that grounding failures occur in feedforward networks, reasoning failures involve late-layer attention, and attribute extraction failures localize to answer-position feedforward computations, revealing that different failure types require distinct corrective strategies.
该研究探讨了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在组合式视觉问答任务中的失败原因，该任务要求模型执行如物体选择和空间关系解析等多重推理操作。作者提出一种以操作为中心的框架，将失败按其起源的推理操作和内部传播路径进行分解。分析发现四种机制上不同的失败模式——定位失败、推理失败、属性提取失败和语言先验主导失败——各自具有独特的视觉定位强度与答案正确性关系。通过在Transformer各层实施因果干预，研究发现定位失败仅通过前馈网络传播，推理失败通过深层注意力机制，属性提取失败则局限于答案位置的前馈计算，表明不同失败类型需采取截然不同的修正策略。
DADiff: Diffusion-Driven Cross-Domain Policy Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Hanyang Chen, Anirudh Satheesh, Longchao Da, Hua Wei
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-17T16:20:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:20:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transferring policies across domains poses a vital challenge in reinforcement learning, due to the dynamics mismatch between the source and target domains. In this paper, we consider the setting of online dynamics adaptation, where policies are trained in the source domain with sufficient data, while only limited interactions with the target domain are allowed. There are a few existing works that address the dynamics mismatch by employing domain classifiers, value-guided data filtering, or representation learning. Instead, we study the domain adaptation problem from a generative modeling perspective. Specifically, we introduce DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that leverages the discrepancy between source and target domain generative trajectories in the generation process of the next state to estimate the dynamics mismatch. Both reward modification and data selection variants are developed to adapt the policy to the target domain. We also provide a theoretical analysis to show that the performance difference of a given policy between the two domains is bounded by the generative trajectory deviation. More discussions on the applicability of the variants and the connection between our theoretical analysis and the prior work are further provided. We conduct extensive experiments in environments with various shifts to validate the effectiveness of our method. The results demonstrate that our method provides superior performance compared to existing approaches, effectively addressing the dynamics mismatch. We provide the code of our method at https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release
中文标题/摘要
标题：DADiff：基于扩散模型的跨域策略自适应强化学习方法
在强化学习中，跨域策略迁移面临关键挑战，源于源域与目标域之间的动态不匹配。本文研究在线动态自适应场景，即策略在源域中使用充足数据训练，而在目标域仅允许有限交互。现有方法多通过域分类器、价值引导的数据过滤或表征学习解决动态不匹配问题。本文从生成建模视角研究域自适应问题，提出DADiff框架，利用源域与目标域在生成下一状态轨迹时的差异来估计动态不匹配。我们开发了奖励修改与数据选择两种变体以适应目标域，并提供理论分析表明，给定策略在两域间的性能差异可被生成轨迹偏差所界定。进一步讨论了变体适用性及理论分析与先前工作的关联。我们在多种域偏移环境中进行大量实验，结果表明本方法优于现有方法，有效缓解动态不匹配问题。代码见https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of transferring reinforcement learning policies across domains with mismatched dynamics, this paper proposes DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that models domain adaptation through generative trajectory discrepancies. Instead of relying on domain classifiers or value-guided filtering, DADiff estimates dynamics mismatch by comparing source and target domain state transitions during diffusion-based generation. Two variants—reward modification and data selection—are introduced to adapt policies to the target domain, supported by theoretical bounds linking policy performance to trajectory deviation. Experiments across diverse domain shifts confirm DADiff outperforms existing methods, demonstrating robust adaptation under limited target interactions.
为解决强化学习中跨域策略迁移因动态不匹配导致的性能下降问题，本文提出DADiff框架，通过扩散模型建模源域与目标域下一状态生成轨迹的差异来量化动态不匹配。不同于现有方法依赖分类器或价值引导过滤，DADiff从生成建模视角出发，设计奖励修正与数据选择两种变体以适应目标域，理论分析表明策略性能差异可被轨迹偏差所界定。在多种域偏移环境中的实验验证了该方法在仅有限目标交互下优于现有方法的适应能力。
Neural spectroscopy of AlphaFold2 reveals encoded protein conformational landscapes
Authors: Kaustav Mehta
First: 2026-07-17T16:18:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:18:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters, shaped by the evolutionary record of protein structure encoded in the Protein Data Bank and in sequence alignments, are conventionally treated only as machinery for converting sequence to structure. We propose they are also a scientific object that can be analyzed directly: a learned encoding of protein conformational organization that can be probed and characterized. By smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with a Gaussian convolution and scaling the result, we show that the trained model produces physically structured conformational landscapes. Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in the order established by decades of folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered under perturbation. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where the training signal has determined the representation and where it has not. Matched-power noise controls confirm that random corruption of equal magnitude produces debris, not conformations. The model learned to predict static structures; the conformational organization visible under perturbation was not an explicit training target, suggesting it emerged as a byproduct of that objective. AlphaFold2's weights appear to encode structural constraints, shaped by evolutionary and structural training data, that extend beyond what unperturbed inference reveals. We call the approach of reading them neural spectroscopy, and Scaled Gaussian Convolution one such protocol.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AlphaFold2的神经谱学揭示了编码的蛋白质构象景观
AlphaFold2的9300万参数，由蛋白质结构的进化记录（来自蛋白质数据库和序列比对）塑造，传统上仅被视为将序列转化为结构的工具。我们提出，这些参数也可作为可直接分析的科学对象：一种学习到的蛋白质构象组织编码，可被探测和表征。通过用高斯卷积平滑Evoformer的权重张量并缩放结果，我们表明训练模型生成了具有物理结构的构象景观。在扰动下，泛素的天然接触按数十年折叠实验确立的顺序断裂。对于KaiB，五个独立训练的模型一致认为在扰动下无法恢复替代折叠。对于α-突触核蛋白，五个模型生成五个不同但一致的景观，映射出训练信号决定表征和未决定表征的区域。匹配功率噪声控制证实，同等幅度的随机破坏产生的是碎片而非构象。模型学习预测静态结构；扰动下可见的构象组织并非显式训练目标，表明其作为该目标的副产品出现。AlphaFold2的权重似乎编码了由进化和结构训练数据塑造的结构约束，其范围超越了未扰动推断所揭示的内容。我们将此方法称为神经谱学，缩放高斯卷积是其中一种协议。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters encode more than just sequence-to-structure mapping, researchers propose treating them as a scientific object for analysis. Using a method called Scaled Gaussian Convolution to smooth the Evoformer's weight tensors, they reveal physically structured conformational landscapes. Experiments show that under perturbation, ubiquitin’s native contacts break in experimentally established order, KaiB’s alternative fold remains inaccessible across multiple models, and alpha-synuclein’s landscapes vary yet remain coherent, indicating training signal distribution. Noise controls confirm that random corruption yields no meaningful conformations, suggesting the conformational organization emerges as a byproduct of structure prediction, not explicit training. This approach, termed neural spectroscopy, reveals structural constraints encoded in weights beyond what static inference shows.
研究者认为AlphaFold2的参数不仅用于序列到结构的转换，更可视为编码蛋白质构象组织的科学对象。通过使用缩放高斯卷积平滑Evoformer权重张量，他们揭示了具有物理结构的构象景观。实验表明，扰动下泛素的天然接触按几十年实验验证的顺序断裂，KaiB的替代折叠在多个模型中均无法恢复，而α-突触核蛋白的景观虽不同但具一致性，反映训练信号强弱分布。噪声控制实验表明，同等幅度的随机破坏不会产生有效构象，说明构象组织是训练副产物而非目标。该方法称为神经光谱学，揭示了权重中编码的结构约束，其范围超越静态预测。
Controlling Implicit Shortcut Reliance in L2 Spoken English Auto-markers
Authors: Shilin Gao, Mark J. F. Gales, Kate M. Knill
First: 2026-07-17T16:15:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:15:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Increasingly, speech and language processing tasks take either audio or text directly rather than extracting features from these as the input to the classifier or regressor. Often these systems make use of complex, for example transformer-based, processes that have the ability to derive highly non-linear mappings between the input and the output. Unfortunately these systems can also learn ''shortcuts'' where the classifier is overly reliant on particular aspects of the input to yield the output. For the task of language proficiency assessment, this over-reliance can enable learners to increase their score by exploiting the shortcut rather than improving their ability. This paper introduces a novel training criterion that is able to reduce the classifier's reliance on shortcuts, thus for example limiting this option for malpractice in language assessment. This process is illustrated on two forms of assessment system, one based on the audio the other on the speech recognition text. The results show that, for both systems, there is higher correlations with features that could be exploited for malpractice than expected from the human reference, indicating an over-reliance on these features. By introducing the modified training criterion, this correlation can be reduced to be closer to the reference correlation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：控制L2口语英语自动评分系统中的隐式快捷方式依赖
越来越多的语音和语言处理任务直接使用音频或文本，而非从中提取特征作为分类器或回归器的输入。这些系统常采用复杂的处理方式（如基于Transformer的模型），能够建立输入与输出之间高度非线性的映射。不幸的是，这些系统也可能学习到“捷径”，即分类器过度依赖输入的某些特定方面以生成输出。在语言能力评估任务中，这种过度依赖可能使学习者通过利用捷径提高分数，而非真正提升能力。本文提出了一种新颖的训练准则，旨在降低分类器对捷径的依赖，从而限制语言评估中的作弊可能性。该过程在两种评估系统中进行了演示：一种基于音频，另一种基于语音识别文本。结果表明，两种系统对可被用于作弊的特征的相关性均高于人工参考值，表明存在对这些特征的过度依赖。通过引入改进的训练准则，该相关性可降低至接近参考值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that modern speech-based language assessment systems may over-rely on input features that learners can exploit for malpractice, this paper proposes a novel training criterion designed to reduce such shortcut reliance. Applied to both audio-based and speech recognition text-based auto-markers, the method modifies the learning objective to discourage overfitting to easily manipulable input patterns. Experimental results show that without intervention, both systems exhibited unexpectedly high correlations with malpractice-prone features compared to human reference scores, indicating shortcut reliance. Introducing the modified criterion successfully reduced these correlations, aligning them more closely with human assessments.
该论文针对L2口语英语自动评分系统中模型对输入表面特征的过度依赖问题，提出一种新的训练准则以减少这种“捷径”依赖，从而遏制评分作弊。实验在音频和语音识别文本两种评估系统上进行，结果显示未干预时模型输出与可被利用的特征相关性高于人工评分参考值，表明存在捷径依赖。引入改进训练准则后，该相关性显著降低，更贴近人工评分基准。
Pick-to-Learn Calibration of an MPC Policy for an Origin-to-Destination Flight Problem
Authors: Marco C. Campi, Simone Garatti
First: 2026-07-17T16:14:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:14:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper illustrates the Pick-to-Learn methodology applied to the calibration of a Model Predictive Control policy. While developed around a specific example, the presentation is meant to highlight a methodology of broad applicability. The example concerns an aircraft traveling from an origin point to a destination point in the presence of uncertain crosswinds and a low-connectivity zone that should be avoided. The MPC policy is parameterized by two hyperparameters, which are selected from data by the P2L procedure. Starting from a dataset of 400 wind realizations, also called scenarios, P2L identifies a final compression set containing only two informative scenarios. The resulting MPC policy avoids the low-connectivity zone on all available scenarios and, according to the P2L theory, satisfies a probabilistic risk bound of $4.8\%$ at confidence level $1-10^{-5}$, where the risk is the probability of entering the low-connectivity zone in a future flight under a new wind realization not included in the sample.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向起讫点飞行问题的MPC策略的Pick-to-Learn校准
本文展示了Pick-to-Learn方法在模型预测控制（MPC）策略校准中的应用。虽然围绕一个具体示例展开，但其目的在于突出该方法的广泛适用性。该示例涉及一架飞机在存在不确定侧风和需避开的低连通区的情况下，从起始点飞往目的地。MPC策略由两个超参数参数化，通过P2L程序从数据中选择。从400个风况实现（即场景）的数据集中，P2L识别出仅包含两个信息性场景的最终压缩集。由此得到的MPC策略在所有可用场景中均避开低连通区，且根据P2L理论，在置信度1-10^{-5}下，满足4.8%的概率风险上限，其中风险指在未包含于样本的新风况下，未来飞行进入低连通区的概率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to calibrate a Model Predictive Control policy for an aircraft navigating uncertain crosswinds while avoiding a low-connectivity zone, this study applies the Pick-to-Learn (P2L) methodology to select informative wind scenarios from a dataset of 400 realizations. P2L identifies only two critical scenarios that suffice to define an optimal policy, which successfully avoids the low-connectivity zone across all tested scenarios. Theoretical guarantees from P2L confirm that the policy satisfies a probabilistic risk bound of 4.8% at a 99.999% confidence level, meaning the probability of entering the forbidden zone under a new, unseen wind condition is bounded by this value.
为解决飞机在不确定侧风环境下飞行并避开低连接区时的模型预测控制（MPC）策略校准问题，本文采用Pick-to-Learn（P2L）方法从400个风况场景中压缩出仅两个关键场景用于参数调优。最终得到的MPC策略在所有测试场景中均成功避开低连接区，并根据P2L理论，在99.999%置信水平下保证4.8%的风险上限，即未来在未见风况下进入该区域的概率极低。
Energy-Aware Collaborative Exploration for a UAV-UGV Team
Authors: Cahit Ikbal Er, Saikiran Juttu, Yasin Yazicioglu
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-03-23T19:14:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:14:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems 2026 (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present an energy-aware collaborative exploration framework for a UAV-UGV team operating in unknown environments, where the UAV's energy constraint is modeled as a maximum flight-time limit. The UAV executes a sequence of energy-bounded exploration tours, while the UGV simultaneously explores on the ground and serves as a mobile charging station. Rendezvous is enforced under a shared time budget so that the vehicles meet at the end of each tour before the UAV reaches its flight-time limit. We construct a sparsely coupled air-ground roadmap using a density-aware layered probabilistic roadmap (PRM) and formulate tour selection over the roadmap as coupled orienteering problems (OPs) to maximize information gain subject to the rendezvous constraint. The resulting tours are constructed over collision-validated roadmap edges. We validate our method through simulation studies, benchmark comparisons, and real-world experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向未知环境的无人机-地面机器人协同探索能量感知框架
我们提出了一种面向无人机-地面机器人协同探索的能量感知框架，其中无人机的能量约束建模为最大飞行时间限制。无人机执行一系列能量受限的探索巡游，同时地面机器人在地面进行探索并充当移动充电站。在共享的时间预算下强制执行会合，确保每次巡游结束前无人机与地面机器人在飞行时间耗尽前相遇。我们利用密度感知的分层概率路网（PRM）构建稀疏耦合的空地路网，并将巡游选择建模为耦合的寻路问题（OPs），以最大化信息增益并满足会合约束。生成的巡游路径基于碰撞验证的路网边构建。我们通过仿真研究、基准对比和实际实验验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and sustainable exploration in unknown environments, this work proposes an energy-aware collaborative framework for UAV-UGV teams where the UAV’s flight time is constrained. The method employs a density-aware layered PRM to build a sparsely coupled air-ground roadmap, then formulates tour selection as coupled orienteering problems to maximize information gain while ensuring rendezvous within a shared time budget before UAV energy depletion. Experimental results from simulations, benchmarks, and real-world tests demonstrate that the approach outperforms baseline methods in exploration efficiency and energy utilization.
为实现未知环境中高效可持续的探索，本文提出一种面向UAV-UGV协同的能耗感知框架，其中UAV飞行时间受限。方法中UAV执行能量受限的探索巡游，UGV同时地面探索并充当移动充电站，巡游结束前强制双方在共享时间预算内会合。通过密度感知分层概率路网构建空地稀疏耦合路网，将巡游选择建模为耦合寻路问题以最大化信息增益并满足会合约束。仿真、基准对比及实地实验结果表明，该方法相较基线方法在信息增益和能耗利用效率上均有显著提升。
Physics-Based Deep Spatiotemporal Hyperlocal Radar Nowcasting with a Multi-Variable U-Net for High-Resolution Precipitation Forecasting
Authors: Akshay Sunil, Muhammed Rashid, Raja Sekhar Sivaraju, Sushma Nair, Subimal Ghosh
First: 2026-07-17T16:09:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:09:48+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Geoscience and Remote Sensing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Precipitation nowcasting over the immediate 10-90 min period is important for flood management and real-time decision-making in urban regions. Conventional short-range forecasting with high-resolution numerical weather prediction requires frequent data assimilation, model initialization, and spin-up, introducing computational latency. Machine learning provides an alternative by learning storm evolution directly from high-frequency observations and producing forecasts quickly after training. This is particularly relevant for Mumbai, India, where monsoon convection, land-sea interactions, and localized intense rainfall make short-term prediction difficult. Here, we develop a compact radar-only nowcasting framework that combines multi-elevation reflectivity, Doppler radial velocity, and radial-velocity-gradient proxy features within an encoder-decoder U-Net. Using the most recent radar volume scan, the model predicts 12 future composite reflectivity fields at 7.5-min intervals up to 90 min lead time. The derived velocity magnitude, divergence-like, directional-shear, and vorticity-like channels represent kinematic signatures associated with convergence and boundary interactions without requiring full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module improves sensitivity to convective cores, and physics-guided attribution examines whether the learned sensitivities are meteorologically meaningful. The model is trained using Mumbai Doppler radar observations from May to August 2023 and evaluated on temporally independent events. At 90 min lead time, Critical Success Index values are 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for $\geq$10, $\geq$20, and $\geq$30 dBZ thresholds, respectively. Compared with persistence, the model gives lower RMSE and higher spatial correlation at longer lead times. Once trained, it runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理的深度时空超局部雷达短时预报：多变量U-Net用于高分辨率降水预测
在10至90分钟的短时范围内进行降水预报，对城市地区的洪水管理和实时决策至关重要。传统的高分辨率数值天气预报短程预测需要频繁的数据同化、模型初始化和启动，引入计算延迟。机器学习提供了一种替代方案，通过直接从高频观测数据中学习风暴演变，训练后能快速生成预报。这在印度孟买尤为相关，因为季风对流、海陆相互作用和局地强降雨使短期预测困难。本文开发了一种紧凑的仅雷达短时预报框架，结合多高度反射率、多普勒径向速度及径向速度梯度代理特征，嵌入编码器-解码器U-Net结构。利用最新雷达体扫数据，模型以7.5分钟间隔预测未来12个合成反射率场，最长可达90分钟提前量。所推导的速度幅值、散度类、方向剪切类和涡度类通道，代表与汇聚和边界相互作用相关的运动学特征，无需完整风场反演。高反射率注意力模块增强对对流核心区的敏感性，物理引导的归因分析检验所学敏感性是否具有气象意义。模型使用2023年5月至8月孟买多普勒雷达观测数据训练，并在时间独立事件上评估。在90分钟提前量下，≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值的临界成功指数分别为0.437、0.332、0.193。与持续性方法相比，模型在更长提前量下具有更低的RMSE和更高的空间相关性。训练完成后，模型可在标准计算机上运行，数秒内生成预报，适用于实时应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for rapid, high-resolution precipitation forecasting in complex urban environments like Mumbai, this study develops a physics-informed deep learning model that leverages multi-variable radar data to predict storm evolution. The method employs a compact U-Net architecture incorporating reflectivity, Doppler velocity, and velocity-gradient features to capture kinematic signatures without full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module enhances detection of convective cores, and physics-guided attribution validates meteorological relevance. Trained on Mumbai radar data from May to August 2023, the model forecasts 12 future reflectivity fields every 7.5 minutes up to 90 minutes ahead. At 90-minute lead time, it achieves Critical Success Index values of 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for ≥10, ≥20, and ≥30 dBZ thresholds, respectively, outperforming persistence in RMSE and spatial correlation for longer lead times, and runs in seconds on standard hardware.
为解决孟买等复杂城市环境中因季风对流和海陆相互作用导致的短时降水预报难题，本文提出一种基于多变量U-Net架构的物理驱动深度学习模型。该模型融合多高度反射率、多普勒径向速度及运动学代理特征（如散度、涡度类通道），无需完整风场重建。模型在2023年5月至8月孟买多普勒雷达数据上训练，可预测未来12个7.5分钟间隔的复合反射率场，最长预报时长90分钟。实验表明，在90分钟预报时效下，≥10、≥20、≥30 dBZ阈值的临界成功指数分别为0.437、0.332、0.193，相比持续性预报在RMSE上更低、空间相关性更高。
Adaptive Contrast Enhancement and Optimised Feature Matching for RootSIFT-Based Palm-Vein Recognition
Authors: Kaveen Perera, Fouad Khelifi, Ammar Belatreche
First: 2026-07-17T16:04:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:04:15+00:00
Comments: This is a preprint version. It is currently under peer review and pending publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Palm-vein recognition is a highly secure biometric modality due to the uniqueness and subcutaneous nature of vein patterns. However, low contrast in palm-vein images, caused by NIR light scattering and sensor limitations, remains a significant challenge. To address this, we propose the Intensity-Limited Adaptive Contrast Stretching with Bidirectional Gaussian-weighted Overlapping Tiles (ILACS-BGOT) method, an enhancement of the previously developed ILACS with Layered Gaussian-weighted Overlapping Tiles (ILACS-LGOT) technique. ILACS enhances local contrast, while BGOT mitigates blocky artefacts. This study further integrates RootSIFT features with KNN+RT and incorporates the previously introduced Mean and Median Distance (MMD) filter to investigate the parameter variations of both MMD and RT, and their impact on recognition performance. A comprehensive analysis was conducted across three benchmark datasets (CASIA, PolyU, and PUT), using 42 combinations of MMD filter thresholds and RT values. Results were evaluated using EER and Accuracy. Findings reveal that higher template sizes improve performance, while varying MMD thresholds reflect dataset-specific rotational variations. The proposed system demonstrates superior generalisability, achieving significant improvements in both EER and Accuracy over existing methods. Furthermore, the underlying ILACS-BGOT mechanism suggests potential applicability beyond palm vein recognition to other biometric modalities such as finger vein and palmprint recognition, and more generally to low-contrast image enhancement across computer vision applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于RootSIFT的掌静脉识别中的自适应对比度增强与优化特征匹配
掌静脉识别因其静脉图案的独特性和皮下特性，是一种高安全性的生物识别模态。然而，由于近红外光散射和传感器限制导致掌静脉图像对比度偏低，仍是一个重要挑战。为此，我们提出了一种改进的强度限制自适应对比度拉伸与双向高斯加权重叠块（ILACS-BGOT）方法，该方法在先前开发的ILACS-LGOT技术基础上进行优化。ILACS增强局部对比度，BGOT则减轻块状伪影。本研究进一步将RootSIFT特征与KNN+RT结合，并引入先前提出的均值与中位数距离（MMD）滤波器，以研究MMD和RT参数变化及其对识别性能的影响。在CASIA、PolyU和PUT三个基准数据集上，共测试了42种MMD滤波器阈值与RT值组合。结果通过等错误率（EER）和准确率评估。研究发现，更大的模板尺寸可提升性能，而不同MMD阈值反映了各数据集特有的旋转变化。所提系统表现出更优的泛化能力，在EER和准确率上均显著优于现有方法。此外，ILACS-BGOT机制本身也暗示其在其他生物识别模态（如指静脉和掌纹识别）及计算机视觉领域低对比度图像增强中具有潜在应用价值。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the challenge of low contrast in palm-vein images due to NIR scattering and sensor constraints, this study proposes ILACS-BGOT—a refined contrast enhancement method combining intensity-limited adaptive stretching with bidirectional Gaussian-weighted overlapping tiles—to improve local contrast while reducing block artifacts. The system integrates RootSIFT features with KNN+RT classification and applies a Mean and Median Distance filter to explore parameter sensitivity across three datasets. Experimental results across 42 parameter combinations show that larger template sizes enhance performance, while MMD thresholds correlate with dataset-specific rotational variations. The method outperforms existing approaches in both EER and Accuracy, demonstrating strong generalizability and potential for broader low-contrast image enhancement applications.
为解决近红外成像中因散射和传感器限制导致的掌静脉图像对比度低的问题，本文提出ILACS-BGOT方法，结合强度限制自适应对比度拉伸与双向高斯加权重叠块，以减少块状伪影。该方法与RootSIFT特征、KNN+RT分类器及MMD滤波器结合，通过在三个数据集上测试42组参数组合，发现模板尺寸越大性能越好，MMD阈值因数据集旋转特性而异，系统在EER和准确率上显著优于现有方法，且其增强机制可推广至指纹静脉、掌纹识别及其他低对比度图像处理任务。
HCIG: A Hierarchical Cross-Modal Incongruity Graph Network for Multimodal Sarcasm and Cyberbullying Detection
Authors: Bhavana Verma, Priyanka Meel, Dinesh Kumar Vishwakarma
First: 2026-07-17T16:02:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T16:02:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal sarcasm and cyberbullying detection remain challenging because the intended meaning often emerges from incongruity between textual and visual information rather than from either modality alone. Existing multimodal approaches primarily rely on feature fusion or cross-modal attention, which may not effectively capture hierarchical semantic inconsistencies across different levels of representation. To address this limitation, this paper proposes HCIG (Hierarchical Cross-modal Incongruity Graph Network), a novel framework that models cross-modal incongruity at token, phrase, and global levels using graph attention networks and adaptively integrates these representations through a learned hierarchical attention mechanism. As a complementary architecture, we also introduce GCCN (Graph-based Cross-modal Contradiction Network), which performs graph-based reasoning using contradiction-aware pooling for efficient multimodal interaction learning. The proposed models are evaluated on the MMSD sarcasm benchmark and the MultiBully cyberbullying dataset, together with comprehensive ablation studies and cross-task transfer experiments. Experimental results demonstrate that HCIG achieves the best performance on MMSD with 85.74% accuracy and 85.29% macro-F1, while GCCN attains the highest macro-F1 (68.66%) on MultiBully and HCIG achieves the highest accuracy (69.62%) and bullying-class F1 (74.90%). The findings demonstrate that hierarchical multi-granularity incongruity modeling provides more effective multimodal reasoning than conventional fusion strategies, offering a robust framework for sarcasm and cyberbullying detection in social media.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HCIG：一种用于多模态讽刺与网络欺凌检测的层次化跨模态不一致图网络
多模态讽刺与网络欺凌检测仍具挑战性，因为其本意常源于文本与视觉信息间的不一致，而非单一模态。现有方法主要依赖特征融合或跨模态注意力，难以有效捕捉不同表征层级上的层次化语义不一致。为此，本文提出HCIG（层次化跨模态不一致图网络），利用图注意力网络在词、短语和全局层级建模跨模态不一致，并通过学习的层次化注意力机制自适应融合这些表示。作为补充架构，我们还引入GCCN（基于图的跨模态矛盾网络），利用矛盾感知池化进行图推理，以高效学习多模态交互。所提模型在MMSD讽刺基准与MultiBully网络欺凌数据集上评估，并辅以全面消融研究与跨任务迁移实验。实验表明，HCIG在MMSD上准确率达85.74%、宏F1达85.29%，GCCN在MultiBully上宏F1最高（68.66%），HCIG在MultiBully上准确率最高（69.62%）且欺凌类F1最高（74.90%）。结果表明，层次化多粒度不一致建模比传统融合策略更有效，为社交媒体中的讽刺与网络欺凌检测提供稳健框架。
Summary / 总结
Detecting sarcasm and cyberbullying in social media is difficult because meaning often arises from mismatches between text and images, not from either alone. The paper introduces HCIG, a hierarchical graph network that models incongruity at token, phrase, and global levels using graph attention, then fuses these levels via learned hierarchical attention. It also presents GCCN, a graph-based contradiction network for efficient multimodal reasoning. Evaluated on MMSD and MultiBully datasets, HCIG achieved 85.74% accuracy and 85.29% macro-F1 on MMSD, while GCCN led in macro-F1 on MultiBully (68.66%) and HCIG led in accuracy and bullying-class F1 (69.62% and 74.90%). These results show that multi-granularity incongruity modeling outperforms traditional fusion methods.
为解决通过图文语义不一致而非单一模态检测讽刺与网络欺凌的难题，本文提出HCIG框架，利用图注意力网络在词、短语和全局层级建模跨模态语义不一致，并通过学习的层次注意力机制进行融合；同时提出GCCN网络，采用矛盾感知池化进行图结构推理以高效学习跨模态交互。在MMSD与MultiBully数据集上的实验表明，HCIG在MMSD上达到85.74%准确率和85.29%宏F1，GCCN在MultiBully上获得最高宏F1（68.66%），HCIG在MultiBully上实现最高准确率（69.62%）和欺凌类F1（74.90%），证明层次化多粒度不一致建模优于传统融合方法。
JoyNexus: Service-Oriented Multi-Tenant Post-Training for VLA Models
Authors: Haoran Sun, Wentao Zhang, Junyang Hua, Hedan Yang, Yongjian Guo, Yifei Zhang, Xiaolong Xiang, Mingxi Luo, Jing Long, Chen Zhao, Chen Zhou, Wanting Xu, Qiming Yang, Hui Zhang, Song Wang, Xiaodong Bai, Shuai Di, Xu Chu, Xiaotie Deng, Yicheng Gong, Junwu Xiong
First: 2026-07-17T15:58:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:58:20+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The post-training of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential due to the diversity of simulators, robot embodiments, and task objectives. Existing compute services, whether offered as direct accelerator rental or batch-workload submission, typically allocate an exclusive set of GPU and CPU resources to a single tenant. While this paradigm maximizes client flexibility, it burdens users with infrastructure adaptation, and the fixed card-hour accounting model renders short or bursty workloads both expensive for tenants and inefficient for the service provider. To address these challenges, we present JoyNexus, a unified service for multi-tenant VLA supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. JoyNexus decouples the Training Model Service, Inference Model Service, and Environment Service, each accessed through APIs and backed by resident shared base models with tenant-specific slots. Tenants can directly invoke high-level semantic APIs for training, rollout, and evaluation, or compose custom algorithms using lower-level APIs and their assigned endpoints. Multiple tenants submit workloads concurrently; their action modules, optimizers, rollout records, and policy versions remain isolated, and the service is scheduled by the global Training Queue and Inference Queue. To further improve multi-tenant training efficiency, JoyNexus introduces group batching for heterogeneous VLA data schemas that share a compatible model-facing prefix, enabling a single shared backbone forward pass over grouped samples. Finally, we evaluate JoyNexus through workload simulation and a group-batching pipeline in a realistic embodied scenario. Results show that, compared with isolated single-tenant execution, JoyNexus reduces aggregate GPU time and improves service utilization via cross-tenant scheduling on shared resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JoyNexus：面向多租户视觉-语言-动作模型的后训练服务架构
由于模拟器、机器人本体和任务目标的多样性，视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的后训练至关重要。现有计算服务，无论是直接租用加速器还是批量任务提交，通常为单个租户分配专属的GPU和CPU资源。虽然这种范式最大化了客户端灵活性，但迫使用户承担基础设施适配成本，且固定显卡小时计费模式使短时或突发性工作负载对租户昂贵，对服务提供商低效。为解决这些问题，我们提出JoyNexus，一个统一的多租户VLA监督微调、强化学习与评估服务。JoyNexus将训练模型服务、推理模型服务和环境服务解耦，各服务通过API访问，由驻留的共享基础模型支持，每个租户拥有专属插槽。租户可直接调用高层语义API进行训练、执行与评估，或使用低层API及其分配端点组合自定义算法。多个租户可并发提交任务；其动作模块、优化器、执行记录和策略版本相互隔离，服务由全局训练队列和推理队列调度。为进一步提升多租户训练效率，JoyNexus引入组批机制，支持异构VLA数据模式（共享兼容模型前缀），实现对分组样本的单次共享主干前向传播。最后，我们在真实具身场景中通过工作负载模拟和组批管道评估JoyNexus。结果表明，与独立单租户执行相比，JoyNexus通过跨租户调度共享资源，减少了总GPU时间，提升了服务利用率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of exclusive GPU/CPU allocation in VLA model post-training, JoyNexus introduces a multi-tenant service architecture that decouples training, inference, and environment services via API-accessible shared base models with tenant-specific slots. Tenants can use high-level semantic APIs or custom low-level APIs, with workloads scheduled via global queues and isolated execution contexts. The system further enhances efficiency through group batching of heterogeneous VLA data sharing compatible prefixes, enabling shared forward passes. Experimental results demonstrate reduced aggregate GPU usage and improved resource utilization compared to single-tenant execution.
为解决VLA模型后训练中独占GPU资源导致的效率低下问题，JoyNexus提出多租户服务架构，将训练、推理与环境服务解耦，通过API访问共享基础模型及租户专属槽位。租户可调用高层语义API或自定义底层工作流，工作负载由全局队列调度以确保状态与策略版本隔离。系统引入异构VLA数据的组批机制，共享兼容前缀以实现单次前向传播。实验表明，与单租户执行相比，JoyNexus显著降低GPU总耗时并提升资源利用率。
Digital Pantheon: Simulating and Auditing Coalition Formation with LLM Agents
Authors: Dylan Van Mulders, Matthias Bogaert, Dirk Van den Poel
First: 2026-07-16T15:08:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:57:42+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables, To be published in the AIDEM Workshop proceedings of the ECML PKDD 2026 Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The formation of political coalitions is a complex negotiation driven by both concrete policy objectives and deep-seated ideological convictions. While Large Language Models (LLMs) open new avenues for computational political science, the neutrality and helpfulness biases instilled by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) prevent them from sustaining steadfast partisan behaviour. We present a multi-agent framework that reconciles factual grounding with ideological alignment by combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG): DPO instils aggressive party-specific personas, while a per-party RAG pipeline keeps each agent bounded to its official manifesto. We operationalize the framework on the 2019 Flemish election, deploying the partisan agents in a hub-and-spoke negotiation arbitrated by a formateur. To make the emergent negotiation interpretable, we introduce a Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology (MILT) that traces every clause in the final agreement back to its manifesto origin and classifies it into five provenance states, a Coalition Influence Score (CIS) that aggregates these traceable contributions to identify which party shaped the agreement, and a real-world grounding pass that benchmarks each simulated provision against the historically adopted coalition agreement. Across three independent simulations the framework yields a stable winner and ranking (N-VA ahead of CD\&V and Open Vld), and manifesto-anchored lineage reliably predicts real-world materialization whereas hallucinated content does not. The result is a transparent, scalable testbed for the ex-ante exploration of party compatibility and formateur-mediated compromise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数字神殿：利用大语言模型代理模拟与审计联盟形成
