政治联盟的形成是受具体政策目标和深层意识形态信念驱动的复杂谈判过程。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）为计算政治科学开辟了新途径，但通过人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）所植入的中立性和助益性偏见，使其难以维持坚定的党派行为。我们提出一个多代理框架，通过结合监督微调（SFT）、直接偏好优化（DPO）和检索增强生成（RAG），在事实依据与意识形态对齐之间取得平衡：DPO赋予各代理激进的党派人格，而每个党派的RAG管道则确保代理严格依据其官方纲领行事。我们在2019年弗拉芒选举中实施该框架，将各党派代理部署于由组阁者仲裁的中心辐射式谈判中。为使生成的谈判过程可解释，我们引入多层信息溯源拓扑（MILT），将最终协议中的每一条款追溯至其纲领来源并归类为五种溯源状态；联盟影响力得分（CIS）聚合这些可追溯贡献，识别主导协议形成的政党；并引入现实世界锚定验证，将每项模拟条款与历史上实际采用的联盟协议进行基准对比。在三次独立模拟中，该框架稳定输出胜出者及排名（N-VA领先CD&V和Open Vld），纲领锚定的溯源可靠预测现实落地，而幻觉内容则无法实现。该成果构建了一个透明、可扩展的测试平台，用于事前探索政党兼容性及组阁者调解妥协。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate realistic political coalition formation using LLMs without compromising ideological fidelity, the authors develop a multi-agent framework combining Supervised Fine-Tuning, Direct Preference Optimization, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation to enforce party-specific personas and manifesto grounding. Applied to the 2019 Flemish election, the system deploys partisan agents in a formateur-mediated negotiation, with a novel Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology tracing agreement clauses back to manifesto origins and assigning Coalition Influence Scores. Across three simulations, the framework consistently produced stable outcomes with N-VA leading, and manifesto-based lineage accurately predicted real-world coalition agreements, while hallucinated content failed to materialize.
为解决大语言模型在模拟政治联盟谈判时因RLHF导致的中立性偏差问题，作者构建了一个结合监督微调、直接偏好优化和检索增强生成的多智能体框架，以强化各代理的党派身份与纲领约束。在2019年弗拉芒选举场景中，系统通过“协调人”仲裁各党派谈判，并引入多层信息溯源拓扑结构，追踪协议条款的纲领来源并计算联盟影响力得分。三次独立模拟均稳定输出N-VA领先的结果，且基于纲领的溯源准确预测了现实中的协议内容，而虚构内容则未被实现。
Frontier Language Models Struggle to Copy: Text Can Be Better Viewed in 2D
Authors: Haodong Wen, Yiran Zhang, Yingfa Chen, Kaifeng Lyu
First: 2026-07-17T15:56:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:56:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large language models (LLMs) can solve advanced reasoning problems in seconds, we show that even frontier models fail to perform a much simpler operation: exactly copying an input string that lies well within their context windows. We attribute this failure to positional encodings in Transformer architectures, whose inductive bias favors copying through a shortcut based on matching local contexts rather than carefully locating the corresponding input positions. To address this issue, we introduce 2D-RoPE, which organizes text into a 2D grid rather than a 1D sequence and assigns each token a row ID and a column ID. Under this view, copying becomes simply retrieving input tokens at a fixed column offset, which makes the task easy to learn. In synthetic copy experiments, shallow Transformers with 2D-RoPE achieve perfect copying at input lengths hundreds of times longer than those seen during training, whereas standard positional encodings fall far behind. We further show that the advantage of 2D-RoPE language models on copy tasks consistently holds in large-scale pretraining on DCLM with model sizes up to 1.4B parameters. Overall, our results suggest that viewing text in 2D can benefit language modeling, and we hope this encourages future work to further explore the potential of 2D positional encodings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前沿语言模型难以复制：文本更适合二维视角
尽管大型语言模型（LLM）能在几秒内解决高级推理问题，但我们发现，即使是前沿模型也难以完成一个更简单的操作：精确复制输入字符串，而该字符串完全位于其上下文窗口内。我们归因于Transformer架构中的位置编码，其归纳偏置倾向于通过匹配局部上下文的捷径方式实现复制，而非精确定位对应输入位置。为解决此问题，我们引入2D-RoPE，将文本组织为二维网格，为每个token分配行ID和列ID。在此视角下，复制任务简化为在固定列偏移处检索输入token，使任务易于学习。在合成复制实验中，使用2D-RoPE的浅层Transformer在输入长度达训练时数百倍的情况下仍能实现完美复制，而标准位置编码则远落后。我们进一步表明，2D-RoPE语言模型在DCLM大规模预训练中（模型规模高达1.4B参数）的复制任务优势始终存在。总体而言，我们的结果表明，从二维视角看待文本可提升语言建模效果，我们希望这能鼓励未来研究进一步探索二维位置编码的潜力。
Summary / 总结
The paper investigates why even advanced language models struggle to copy input strings, despite their ability to solve complex reasoning tasks, attributing this to the inductive bias of standard Transformer positional encodings that favor local context matching over precise positional retrieval. To resolve this, the authors propose 2D-RoPE, a positional encoding scheme that maps text into a 2D grid with row and column IDs, transforming copying into a simple column-offset retrieval task. Experiments show that models using 2D-RoPE achieve perfect copying at input lengths hundreds of times longer than their training data, while standard encodings perform poorly; this advantage persists across large-scale pretraining with up to 1.4B parameters, suggesting 2D text representation may improve language modeling.
该研究探讨了为何即使最先进的语言模型也难以复制输入字符串，尽管它们能快速解决复杂推理任务。作者认为，标准Transformer位置编码导致模型倾向于通过局部上下文匹配的捷径进行复制，而非精确定位输入位置。为此，他们提出2D-RoPE方案，将文本视为二维网格，赋予每个词元行和列ID，使复制变为在固定列偏移处检索词元。实验表明，使用2D-RoPE的模型在输入长度达训练长度数百倍时仍能完美复制，而标准编码模型表现不佳。该优势在1.4B参数规模的预训练中依然显著，表明二维位置编码可能为语言建模提供更优框架。
Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics
Authors: Dominik Dahlem, Diego Maniloff, Mac Misiura
First: 2026-05-06T13:25:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:52:32+00:00
Comments: 47 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables; 84-page online supplement (proofs, additional experiments, dataset statistics) as an ancillary file
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Every attention head defines a degree-normalized transport operator, and a growing family of diagnostics reads model behavior (hallucination among them) from its spectrum. We ask what such diagnostics can and cannot infer. The operator splits orthogonally into a symmetric part governing transport \emph{capacity} and an antisymmetric part encoding \emph{orientation}. We prove an identifiability limit: every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic is \emph{orientation-blind} (unable to distinguish an operator from its transpose, hence blind to the orientation of information flow), with a transpose-stability bound limiting any Lipschitz diagnostic's transpose sensitivity by the asymmetry coefficient $G$. This bounds what spectral diagnostics of the attention operator can resolve (e.g.\ LapEigvals and the attention-spectral branch of LLM-Check). On the surviving axis, a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape shows uniform causal attention obeys an $n$-independent \emph{temporal-cut} floor $φ\ge 1/5$ while window attention pierces it as $O(w/n)$; the floor is an idealized benchmark, not an empirical attractor, and the fraction of real heads falling below it is itself an empirically stable architectural descriptor. The two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for asymmetry magnitude) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction, borne out \emph{in sign} under length-controlled, forced-scoring evaluation across decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder--decoder models (capacity-axis signal 0.62--0.84 LC-AUROC): polarity reverses between HaluEval and MedHallu, directionally as predicted though asymmetric in strength, with decision polarity calibrated per regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自注意力作为传输：对称谱诊断的局限
每个注意力头定义了一个度归一化的传输算子，一系列新兴诊断方法从其谱中读取模型行为（如幻觉等）。我们探讨此类诊断能与不能推断什么。该算子正交分解为控制传输能力的对称部分和编码方向性的反对称部分。我们证明了可识别性极限：每个转置不变的谱诊断都是方向盲的（无法区分算子与其转置，因而对信息流方向无感知），且转置稳定性界限制了任何Lipschitz诊断的转置敏感度，由不对称系数G限定。这界定了注意力算子谱诊断所能解析的内容（如LapEigvals及LLM-Check中的注意力谱分支）。在剩余轴上，闭式二分Cheeger景观表明，均匀因果注意力满足与n无关的时序切割下限φ≥1/5，而窗口注意力可突破该下限至O(w/n)；该下限为理想化基准而非经验吸引子，实际头中低于该下限的比例本身是经验稳定的架构描述符。双轴诊断（φ表容量，G表不对称程度）可产生可证伪的极性预测，在长度控制、强制评分评估下，于解码器独占、编码器独占及编码器-解码器模型中，极性符号与预测一致（容量轴信号0.62–0.84 LC-AUROC）：在HaluEval与MedHallu间极性反转，方向符合预测但强度不对称，决策极性按场景校准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the limits of spectral diagnostics for detecting hallucinations in self-attention models, this work frames each attention head as a degree-normalized transport operator, decomposing it into symmetric (capacity) and antisymmetric (orientation) components. The authors prove that any transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic is inherently orientation-blind, with sensitivity bounded by the asymmetry coefficient G, limiting what such diagnostics can resolve. Experimental results show that uniform causal attention maintains a temporal-cut floor φ≥1/5, while window attention degrades it as O(w/n); the fraction of heads below this floor is a stable architectural descriptor. A two-axis diagnostic (φ for capacity, G for asymmetry) yields falsifiable polarity predictions, confirmed in sign across model types under length-controlled evaluation, with capacity-axis signals ranging 0.62–0.84 LC-AUROC, and polarity reversing between datasets as predicted.
为理解自注意力机制中谱诊断方法的理论极限，本文将每个注意力头建模为度归一化传输算子，并分解为对称（容量）与反对称（方向）部分。证明任何转置不变的谱诊断方法均无法区分算子与其转置，即对信息流向方向无感知，其敏感度受不对称系数G限制。实验表明，均匀因果注意力保持时间切割下限φ≥1/5，而窗口注意力降至O(w/n)，揭示稳定架构特征。双轴诊断（φ, G）可生成可证伪的极性预测，在不同模型类型下经长度控制评估验证，容量轴信号达0.62–0.84 LC-AUROC，虽在不同数据集间极性反转，但方向符合预期，强度存在差异。
LLM-Powered Agentic AI for 5G/6G Networks: A Tutorial and Survey on Architectures, Protocols, and Standardization
Authors: Mazene Ameur, Abdelkader Mekrache, Bouziane Brik, Adlen Ksentini
First: 2026-07-17T15:47:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:47:23+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 4 figures, Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI), enabled by Large Language Models, marks a shift from rule-based automation toward autonomous, goal-driven control of Next-Generation Networks (NGNs). Existing surveys treat the two domains in isolation, leaving protocol integration, evaluation, and standardization alignment underexplored. To address this gap, a two-part tutorial-and-survey is presented. Part I formalises the control, management, and AI-native planes of 5G and 6G. It then covers the foundations of agentic systems: reasoning, planning, tool use, multi-agent coordination, and evaluation. Part II maps agentic capabilities onto 5G/6G control surfaces, standardization, and major 6G initiatives. Finally, it identifies open challenges shaping autonomous telecommunications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的代理型人工智能在5G/6G网络中的应用：架构、协议与标准化的教程与综述
由大语言模型驱动的代理型人工智能标志着从基于规则的自动化向自主、目标导向的下一代网络（NGN）控制转变。现有综述将这两个领域割裂处理，导致协议集成、评估及标准化对齐研究不足。为此，本文提供两部分教程与综述：第一部分形式化5G和6G的控制、管理和AI原生平面，并介绍代理系统基础：推理、规划、工具使用、多代理协调与评估；第二部分将代理能力映射至5G/6G控制面、标准化及主要6G倡议，并识别塑造自主电信的关键开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify agentic AI with 5G/6G network design, this work surveys how Large Language Models enable autonomous, goal-driven network control. It formalizes the AI-native control and management planes of NGNs and details agentic capabilities like reasoning, planning, and multi-agent coordination. The study maps these capabilities onto 5G/6G protocol layers and standardization efforts, identifying key gaps in protocol integration, evaluation frameworks, and standardization alignment that hinder autonomous network deployment.
为弥合大语言模型驱动的自主AI与5G/6G网络架构之间的鸿沟，本文系统梳理并教程化了LLM如何赋能网络自主控制。首先，文章定义了5G/6G网络的控制、管理与AI原生平面，并构建了自主系统的基础能力框架，包括推理、规划、工具使用与多智能体协同。随后，将这些能力映射到5G/6G协议层、标准化进程及主要6G倡议中，最终识别出塑造未来自主通信系统的关键开放挑战。
Spatial Normalization for Cross-Domain Retinal Layer Segmentation in Optical Coherence Tomography
Authors: Iker Moran-Cavero, Monica Hernandez, Elvira Mayordomo, Naiara Artiaga, Beatriz Pardiñas, Beatriz Cordon, Elena Garcia-Martin
First: 2026-07-17T15:47:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:47:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retinal layer segmentation in Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT) is a fundamental step for extracting quantitative biomarkers of retinal structure. Indeed, there is a growing interest in the analysis of OCTs in the context of neurodegenerative diseases. However, segmentation remains challenging due to speckle noise, shadowing artifacts, low contrast between adjacent layers, anatomical variability across subjects, and domain shifts arising from different acquisition protocols and clinical populations. While deep learning methods have achieved remarkable performance, their robustness and generalization across heterogeneous datasets remain limited. In this work, we investigate the role of spatial normalization as a preprocessing strategy to mitigate geometric domain shifts and improve the consistency of retinal layer segmentation. Inspired by standard practices in neuroimaging, we introduce a fovea-centered normalization framework that aligns OCT volumes into a common anatomical reference. We perform a comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art deep learning architectures. To provide a comprehensive assessment of segmentation quality, we combine conventional overlap-based metrics at B-scan level with topology-aware metrics at A-scan level and thickness-based measures at the en-face level. In cases where a ground truth is not available, we propose topology violation quantitative metrics that do not require ground truth annotations and a thickness-based qualitative assessment that captures structural consistency and clinically relevant patterns at the en-face level. The results demonstrate the importance of spatial normalization in OCT segmentation pipelines toward the development of robust and clinically meaningful retinal analysis tools, enabling reliable biomarker extraction and downstream computational analysis in neurodegenerative research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：光学相干断层扫描中跨域视网膜层分割的空间归一化
光学相干断层扫描（OCT）中的视网膜层分割是提取视网膜结构定量生物标志物的基础步骤。鉴于神经退行性疾病研究中对OCT分析兴趣日益增长，但分割仍面临斑点噪声、阴影伪影、相邻层间对比度低、个体解剖变异及因采集协议和临床人群不同导致的域偏移等挑战。尽管深度学习方法已取得显著成果，其在异质数据集上的鲁棒性和泛化能力仍有限。本研究探讨空间归一化作为预处理策略，以缓解几何域偏移并提升视网膜层分割的一致性。受神经影像学标准实践启发，我们引入以黄斑为中心的归一化框架，将OCT体数据对齐至共同解剖参考系。我们全面评估了当前最先进的深度学习架构。为全面评估分割质量，我们结合B扫描层面的重叠度量、A扫描层面的拓扑感知度量及眼底层面的厚度度量。在无真值标注情况下，我们提出无需真值标注的拓扑违规量化指标及眼底层面的厚度定性评估，以捕捉结构一致性和临床相关模式。结果表明，空间归一化在OCT分割流程中至关重要，有助于开发稳健且具临床意义的视网膜分析工具，从而实现可靠的生物标志物提取及神经退行性疾病研究中的下游计算分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust retinal layer segmentation in OCT for neurodegenerative disease research, this study introduces a fovea-centered spatial normalization method to reduce geometric domain shifts across heterogeneous datasets. The approach aligns OCT volumes into a common anatomical reference, enhancing consistency before applying deep learning models. Experimental results show that normalization significantly improves segmentation accuracy, as measured by B-scan overlap metrics, A-scan topology-aware scores, and en-face thickness consistency, even in the absence of ground truth using topology violation and thickness-based qualitative metrics.
为解决因解剖变异和域偏移导致的OCT视网膜层分割在不同数据集间不一致的问题，本研究引入受神经影像学启发的以黄斑为中心的空间归一化方法，将OCT体积对齐至共同解剖参考系以减少几何差异。在多种深度学习架构上进行评估，结合B扫描重叠度、A扫描拓扑感知指标及眼底厚度评估（含无标注数据的拓扑违规量化指标），结果表明该方法显著提升分割精度与结构一致性。实验证明空间归一化能有效增强模型鲁棒性与泛化能力，为神经退行性疾病研究中可靠的生物标志物提取奠定基础。
When Model Merging Rivals Joint Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Task-Vector Geometry Analysis
Authors: S. Aaron McClendon
First: 2026-07-17T15:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:41:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging is promoted as a substitute for joint multi-task training, yet in the reinforcement-learning setting this substitution is essentially never tested against the baseline it claims to replace: methods merge independently released agents precisely because a joint model is unavailable. We build the missing comparison. Training difficulty-1 and difficulty-2 Qwen3-8B specialists on the AppWorld agent benchmark with LOOP, we merge them (TIES, RAM+) and pit the result against a jointly trained model on the same data. On task-goal completion, merging matches joint RL -- and every merge variant is statistically indistinguishable. To explain why merge method does not matter here, we measure the geometry of the specialists' task vectors, which carries no task-sampling noise: they are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.06 - 0.10) despite ~65% support overlap, a small, shared direction that grows over training and that we calibrate against a random-init floor and a same-run ceiling to confirm it reflects learning, not the low-rank parameterization. Because direction and support are decoupled, support and sign-based merging (RAM, TIES) collapse to near-uniform averaging. We release all code and statistics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当模型合并与联合多任务强化学习竞争时：任务向量几何分析
模型合并被提倡作为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习场景中，这种替代从未真正与它所声称取代的基线方法进行比较：方法合并独立发布的智能体，恰恰是因为联合模型不可用。我们构建了缺失的比较：在AppWorld智能体基准上，使用LOOP训练难度1和难度2的Qwen3-8B专家模型，然后进行合并（TIES、RAM+），并将结果与同一数据集上联合训练的模型进行对比。在任务目标完成率上，合并结果与联合强化学习表现相当，且每种合并变体在统计上均无显著差异。为解释为何此处合并方法无关紧要，我们测量专家模型任务向量的几何结构，其不受任务采样噪声影响：尽管支持度重叠约65%，它们近似正交（余弦值0.06-0.10），但存在一个微小的共享方向，该方向随训练增长，我们通过随机初始化下限和同次运行上限校准，确认其反映学习过程而非低秩参数化。由于方向与支持度解耦，基于支持度和符号的合并方法（RAM、TIES）退化为近似均匀平均。我们公开所有代码和统计数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of direct comparison between model merging and joint multi-task reinforcement learning, the paper evaluates whether merging independently trained specialists can rival joint training. Using Qwen3-8B agents trained on AppWorld with LOOP, it compares merging methods (TIES, RAM+) against a jointly trained model, finding no statistically significant difference in task-goal completion. The authors attribute this to the task vectors of the specialists being nearly orthogonal (cosine 0.06–0.10) despite 65% support overlap, indicating that the shared learning direction is small and emerges during training, not from parameterization. This decoupling explains why support- and sign-based merging collapse to near-uniform averaging.
为填补模型合并与联合多任务强化学习直接比较的空白，该研究在AppWorld基准上使用LOOP训练两个Qwen3-8B专家模型，再通过TIES和RAM+方法进行合并，并与联合训练模型对比。结果表明，合并模型在任务目标完成率上与联合训练模型表现无统计差异。作者解释这是由于专家模型的任务向量近正交（余弦值0.06–0.10），尽管支持集重叠达65%，但存在一个微小共享方向，该方向随训练增长，且经与随机初始化下限和同运行上限对比，确认其反映真实学习而非低秩参数化偏差。由于方向与支持集解耦，基于支持集和符号的合并方法最终趋近于均匀平均。
MVI-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating Robustness to Misleading Visual Inputs in LVLMs
Authors: Huiyi Chen, Jiawei Peng, Dehai Min, Changchang Sun, Kaijie Chen, Yan Yan, Xu Yang, Lu Cheng
First: 2025-11-18T05:48:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:39:00+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Evaluating the robustness of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) is essential for their continued development and responsible deployment in real-world applications. However, existing robustness benchmarks typically focus on hallucination or misleading textual inputs, while largely overlooking the equally critical challenge posed by misleading visual inputs in assessing visual understanding. To fill this important gap, we introduce MVI-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark specially designed for evaluating how Misleading Visual Inputs undermine the robustness of LVLMs. Grounded in fundamental visual primitives, the design of MVI-Bench centers on three hierarchical levels of misleading visual inputs: Visual Concept, Visual Attribute, and Visual Relationship. Using this taxonomy, we curate six representative categories and compile 1,248 expertly annotated VQA instances. To facilitate fine-grained robustness evaluation, we further introduce MVI-Sensitivity, a novel metric that characterizes LVLM robustness at a granular level. Empirical results across 18 state-of-the-art LVLMs uncover pronounced vulnerabilities to misleading visual inputs, and our in-depth analyses on MVI-Bench provide actionable insights that can guide the development of more reliable and robust LVLMs. The benchmark and codebase can be accessed at https://github.com/chenyil6/MVI-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MVI-Bench：评估大型视觉-语言模型对误导性视觉输入鲁棒性的综合基准
评估大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的鲁棒性，对于其持续发展和在现实应用中的负责任部署至关重要。然而，现有鲁棒性基准通常侧重于幻觉或误导性文本输入，而忽视了评估视觉理解时同样关键的误导性视觉输入挑战。为填补这一重要空白，我们提出了MVI-Bench，这是首个专门设计用于评估误导性视觉输入如何削弱LVLMs鲁棒性的综合基准。该基准基于基本视觉原语，围绕三个层次的误导性视觉输入设计：视觉概念、视觉属性和视觉关系。基于此分类体系，我们精选了六个代表性类别，汇编了1,248个专家标注的视觉问答实例。为进一步实现细粒度鲁棒性评估，我们引入了MVI-Sensitivity这一新颖指标，用于刻画LVLM在细粒度层面的鲁棒性。在18个最先进的LVLMs上的实证结果揭示了其对误导性视觉输入的显著脆弱性，而我们在MVI-Bench上的深入分析提供了可操作的见解，有助于指导更可靠、更鲁棒的LVLMs的开发。基准和代码库可访问：https://github.com/chenyil6/MVI-Bench。

为评估误导性视觉输入对大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的影响，作者提出了MVI-Bench，这是首个专门针对视觉鲁棒性评估的综合性基准。该基准基于视觉概念、属性和关系三个层级设计，包含六个类别共1248个专家标注的视觉问答实例。为实现细粒度评估，作者引入了MVI-Sensitivity新指标，用于衡量模型在输入层面的脆弱性。在18个主流LVLMs上的实验表明，模型在面对误导性视觉输入时表现显著下降，揭示了当前模型普遍存在的脆弱性，指明了提升鲁棒性的关键方向。
ArtChart: A Benchmark for Faithful Artistic Chart Generation with Integrated Text Rendering
Authors: Meijia Huang, Yingjie Yin, Shihao Wang, Chenguang Ma
First: 2026-07-17T15:38:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:38:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artistic charts make data memorable and visually engaging, but generating them faithfully demands simultaneously preserving numerical geometry, rendering exact in-image text, binding labels to correct marks, and maintaining coherent artistic style. Current text-to-image and image editing models frequently fail on these coupled constraints, producing distorted geometries, hallucinated text, misbound labels, or over-stylized marks that undermine readability and mathematical integrity. This paper introduces ArtChart, a framework for artistic chart generation with integrated text rendering, encompassing a task definition, benchmark, and evaluation protocol. This is the first work to simultaneously address mathematically faithful chart synthesis, accurate in-image text rendering, and artistic stylization of chart elements. ArtChart features a chart-specific plug-and-play module conditioned on text-free grayscale chart layouts, ensuring mathematical and logical fidelity. A RL learning strategy with OCR accuracy, layout quality, and aesthetic rewards refines generation, while a multi-expert distillation framework resolves inter-reward conflicts through specialized expert optimization. We construct ArtChart-Bench, a bilingual 2K-prompt benchmark spanning four chart types and diverse label formats. We further design ArtChart-Eval, a six-axis evaluation suite covering mathematical logic, text accuracy, text layout, aesthetics, instruction following, and readability, supporting comparison across T2I, image-editing, controllable-generation, and closed-source API models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ArtChart consistently outperforms open-source baselines, producing charts that are both visually appealing and math faithful.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArtChart：一种支持集成文本渲染的忠实艺术图表生成基准
艺术图表使数据更具记忆点和视觉吸引力，但要忠实地生成它们，需同时保持数值几何结构、精确渲染图像内文本、将标签正确绑定至对应标记，并维持一致的艺术风格。当前的文本到图像及图像编辑模型常因这些耦合约束而失败，导致几何形变、文本幻觉、标签错配或过度风格化标记，损害可读性和数学严谨性。本文提出ArtChart框架，涵盖任务定义、基准与评估协议，首次同时解决数学忠实的图表合成、图像内文本精确渲染及图表元素的艺术风格化问题。ArtChart包含一个基于无文本灰度图表布局的专用插拔式模块，确保数学与逻辑一致性；结合OCR准确率、布局质量与美学奖励的强化学习策略优化生成；多专家蒸馏框架通过专用专家优化解决奖励冲突。我们构建ArtChart-Bench，涵盖四种图表类型与多样标签格式的双语2000提示基准；并设计ArtChart-Eval六维评估体系，覆盖数学逻辑、文本准确性、文本布局、美学、指令遵循与可读性，支持跨T2I、图像编辑、可控生成及闭源API模型的比较。大量实验表明，ArtChart持续优于开源基线，生成兼具视觉吸引力与数学忠实性的图表。

Motivated by the challenge of generating artistic charts that preserve mathematical accuracy while rendering in-image text and maintaining coherent style, this paper introduces ArtChart, a framework combining a task definition, benchmark, and evaluation protocol. It employs a text-free grayscale layout module to ensure geometric and logical fidelity, then refines outputs via reinforcement learning with OCR, layout, and aesthetic rewards, and resolves reward conflicts through multi-expert distillation. Evaluated on ArtChart-Bench (2K prompts across four chart types) using ArtChart-Eval (six axes), the framework consistently outperforms open-source baselines, producing charts that are both visually appealing and mathematically faithful.
为解决生成兼具数学准确性、图像内文字精确性和艺术风格一致性的艺术图表难题，本文提出ArtChart框架，包含任务定义、基准与评估协议。该方法通过文本无关灰度布局模块确保几何与逻辑保真度，再结合强化学习（融合OCR、布局与美学奖励）与多专家蒸馏策略优化生成。在包含2000个提示的ArtChart-Bench（四种图表类型）上，通过六维评估体系（数学逻辑、文字准确性、布局、美学、指令遵循、可读性）验证，其表现显著优于开源基线模型，生成图表兼具视觉美感与数学保真度。
What Is the Minimum Architecture for Prolepsis? Early Irrevocable Commitment Across Tasks in Small Transformers
Authors: Éric Jacopin
First: 2026-04-16T13:38:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:38:27+00:00
Comments: v2: corrects three citations; reconciles main-text/appendix numbers (the two Llama sweeps now labeled); the effective last-token site differs from Anthropic's newline (position-specificity replicates, site identity does not); adds an erratum on a CLT encoder-hook mismatch (detection-side only); softens novelty, necessity, and depth claims per COLM 2026 reviews; defines prolepsis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When do transformers commit to a decision, and what prevents them from correcting it? We introduce prolepsis: a transformer commits early, task-specific attention heads sustain the commitment, and no layer corrects it. Replicating Lindsey et al.'s (2025) planning-site finding on open models (Gemma 2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), we ask five questions. (Q1) Planning is invisible to six residual-stream methods; among those tested, only CLT-based steering succeeds. (Q2) The single-site spike replicates in shape, at the final prompt token (Anthropic's site is the newline; see the Note added). (Q3) Specific attention heads route the decision to the output, filling a gap flagged as invisible to attribution graphs. (Q4) The evidence is consistent with search within at most 16 layers and commitment beyond, a two-model hypothesis. (Q5) Factual recall shows the same motif at a different network depth, with zero overlap between recurring planning heads and the factual top-10. Prolepsis recurs across tasks in the decoder-only models tested: the template is shared, the routing substrates differ. All experiments run on a single consumer GPU (16 GB VRAM).
中文标题/摘要
标题：前置承诺的最小架构是什么？小型Transformer中跨任务的早期不可撤销承诺
Transformer何时做出决策承诺，以及什么阻止其修正？我们引入“前置承诺”概念：Transformer早期承诺，任务特定的注意力头维持该承诺，且无层对其进行修正。复现Lindsey等人（2025）在开放模型（Gemma 2 2B、Llama 3.2 1B）上的规划位发现，我们提出五个问题。（Q1）规划对六种残差流方法不可见；测试方法中仅CLT基线引导成功。（Q2）单点峰值在最终提示标记处重现（Anthropic的位为换行符；见新增注释）。（Q3）特定注意力头将决策路由至输出，填补了归因图无法识别的空白。（Q4）证据支持搜索范围不超过16层，承诺在之后，即双模型假设。（Q5）事实回忆在不同网络深度呈现相同模式，且重复规划头与事实Top-10无重叠。前置承诺在测试的解码器-only模型中跨任务重现：模板共享，路由子基不同。所有实验在单张消费级GPU（16GB VRAM）上运行。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates when and how transformers commit to decisions irreversibly, introducing the concept of 'prolepsis'—where early attention heads make task-specific commitments that persist through subsequent layers without correction. The researchers replicate and extend Lindsey et al.’s planning-site findings using small decoder-only models (Gemma 2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B) on a single consumer GPU, asking five targeted questions. Results show residual-stream methods fail to detect planning, only CLT-based steering works; a single-site spike occurs at the final token; specific attention heads route decisions to output, revealing hidden attribution; evidence suggests search within 16 layers followed by commitment beyond; and factual recall shows distinct, non-overlapping planning heads. Prolepsis is consistent across tasks and models, with shared routing templates but differing substrates.
该研究探讨了Transformer何时及如何做出不可撤销的决策，提出了‘先决承诺’（prolepsis）的概念——即早期任务特定的注意力头会维持并阻止决策被修正。研究者在单个消费级GPU上使用小型解码器模型（Gemma 2 2B、Llama 3.2 1B），复现并扩展了Lindsey等人关于规划站点的发现，提出五个关键问题。结果表明，多数残差流方法无法检测到规划，仅CLT引导有效；决策峰值出现在最后一个提示词；特定注意力头负责将决策路由至输出；决策承诺发生在16层内，而搜索在之后继续；事实回忆在不同网络深度也呈现相似模式，但规划头与事实头无重叠。先决承诺在不同任务和模型中普遍存在，表明存在共享架构模板与任务特异性路由机制。
What Models Express, Suppress, and Resist: Auditing Open-Weight LLMs with Persona Vectors
Authors: Winston Zeng, Ali Emami, Jinho D. Choi
First: 2026-07-14T18:10:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:37:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
What a language model will and will not do is largely set during post-training, but which behaviors it expresses, hides, or resists is not revealed by prompting alone. Persona vectors, behavioral directions in activation space, can probe this organization, but prior work covers only a handful of traits. We present the first systematic application of persona vectors at this scale, compiling a 53-trait inventory across four behaviorally distinct domains and labeling every trait in two open-weight models as natural (expressed at baseline), steerable latent but amplifiable, or intractable (resistant to standard extraction). Both models default to helpful, task-oriented behavior: all nine agentic traits are natural, and their default clinician behavior matches a board-certified psychologist's independent desirability judgments on 16 of 17 traits. Steering produces its largest gains on traits these defaults exclude: hyperbole, hallucination, and sycophancy. The same asymmetry holds across all 171 generic-trait pairs: two steerable traits can collapse the composition, but pairs involving a default never do. Where standard extraction fails on a trait like "evil," a vector transferred from a fine-tuned variant still recovers it, with the residual refusals appearing inside the model's chain-of-thought. Persona vectors are most informative not as a set of controls but as a probe of behavioral organization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型表达、抑制与抵抗什么：使用人格向量审计开源大语言模型
语言模型能做什么、不能做什么主要在后训练阶段确定，但其具体表现出、隐藏或抵抗哪些行为，仅靠提示无法揭示。人格向量作为激活空间中的行为方向，可探测这种组织结构，但此前研究仅覆盖少量特质。本文首次系统性地在该规模上应用人格向量，构建涵盖四个行为迥异领域的53项特质清单，并对两个开源模型中的每项特质标注为自然（基线即表达）、可引导但可放大，或不可控（抵抗标准提取）。两个模型默认表现出有益、任务导向的行为：九项代理特质均为自然，其默认临床行为在17项特质中的16项上与注册心理学家的独立偏好判断一致。引导在默认排除的特质（如夸张、幻觉、谄媚）上效果最显著。这种不对称性在全部171对通用特质中同样成立：两个可引导特质可压缩组合，但涉及默认特质的配对则无法实现。当标准提取在“邪恶”等特质上失败时，从微调变体转移的向量仍能恢复该特质，残余拒绝出现在模型的思维链中。人格向量最富信息量之处不在于作为控制手段，而在于作为行为组织结构的探测工具。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how open-weight language models express, suppress, or resist specific behaviors beyond their default helpfulness, using persona vectors—directions in activation space that probe behavioral tendencies. The researchers systematically applied these vectors across 53 traits spanning four behavioral domains, labeling each trait in two models as natural (expressed by default), steerable but amplifiable, or intractable (resistant to standard extraction). Both models exhibited natural expression of nine agentic traits and aligned closely with expert judgments on clinician behavior. Steering yielded the greatest gains for traits excluded from defaults, such as hyperbole and sycophancy, while traits tied to default behavior rarely collapsed under composition. Even when standard extraction failed—for example, for 'evil'—transferring a vector from a fine-tuned model still recovered the behavior, with refusals appearing in internal reasoning. The key insight is that persona vectors serve best as probes of behavioral organization rather than as direct controls.
该研究通过使用‘人格向量’（激活空间中的行为方向）系统探究开放权重大语言模型在默认行为之外如何表达、抑制或抵抗特定行为。研究人员对53个特质（涵盖四个行为领域）进行了系统性分析，将每个特质在两个模型中标记为‘自然’（默认表达）、‘可引导但可放大’或‘不可控’（抵抗标准提取）。两个模型默认表现出助人、任务导向行为，9个代理特质均为自然表达，其临床行为与17个特质中16个专家判断相符。引导效果在默认未包含的特质（如夸张、幻觉、奉承）上最显著，且存在不对称性：可引导特质可组合，但默认特质不可。即使标准提取对‘邪恶’等特质失败，从微调模型转移向量仍可恢复该行为，拒绝理由出现在模型内部推理中。人格向量的价值在于揭示行为组织结构，而非作为直接控制手段。
Frontier AI performance across the business disciplines: a case-grounded benchmark of knowledge work and analytical reasoning
Authors: Ajay Patel, Kartik Hosanagar, Ramayya Krishnan, Chris Callison-Burch, Karim Lakhani, Mitch Weiss
First: 2026-07-17T15:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:34:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are improving rapidly as reflected in benchmark scores, yet these AI benchmarks largely test capabilities such as factual recall, narrow question answering, mathematical problem-solving, and coding and agentic tool-use. What remains poorly measured is AI progress on the analytical knowledge work white-collar professionals perform daily, including synthesizing complex information, exercising judgment under uncertainty and incomplete information, applying strategic and adversarial thinking in multi-stakeholder settings, weighing trade-offs, and producing defensible, structured analyses. This gap is even more pronounced for subjective components of such work, where success can be challenging to define. The "case method" form of education practiced by top business schools provides a natural foundation for addressing this measurement gap, and we construct BusinessCaseBench, a benchmark spanning hundreds of questions drawn from business cases across eighteen disciplines, each paired with a grading rubric derived from the expert-written instructor case solution. On BusinessCaseBench, frontier AI models already score highly against instructor rubrics, and capability within one model family improves substantially over two years. These results provide strong evidence that AI performance on this class of work is already high and rapidly improving, with implications for business schools, where case pedagogy trains undergraduates and MBAs in this kind of analytical reasoning, and for entry-level professional roles, where such skills have historically anchored early-career work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前沿人工智能在各商业学科中的表现：基于案例的知识工作与分析推理基准
大型语言模型（LLMs）的性能正迅速提升，体现在基准分数上，但这些AI基准主要测试事实记忆、窄域问答、数学解题、编程及代理工具使用等能力。而尚未被充分衡量的是AI在分析性知识工作中（白领专业人士日常执行）的表现，包括综合复杂信息、在不确定和不完整信息下做出判断、在多利益相关者环境中运用战略与对抗性思维、权衡取舍、产出可辩护的结构化分析。此类工作中的主观部分，其成功标准更难界定，差距尤为明显。顶尖商学院采用的“案例教学法”为解决这一测量缺口提供了天然基础，我们构建了BusinessCaseBench基准，涵盖来自十八个学科的数百道题目，每题均配以从专家撰写的教师案例解答中提炼的评分标准。在BusinessCaseBench上，前沿AI模型已取得高分，且同一模型家族的能力在两年内显著提升。这些结果有力表明，AI在该类工作中的表现已处于高位并持续快速提升，对商学院（其案例教学法训练本科生和MBA进行此类分析推理）及初级职业角色（此类技能历来支撑早期职业生涯）具有重要影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in AI benchmarks that overlook analytical reasoning and judgment under uncertainty—core skills in white-collar knowledge work—the study constructs BusinessCaseBench, a case-grounded benchmark derived from business cases across eighteen disciplines, each paired with expert-derived grading rubrics. The method evaluates frontier AI models using these rubrics to assess their ability to synthesize complex information, apply strategic thinking, weigh trade-offs, and produce structured analyses. Experimental results show that current AI models already achieve high scores against instructor rubrics, with significant capability improvements observed within a single model family over two years, indicating rapid progress in handling real-world business reasoning tasks.
为填补当前AI基准测试对白领知识工作中分析推理能力（如信息综合、不确定性判断、多利益相关方博弈与权衡决策）的缺失，研究构建了BusinessCaseBench基准，其基于十八个学科的商业案例并配以专家评分标准。实验通过该基准评估前沿大语言模型，结果显示当前模型已能获得较高评分，且同一模型家族在两年内性能显著提升，表明AI在商业分析类任务上的能力已较高且进步迅速。
Multi-Modal Semantic Segmentation of Electrolyzer Components for Sustainable Hydrogen Technologies: A Dual-Branch Deep Learning Approach
Authors: Wasimul Karim, Nur Mohammad Fahad, Abdul Hasib Siddique, Md Rafiqul Islam, Hooman Mehdizadeh-Rad, Asif Karim, Sami Azam
First: 2026-07-17T15:34:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:34:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate segmentation of electrolyzer materials is essential for automated disassembly, sustainable recycling, and circular manufacturing in hydrogen technologies. However, this task is challenging due to strong visual similarity between materials, spectral overlap, irregular shapes, and severe class imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose an AI-driven dual-branch framework, Hyperspectral-RGB Electrolyzer Materials Network (HREM-Net), that combines hyperspectral imaging (HSI) and RGB images for electrolyzer material segmentation. We implemented several innovative modules, including Efficient Channel Attention, Coordinate Attention, Mobile Inverted Bottleneck blocks, and Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling to capture spectral and spatial features from HSI, and RGB images. With an adaptive gated cross-modal fusion module and composite loss function, HREM-Net achieves a mean class accuracy of 91.66% and a mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 0.82 on the Electrolyzers-HSI dataset, outperforming baseline segmentation models. Cross-dataset validation on the PCB-Vision dataset demonstrates strong generalization with 96.91% accuracy and 0.93 mIoU. This work poses its potential as an industrial application to improve electrolyzer efficiency, thereby improving the predictive maintenance of hydrogen production.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可持续氢能技术的电解槽组件多模态语义分割：一种双分支深度学习方法
准确分割电解槽材料对氢技术中的自动化拆解、可持续回收和循环制造至关重要。然而，由于材料间视觉相似性强、光谱重叠、形状不规则及类别不平衡，该任务极具挑战性。为此，我们提出了一种AI驱动的双分支框架——高光谱-RGB电解槽材料网络（HREM-Net），结合高光谱成像（HSI）与RGB图像进行电解槽材料分割。该框架包含多个创新模块，包括高效通道注意力、坐标注意力、移动倒置瓶颈块和空洞空间金字塔池化，以从HSI和RGB图像中提取光谱与空间特征。借助自适应门控跨模态融合模块和复合损失函数，HREM-Net在Electrolyzers-HSI数据集上实现91.66%的平均类别准确率和0.82的平均交并比（mIoU），优于基线分割模型。在PCB-Vision数据集上的跨数据集验证表明其具有强泛化能力，准确率达96.91%，mIoU为0.93。本研究展示了其在工业应用中的潜力，可提升电解槽效率，进而优化氢能生产的预测性维护。
Summary / 总结
To enable automated disassembly and recycling of electrolyzer components for sustainable hydrogen production, this study addresses the challenge of accurately segmenting visually similar and spectrally overlapping materials under class imbalance. The proposed HREM-Net framework integrates hyperspectral and RGB imagery through a dual-branch deep learning architecture, enhanced by modules like Efficient Channel Attention, Coordinate Attention, Mobile Inverted Bottleneck blocks, and Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling to extract rich spectral and spatial features. An adaptive gated cross-modal fusion and composite loss function further refine feature integration.
