为实现氢技术中电解槽组件的自动化拆解与循环利用，本研究针对材料视觉相似、光谱重叠及类别不平衡等挑战，提出融合高光谱与RGB图像的双分支深度学习框架HREM-Net。该框架通过高效通道注意力、坐标注意力、Mobile Inverted Bottleneck模块和空洞空间金字塔池化提取光谱与空间特征，并采用自适应门控跨模态融合与复合损失函数优化特征整合。在Electrolyzers-HSI数据集上，模型达到91.66%平均类别准确率与0.82 mIoU，优于基线模型；在PCB-Vision数据集上亦展现强泛化能力，准确率达96.91%，mIoU达0.93。
The Ebb and Flow of Multimodal Focus: Scheduling Visual Relay Windows for Grounded VLM Reasoning
Authors: Wencheng Ye, Yi Bin, Yujuan Ding, Hongye Fang, Zheng Wang, Xing Xu, Jingkuan Song, Yun Zhang, Sirui Da, Heng Tao Shen
First: 2026-07-13T11:44:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:34:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models increasingly succeed on multimodal reasoning benchmarks, yet their visual evidence often becomes unstable once it enters the language stack, weakening evidence-grounded reasoning. To understand this fragility, we examine the internal dynamics of VLMs through a mechanistic lens and uncover a stable three-stage redistribution of multimodal attention focus across depth: an early question-conditioned organization, a critical middle visual-dominant relay, and a late return to answer formation. We operationalize the middle phase as the Visual Relay Window (VRW), and show that its geometry varies with task demand, is causally tied to grounded generation, and distinguishes unsupported answers from stronger reasoning trajectories. Guided by this internal rhythm, we propose TRACE, a task-adaptive inference-time control framework with lightweight trained modules. It reshapes relay allocation during prefill and preserves assembled visual support after handoff during decoding. Across four open-weight VLM backbones and seven benchmarks, TRACE delivers large gains on grounding-sensitive settings, improving them by 4.33 points on average and by up to 6.6 points, while also improving reasoning-heavy tasks. These results show that explicitly controlling multimodal focus across depth offers a unified and effective mechanism for strengthening evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态注意力的涨落：为视觉语言模型的锚定推理调度视觉中继窗口
视觉-语言模型在多模态推理基准上日益取得成功，但其视觉证据一旦进入语言栈便常不稳定，削弱了证据锚定推理能力。为理解这种脆弱性，我们从机制视角考察VLM内部动态，发现多模态注意力在深度上存在稳定三阶段再分配：早期基于问题的组织、关键中间视觉主导中继阶段，以及后期回归答案生成。我们将中间阶段定义为视觉中继窗口（VRW），并证明其几何形态随任务需求变化，与锚定生成存在因果关联，且能区分无支撑答案与更强推理轨迹。基于此内部节奏，我们提出TRACE框架——一种轻量级训练模块构成的任务自适应推理时控制框架。它在预填充阶段重塑中继分配，并在解码阶段保留移交后的视觉支持。在四个开源VLM主干网络和七个基准测试中，TRACE在对锚定敏感的设置上平均提升4.33分，最高达6.6分，同时亦提升推理密集型任务表现。结果表明，显式控制深度上的多模态注意力焦点，可提供统一且有效的机制以强化证据锚定的多模态推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of visual evidence in vision-language models during language processing, this work investigates the internal attention dynamics across model depth and identifies a three-stage attention pattern: early question-conditioned organization, a visually dominant middle relay phase termed Visual Relay Window (VRW), and late answer formation. The VRW’s geometry adapts to task demands and correlates with grounded reasoning quality, distinguishing supported from unsupported outputs. The authors propose TRACE, a lightweight inference-time control framework that dynamically allocates visual attention during prefill and preserves visual support during decoding. Evaluated across four open-weight VLMs and seven benchmarks, TRACE consistently improves grounding-sensitive tasks by an average of 4.33 points and up to 6.6 points, while also enhancing reasoning-heavy tasks, demonstrating that explicit control of multimodal focus across depth significantly boosts evidence-grounded reasoning.
为了解决视觉语言模型在语言处理过程中视觉证据不稳定的问题，该研究从机制角度分析模型内部注意力动态，发现注意力在深度上呈现三阶段分布：早期基于问题的组织阶段、关键的视觉主导中继阶段（称为视觉中继窗口VRW）和后期答案生成阶段。VRW的几何结构随任务需求变化，与生成是否接地强相关，能区分支持与不支持的推理路径。研究提出TRACE框架，通过轻量级训练模块在推理时动态调整VRW分配并保留视觉支持。在四个开源VLM模型和七个基准测试中，TRACE在接地敏感任务上平均提升4.33分，最高达6.6分，同时增强推理密集型任务，证明跨深度显式控制多模态注意力可有效提升证据驱动的多模态推理能力。
Deep and Probabilistic Models for Gene Regulatory Network Inference
Authors: Claudia Skok Gibbs
First: 2026-07-17T15:31:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:31:05+00:00
Comments: PhD thesis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gene regulatory networks (GRNs) link transcription factor (TF) proteins to their target genes, yet reconstructing these networks from genome-wide data remains challenging under practical and methodological constraints. Many methods couple modeling assumptions to a specific inference procedure and rely on heuristic model selection, while evaluation is constrained by incomplete reference networks and point-estimate outputs that lack uncertainty. GRN reconstruction also depends on prior knowledge to constrain TF-gene interactions, yet available priors are often assay-dependent and difficult to transfer across species and less-characterized systems. In this thesis, we develop two complementary frameworks that address these limitations. In the first, PMF-GRN casts GRN inference as a probabilistic graphical model optimized by variational inference, enabling principled model selection and uncertainty-aware edge estimates. In the second, GLM-Prior addresses the prior bottleneck by fine-tuning the pretrained Nucleotide Transformer to predict TF-target gene interactions directly from nucleotide sequence, while generalizing across yeast, mouse, and human settings. Together, PMF-GRN and GLM-Prior motivate a dual-stage view of GRN reconstruction in which sequence-derived priors provide a transferable starting scaffold and probabilistic inference refines regulatory estimates with quantified uncertainty under incomplete evaluation resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于基因调控网络推断的深度与概率模型
基因调控网络（GRN）将转录因子（TF）蛋白与其靶基因相连，但受限于实际和方法学约束，从全基因组数据重建这些网络仍具挑战。许多方法将建模假设与特定推断程序耦合，并依赖启发式模型选择，而评估受限于不完整的参考网络和缺乏不确定性的点估计输出。GRN重建还依赖先验知识以约束TF-基因相互作用，但现有先验常依赖特定检测方法，难以跨物种或在特征较少的系统中迁移。本论文开发了两个互补框架以解决这些局限。首先，PMF-GRN将GRN推断建模为概率图模型，通过变分推断优化，实现原则性的模型选择和不确定性感知的边估计。其次，GLM-Prior通过微调预训练的核苷酸Transformer，直接从核苷酸序列预测TF-靶基因相互作用，同时在酵母、小鼠和人类系统中实现泛化。二者共同提出GRN重建的双阶段视角：序列衍生先验提供可迁移的初始框架，概率推断则在评估资源不完整的情况下，量化不确定性并优化调控估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges in reconstructing gene regulatory networks from genome-wide data—such as reliance on heuristic methods, lack of uncertainty quantification, and limited transferable priors—the thesis introduces two complementary frameworks. PMF-GRN frames GRN inference as a probabilistic graphical model optimized via variational inference, enabling principled model selection and uncertainty-aware edge predictions. GLM-Prior overcomes prior limitations by fine-tuning a pretrained Nucleotide Transformer to predict TF-gene interactions directly from sequence, generalizing across yeast, mouse, and human systems. Together, these methods support a dual-stage approach: sequence-based priors provide a transferable scaffold, while probabilistic inference refines estimates under incomplete data and quantifies uncertainty.
为应对从全基因组数据重建基因调控网络时面临的挑战——如依赖启发式方法、缺乏不确定性量化、先验知识难以跨物种迁移——本研究提出两个互补框架：PMF-GRN将网络推断建模为通过变分推断优化的概率图模型，支持原则性模型选择与不确定性感知的边估计；GLM-Prior则通过微调核苷酸变换器，直接从序列预测转录因子-靶基因相互作用，并在酵母、小鼠和人类系统中实现泛化。二者结合形成双阶段方法：序列先验提供可迁移的初始框架，概率推断在评估资源有限条件下量化不确定性并优化调控预测，从而提升网络重建的稳健性与可解释性。
Loop the Loopies!
Authors: Zitian Gao, Yilong Chen, Yihao Xiao, Xinyu Yang, Ran Tao, Joey Zhou, Bryan Dai
First: 2026-07-17T15:28:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:28:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Loopie, the most powerful looped Transformer to date. The Loopie series consists of two Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: a 20B-parameter model with 2B active parameters and a 6Bparameter model with 0.6B active parameters. Looped Transformers have long faced a challenge: given an N-fold increase in pre-training compute, increasing the parameter count by a factor of N usually outperforms looping a model N times. Loopie addresses this challenge. Extensive ablation studies, including comparisons with a vanilla 30B-A3B model, show that Loopie substantially outperforms vanilla Transformer baselines trained with the same compute budget. Our novel post-training pipeline equips Loopie with strong reasoning abilities. At the 2025 IMO and IPhO, Loopie achieves gold-medal performance without tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环循环体！
我们推出了Loopie，迄今为止最强大的循环Transformer。Loopie系列包含两个专家混合（MoE）模型：一个200亿参数模型，其中20亿参数活跃；一个60亿参数模型，其中6亿参数活跃。循环Transformer长期以来面临一个挑战：在预训练计算量增加N倍的情况下，通常增加参数量N倍的效果优于循环模型N次。Loopie解决了这一挑战。广泛的消融研究，包括与30B-A3B基础模型的对比，表明Loopie在相同计算预算下显著优于常规Transformer基线。我们新颖的后训练流程赋予Loopie强大的推理能力。在2025年IMO和IPhO竞赛中，Loopie无需工具即取得金牌表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional looping techniques in Transformers, where scaling parameters outperforms looping despite increased compute, the authors introduce Loopie, a novel looped MoE architecture with two variants (20B and 6B parameter models, activating 2B and 0.6B parameters respectively). Loopie employs a post-training pipeline to enhance reasoning capabilities, and experiments show it significantly outperforms vanilla Transformer baselines trained under identical compute budgets, achieving gold-medal results on the 2025 IMO and IPhO without external tools.
为解决循环Transformer在计算资源增加时性能提升不足的问题，作者提出Loopie，一种新型循环MoE架构，包含20B和6B参数版本（分别激活2B和0.6B参数）。与传统循环方法不同，Loopie通过后训练流程增强推理能力，在同等计算预算下显著优于30B基础Transformer基线模型，并在2025年IMO和IPhO竞赛中无需工具即斩获金牌。
DELUGE: Towards Continental-Scale Daily Pluvial Flood Damage Prediction via Interpretable Conditioning on Foundation Model Embeddings
Authors: Yuya Kawakami, Daniel Cayan, Dongyu Liu, Kwan-Liu Ma, Tom Corringham
First: 2026-07-17T15:27:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:27:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pluvial (rainfall-driven) flooding accounts for 45% of National Flood Insurance Program (NFIP) claims in the United States and is harder to predict than its riverine and coastal counterparts, with existing approaches limited to coarse resolution, regional domains, or computationally intensive process-based models unsuitable for daily continental-scale use. We present DELUGE, a multimodal deep learning framework for daily pluvial flood damage prediction at ~1 km resolution and national scale, trained on spatially and temporally corrected NFIP claims (2017-2022) and structured around the hazard, exposure, and vulnerability components of disaster risk. Rather than blanket coverage of the Conterminous United States (CONUS), we model the top 100 highest-claim 75 km cells, distributed nationwide and accounting for ~81% of total pluvial flood claims. Our architectural novelty is a pair of parametric modules in the hydrometeorology branch, a Value Modulator and a Temporal Modulator, conditioned on terrain descriptors and AlphaEarth foundation-model embeddings, that expose directly inspectable hydrological response parameters and provide architecture-level interpretability-by-design. Under a spatial block holdout, DELUGE outperforms tuned Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM baselines by 9% to 30% on a dollar-weighted area under the precision-recall curve (PR-AUC), a metric that emphasizes the rare, high-cost claims of greatest operational interest. Beyond DELUGE, we argue this interpretable conditioning scheme is a transferable pattern for integrating foundation-model embeddings into other geospatial prediction tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DELUGE：通过可解释的基座模型嵌入条件化，面向大陆尺度每日城市内涝灾害损失预测
城市内涝（降雨驱动）洪水占美国国家洪水保险计划（NFIP）索赔的45%，其预测难度高于河流型和沿海型洪水，现有方法受限于粗分辨率、区域范围或计算密集型的过程模型，难以满足每日大陆尺度应用需求。我们提出DELUGE，一种多模态深度学习框架，可在约1公里分辨率和全国尺度上预测每日城市内涝灾害损失，训练数据为经空间与时间校正的NFIP索赔数据（2017-2022），结构围绕灾害风险的致灾因子、暴露因子与脆弱性三要素。我们未覆盖整个美国本土（CONUS），而是聚焦全国范围内索赔最高的100个75公里单元格，覆盖约81%的总内涝索赔。架构创新在于水文气象分支中的两个参数化模块——价值调节器与时间调节器，其条件化于地形描述符与AlphaEarth基座模型嵌入，可直接暴露可检视的水文响应参数，并在架构层面实现可解释性设计。在空间区块留出验证下，DELUGE在加权精度-召回率曲线下面积（PR-AUC）指标上，相较调优后的随机森林、XGBoost与LightGBM基线模型提升9%至30%，该指标强调对运营最关注的罕见高成本索赔。除DELUGE外，我们认为该可解释条件化方案可作为将基座模型嵌入整合至其他地理空间预测任务的可迁移范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting daily pluvial flood damage across the continental U.S. with high resolution and interpretability, DELUGE employs a multimodal deep learning framework trained on NFIP claims data (2017–2022) and structured around hazard, exposure, and vulnerability. It focuses on the top 100 high-claim 75 km cells, covering ~81% of total claims, and introduces a Value Modulator and Temporal Modulator in its hydrometeorology branch, conditioned on terrain and AlphaEarth embeddings, to enable direct inspection of hydrological parameters. Experiments show DELUGE outperforms Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM by 9% to 30% on PR-AUC, a metric prioritizing rare high-cost claims, demonstrating both accuracy and architectural interpretability.
为解决美国大陆尺度每日城市内涝灾害损失预测的精度与可解释性难题，DELUGE采用多模态深度学习框架，基于2017–2022年修正后的NFIP索赔数据，围绕灾害风险的致灾、暴露与脆弱性三要素构建。模型聚焦全国前100个高索赔75公里单元，覆盖约81%总索赔额，并引入基于地形与AlphaEarth嵌入条件化的参数模块（价值调节器与时间调节器），以输出可解释的水文响应参数。实验表明，DELUGE在PR-AUC指标上较随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM分别提升9%至30%，显著优于传统方法，同时具备架构级可解释性。
Latency-Response Theory Model: Evaluating Large Language Models via Response Accuracy and Chain-of-Thought Length
Authors: Zhiyu Xu, Jia Liu, Yixin Wang, Yuqi Gu
First: 2025-12-07T22:06:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:19:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) necessitates valid evaluation methods to provide guidance for both downstream applications and actionable future improvements. The Item Response Theory (IRT) model with Computerized Adaptive Testing has recently emerged as a promising framework for evaluating LLMs via their response accuracy. Beyond simple response accuracy, LLMs' chain of thought (CoT) lengths serve as a vital indicator of their reasoning ability. To leverage the CoT length information to assist LLM evaluation, we propose the \textbf{La}tency-\textbf{R}esponse \textbf{T}heory (LaRT) model, which jointly models both the response accuracy and CoT length by introducing a key correlation parameter between the latent ability and the latent speed. We derive an efficient stochastic approximation Expectation-Maximization algorithm for parameter estimation. We establish rigorous identifiability results for the latent ability and latent speed parameters to ensure the statistical validity of their estimation. Through both theoretical asymptotic analyses and simulation studies, we demonstrate LaRT's advantages over IRT in terms of superior estimation accuracy and shorter confidence intervals for latent trait estimation. To evaluate LaRT in real data, we collect responses from diverse LLMs on popular benchmark datasets. We find that LaRT yields different LLM rankings than IRT and outperforms IRT across multiple key evaluation metrics including predictive power, item efficiency, ranking validity, and LLM evaluation efficiency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Toby-X/Latency-Response-Theory-Model
中文标题/摘要
标题：延迟-反应理论模型：通过反应准确率与思维链长度评估大语言模型
大语言模型（LLMs）的广泛应用亟需有效的评估方法，以指导下游应用并推动未来改进。项目反应理论（IRT）结合计算机自适应测试，近期成为通过反应准确率评估LLMs的有前景框架。除反应准确率外，LLMs的思维链（CoT）长度是其推理能力的重要指标。为利用CoT长度信息辅助LLMs评估，我们提出**延迟-反应理论（LaRT）**模型，通过引入潜在能力与潜在速度之间的关键相关参数，联合建模反应准确率与CoT长度。我们推导出一种高效的随机近似期望最大化算法用于参数估计，并建立潜在能力与潜在速度参数的严格可识别性结果，确保其统计有效性。通过理论渐近分析与模拟研究，我们证明LaRT在估计精度和潜在特质置信区间长度方面优于IRT。为在真实数据中评估LaRT，我们收集了多种LLMs在流行基准数据集上的响应结果。发现LaRT生成的LLMs排序与IRT不同，并在预测能力、题目效率、排序有效性及LLMs评估效率等多个关键评估指标上优于IRT。代码与数据见https://github.com/Toby-X/Latency-Response-Theory-Model
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs), this paper introduces the Latency-Response Theory (LaRT) model, which jointly models response accuracy and chain-of-thought (CoT) length by incorporating a correlation parameter between latent ability and latent speed. The authors derive a stochastic approximation Expectation-Maximization algorithm for parameter estimation and establish theoretical identifiability guarantees for the latent parameters. Experimental results show LaRT outperforms traditional Item Response Theory (IRT) in estimation accuracy, confidence interval width, and evaluation efficiency, yielding different LLM rankings and superior performance across multiple metrics including predictive power and item efficiency.
为应对大语言模型（LLM）评估需求，本文提出Latency-Response Theory（LaRT）模型，联合建模回答准确率与思维链长度，引入能力与速度的潜在相关参数。通过随机逼近EM算法进行参数估计，并提供理论可识别性保证。实验表明，LaRT在估计精度、置信区间宽度、预测能力等关键指标上优于传统IRT模型，且能生成不同模型排序，提升评估效率。
A Systematic Study of Large Language Models for Task and Motion Planning With PDDLStream
Authors: Jorge Mendez-Mendez
Venue: IROS
First: 2025-09-30T19:03:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:18:48+00:00
Comments: 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS). 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While we know that large language models (LLMs) can solve some planning problems, we do not understand the extent of these capabilities for robotics. One promising direction is to integrate the semantic knowledge of LLMs with the formal reasoning of task and motion planning (TAMP). However, designing such systems is complicated by the myriad of choices for how to integrate LLMs within TAMP. We develop 16 algorithms that use LLMs to substitute key TAMP components. Our zero-shot experiments across 13750 evaluations and three domains reveal that LLM-based planners exhibit lower success rates and higher planning times than engineered systems. Providing geometric details increases the number of task-planning errors compared to pure PDDL descriptions, and (faster) direct LLM variants outperform (slower) reasoning variants in most cases. Code and results are available at https://github.com/jorge-a-mendez/llm-pddlstream.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于PDDLStream的大语言模型在任务与运动规划中的系统研究
尽管我们知道大语言模型（LLMs）可以解决部分规划问题，但我们尚不清楚其在机器人领域的适用范围。一个有前景的方向是将LLMs的语义知识与任务与运动规划（TAMP）的形式化推理相结合。然而，如何在TAMP中集成LLMs存在多种选择，设计此类系统较为复杂。我们开发了16种算法，利用LLMs替代TAMP中的关键组件。在13750次评估和三个领域中的零样本实验表明，基于LLMs的规划器成功率较低、规划时间较长。提供几何细节反而比纯PDDL描述导致更多任务规划错误，且（更快的）直接LLM变体在大多数情况下优于（较慢的）推理变体。代码和结果见https://github.com/jorge-a-mendez/llm-pddlstream。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of large language models (LLMs) to augment robotics planning, this study systematically explores how LLMs can replace core components of task and motion planning (TAMP) systems. The authors design 16 distinct algorithms integrating LLMs into PDDLStream, testing them across 13,750 evaluations in three domains. Results show that LLM-based planners consistently underperform engineered systems in success rate and planning efficiency. Including geometric details in prompts worsens task-planning accuracy, while faster, direct LLM approaches outperform slower, reasoning-based variants in most cases.
为探索大语言模型（LLM）在机器人任务与运动规划中的潜力，本研究系统评估了16种替换PDDLStream框架关键组件的LLM算法。在三个领域共13750次实验中，LLM规划器整体表现劣于工程化系统，成功率更低、规划耗时更长。添加几何细节反而增加任务规划错误率，而直接快速的LLM变体在多数情况下优于需推理的慢速变体。
WeEdit: A Dataset, Benchmark and Glyph-Guided Framework for Text-centric Image Editing
Authors: Hui Zhang, Juntao Liu, Zongkai Liu, Liqiang Niu, Fandong Meng, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2026-03-12T06:25:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:14:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instruction-based image editing aims to modify specific content within existing images according to user-provided instructions while preserving non-target regions. Beyond traditional object- and style-centric manipulation, text-centric image editing focuses on modifying, translating, or rearranging textual elements embedded within images. However, existing leading models often struggle to execute complex text editing precisely, frequently producing blurry or hallucinated characters. We attribute these failures primarily to the lack of specialized training paradigms tailored for text-centric editing, as well as the absence of large-scale datasets and standardized benchmarks necessary for a closed-loop training and evaluation system. To address these limitations, we present WeEdit, a systematic solution encompassing a scalable data construction pipeline, two benchmarks, and a tailored two-stage training strategy. Specifically, we propose a novel HTML-based automatic editing pipeline, which generates 330K training pairs covering diverse editing operations and 15 languages, accompanied by standardized bilingual and multilingual benchmarks for comprehensive evaluation. On the algorithmic side, we employ glyph-guided supervised fine-tuning to inject explicit spatial and content priors, followed by a multi-objective reinforcement learning stage to align generation with instruction adherence, text clarity, and background preservation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WeEdit outperforms previous open-source models by a clear margin across diverse editing operations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WeEdit：面向文本中心图像编辑的数据集、基准与字形引导框架
基于指令的图像编辑旨在根据用户提供的指令修改现有图像中的特定内容，同时保留非目标区域。相较于传统的对象和风格中心编辑，文本中心图像编辑聚焦于修改、翻译或重新排列图像中嵌入的文本元素。然而，现有主流模型常难以精确执行复杂的文本编辑，常产生模糊或幻觉字符。我们归因于缺乏针对文本中心编辑的专用训练范式，以及缺乏大规模数据集和标准化基准，以支持闭环训练与评估系统。为解决上述问题，我们提出WeEdit，一个系统性解决方案，包含可扩展的数据构建流水线、两个基准和定制的两阶段训练策略。具体而言，我们提出一种基于HTML的自动编辑流水线，生成涵盖多样编辑操作和15种语言的33万训练对，并配套标准化的双语及多语基准以全面评估。算法层面，我们采用字形引导的监督微调以注入显式空间与内容先验，随后通过多目标强化学习阶段对生成结果进行优化，使其符合指令遵循、文本清晰度与背景保留的要求。大量实验表明，WeEdit在各类编辑操作中显著优于先前开源模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of existing models in precise text-centric image editing, which often produce blurry or hallucinated characters, the authors introduce WeEdit, a comprehensive solution comprising a scalable data pipeline, two benchmarks, and a glyph-guided training framework. They construct a 330K training dataset using an HTML-based automatic editing pipeline covering 15 languages and diverse editing tasks, and establish standardized bilingual and multilingual benchmarks for evaluation. Their method employs glyph-guided supervised fine-tuning to encode spatial and content priors, followed by multi-objective reinforcement learning to optimize instruction adherence, text clarity, and background preservation. Experimental results show WeEdit significantly outperforms prior open-source models across multiple editing tasks.
为解决现有模型在文本图像编辑中常出现模糊或幻觉字符的问题，作者提出了WeEdit，包含可扩展的数据构建流程、两个基准和基于字形引导的训练框架。方法采用HTML自动化流程生成33万条多语言训练对及标准化基准，再通过字形引导监督微调注入空间与内容先验，继而使用多目标强化学习优化指令遵循、文字清晰度与背景保留。实验表明，WeEdit在各类文本编辑任务上显著优于现有开源模型。
SciForge: An AI-Native, Multimodal Workbench for Scientific Discovery
Authors: SciForge Team, Zhangyang Gao, Minghao Fang, Yifei Liu, Hanhui Yang, Xinyu Gu, Shixiang Tang, Siqi Sun, Lei Bai, Cheng Tan, Mengdi Liu, Hao Wu, Shuizhou Chen
First: 2026-07-17T15:13:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:13:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scientific work increasingly spans heterogeneous artifacts -- papers, code, datasets, scientific file formats, model outputs, figures, manuscripts, and team decisions -- yet general-purpose AI assistants rarely preserve these objects as a coherent, auditable research state. We present SciForge, a multimodal research-native AI workbench that reserves the graphical interface for human judgment while search, parsing, model routing, workflow execution, plotting, writing, and presentation generation run as modular agent-accessible services. SciForge is built around five pillars: (i) \emph{goal-scoped scientific decision governance} for \textbf{goal-oriented} research, with review gates and shared review surfaces; (ii) \emph{translate-then-reason} for \textbf{multimodal} input, routing scientific objects through domain translators before the agent reasons; (iii) \emph{evidence governance} for \textbf{auditable} traceability, linking claims to provenance chains and audit findings; (iv) \emph{collaborative team science} for \textbf{collaborative} research, enabling multi-role decision governance, with shared team workspaces planned for future releases; and (v) \emph{real-world application scenarios} for \textbf{practical} impact, demonstrated through eight end-to-end user cases, with flagship demonstrations including multi-day agentic research sprints for gene discovery, AI-guided de novo protein design, molecular optimization, and genome-to-BGC discovery. The system combines a thin interaction layer, contextual research capability patterns, an Agent Runtime and Workflow Engine, an Evidence-DAG audit sidecar and a Scientific Model Router. SciForge currently runs as a desktop application, with mobile supervision support; future releases will deepen team collaboration. The system is open-source and available at https://github.com/AGI4Sci/SciForge
中文标题/摘要
标题：SciForge：面向科学发现的AI原生多模态工作台
科学研究日益涉及异构成果——论文、代码、数据集、科学文件格式、模型输出、图表、手稿和团队决策——但通用AI助手很少将这些对象保存为连贯且可审计的研究状态。我们提出SciForge，一个面向科研的多模态AI工作台，保留图形界面供人类判断，而搜索、解析、模型路由、工作流执行、绘图、写作和演示生成则作为模块化、可被代理访问的服务运行。SciForge基于五大支柱构建：(i) 面向目标的科学决策治理，配备评审关卡与共享评审界面；(ii) 转换后推理，用于多模态输入，先通过领域翻译器路由科学对象再由代理推理；(iii) 证据治理，确保可审计追溯性，将主张链接至溯源链与审计结果；(iv) 协作式团队科学，支持协作研究，提供多角色决策治理，未来版本将推出共享团队工作区；(v) 实际应用场景，通过八个端到端用户案例展示实际影响，包括基因发现的多日代理研究冲刺、AI引导的从头蛋白质设计、分子优化及基因组到BGC发现。系统结合轻量交互层、上下文研究能力模式、代理运行时与工作流引擎、证据DAG审计侧边车及科学模型路由器。当前SciForge以桌面应用形式运行，支持移动端监督；未来版本将深化团队协作。系统开源，详见https://github.com/AGI4Sci/SciForge
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve heterogeneous scientific artifacts as coherent, auditable research states, SciForge introduces an AI-native workbench that separates human judgment from automated tasks like search, parsing, and workflow execution. Built on five pillars—goal-scoped decision governance, multimodal translation, evidence traceability, team collaboration, and real-world applications—the system routes scientific objects through domain-specific translators, executes modular agent services, and maintains provenance chains for auditability. Evaluated through eight end-to-end user cases, including gene discovery and protein design, SciForge demonstrated effective support for multi-day agentic research sprints and complex scientific workflows, with current deployment as a desktop app and future plans for mobile supervision and team collaboration.
为解决科学工作中异构研究材料难以保持连贯可追溯状态的问题，SciForge提出一个AI原生的多模态研究工作台，将人类判断与自动化任务（如搜索、解析、工作流执行）分离。系统基于五大支柱构建：目标导向的决策治理、输入前的多模态翻译、可追溯的证据治理、团队协作支持及实际应用场景，通过模块化可访问服务实现端到端研究自动化。实验结果在八个用例中验证其有效性，包括多日代理研究冲刺（如基因发现、从头蛋白设计、分子优化及基因组到生物合成基因簇发现），展示其可扩展、可审计且支持协作的科研工作流能力。
Attention to Detail: Evaluating Energy, Performance, and Accuracy Trade-offs Across vLLM Configurations
Authors: Nada Zine, Tristan Coignion, Vincenzo Stoico, Clément Quinton, Ivano Malavolta, Romain Rouvoy, Patricia Lago
First: 2026-07-10T08:04:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:11:48+00:00
Comments: Submitted at a conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models are reshaping how software is developed and maintained. They are typically deployed in production using inference engines such as vLLM, which can efficiently serve pre-trained, highly configurable models. While prior work has focused on model architectures and hardware acceleration, the impact of inference engine configuration on energy consumption, performance, and output quality remains poorly understood. In this paper, we present a large-scale controlled study of three selected vLLM configuration options: attention kernel type, prefix caching, and chunked prefill. We evaluate all combinations of these configurations across 5 open-weight LLMs and 5 diverse inference tasks, totaling $9,000$ runs and $93,600$ measures. We analyze energy consumption, latency, and accuracy, and examine both main effects and interaction effects between configuration options and tasks. Our results show that the studied configuration options significantly impact energy and performance, mainly driven by attention type and prefix caching, while chunked prefill has a limited effect under the default vLLM serving configuration and evaluated workloads. These effects are highly model- and workload-dependent, and no configuration is universally optimal. We further show that model choice dominates global trade-offs, while configuration tuning provides local improvements along the Pareto frontier. Unexpectedly, inference options can also affect model accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关注细节：评估vLLM配置在能耗、性能与准确率之间的权衡
大型语言模型正在重塑软件的开发与维护方式。它们通常在生产环境中通过推理引擎（如vLLM）部署，以高效服务预训练且高度可配置的模型。尽管先前研究聚焦于模型架构和硬件加速，但推理引擎配置对能耗、性能和输出质量的影响仍不甚明了。 本文对三种选定的vLLM配置选项——注意力核类型、前缀缓存和分块预填充——进行了大规模控制实验。我们在5个开源大语言模型和5个多样化推理任务上评估了所有配置组合，总计9,000次运行和93,600个测量值。我们分析了能耗、延迟和准确率，并考察了配置选项与任务之间的主效应和交互效应。 结果表明，所研究的配置选项显著影响能耗和性能，主要由注意力类型和前缀缓存驱动，而分块预填充在默认vLLM服务配置和评估负载下影响有限。这些效应高度依赖模型和工作负载，不存在普适最优配置。我们进一步表明，模型选择主导全局权衡，而配置调优可在帕累托前沿提供局部改进。出人意料的是，推理选项本身也可能影响模型准确率。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how configuration choices in the vLLM inference engine affect energy, performance, and accuracy in large language models. Motivated by the lack of understanding regarding inference engine tuning, the authors conduct a controlled experiment across three key configurations—attention kernel type, prefix caching, and chunked prefill—evaluating 9,000 runs across five models and five tasks. Results show that attention type and prefix caching significantly influence energy and latency, while chunked prefill has minimal impact under default settings. The effects vary by model and task, indicating no universal optimal configuration; instead, model selection dominates global trade-offs, while tuning offers localized improvements along the Pareto frontier. Surprisingly, inference settings also influence output accuracy.
该研究探讨了vLLM推理引擎配置对大语言模型部署中能耗、性能和准确率的影响。由于此前对推理引擎调优缺乏系统理解，作者系统评估了三种关键配置——注意力核类型、前缀缓存和分块预填充——在五个开源模型和五个多样化任务中的组合效果，共完成9000次运行。结果表明，注意力核类型和前缀缓存显著影响能耗与延迟，而分块预填充在默认设置下影响有限。这些效果高度依赖模型与任务，不存在普适最优配置；模型选择主导全局权衡，而配置调优可在帕累托前沿提供局部改进。出人意料的是，部分推理选项也会影响输出准确率。
Revisiting data-driven dynamic security assessment with a tabular foundation model
Authors: Olayiwola Arowolo, Maosheng Yang, Jochen Cremer
First: 2026-07-17T15:06:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:06:11+00:00
Comments: Paper is in the review process
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data-driven pre-fault dynamic security assessment (DSA) rapidly evaluates the dynamic risk of credible contingencies on a power system using machine learning. Existing approaches face two limitations. First, they require a large labelled database for training, with a separate model trained, tuned, and maintained for each contingency in a potentially long list of credible contingencies. Second, the trained models generalize poorly to unseen contingencies. This work addresses the limitations by using a tabular foundation model (TFM) that assesses stability through in-context learning, requiring no retraining or hyperparameter optimization. A single TFM can assess many contingencies at once, removing the need for one model per classifier. We also characterize when the use of electrical distance coordinates (EDC) as continuous features enables generalization of TFM to unseen contingencies and when they do not, demonstrating how a few labelled samples can reliably improve generalization. Through comprehensive case studies on the IEEE 68-bus system, we show that a single TFM attains an average Macro F1 score of about 90% with only 120 labelled samples per contingency, roughly two orders of magnitude fewer than conventionally assumed, without any model retraining or hyperparameter tuning. For new/unseen contingencies, we show that using just 10 labelled samples of the new contingency with EDC encoding matches the best achievable transfer learning oracle model, which requires fully labelled data and is not deployable in practice. Overall, this initial study paves the way towards developing and deploying foundation models for power system operations, with possible applications across multiple operational tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于表格基础模型的数据驱动动态安全评估重访
数据驱动的预故障动态安全评估（DSA）利用机器学习快速评估电力系统中可信故障情景的动态风险。现有方法存在两个局限：一是需要大量标注数据集进行训练，且需为每个可能的故障情景单独训练、调优和维护模型；二是训练好的模型难以泛化到未见情景。本文通过使用表格基础模型（TFM），利用上下文学习评估稳定性，无需重新训练或超参数优化。单个TFM可同时评估多个故障情景，无需为每个分类器单独建模。我们还分析了在何种情况下将电气距离坐标（EDC）作为连续特征可使TFM泛化至未见情景，以及在何种情况下不能，展示了少量标注样本如何可靠提升泛化能力。通过在IEEE 68节点系统上的全面案例研究，我们表明单个TFM仅需每个故障情景120个标注样本，即可达到约90%的平均Macro F1分数，约为传统方法所需样本量的两个数量级，且无需任何模型再训练或超参数调优。对于新/未见故障情景，我们证明仅使用10个新情景的EDC编码标注样本，其性能可媲美需要全标注数据且在实践中不可部署的最佳迁移学习oracle模型。总体而言，本初步研究为开发和部署电力系统操作的基础模型铺平了道路，可能应用于多种操作任务。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the inefficiencies in data-driven dynamic security assessment by proposing a tabular foundation model that eliminates the need for separate models per contingency and avoids retraining. Motivated by the high resource cost and poor generalization of existing ML approaches, the method leverages in-context learning with electrical distance coordinates to enable a single model to assess multiple contingencies. Experimental results on the IEEE 68-bus system demonstrate that the model achieves 90% average Macro F1 score using only 120 labeled samples per contingency—two orders of magnitude fewer than typical requirements—and generalizes effectively to unseen contingencies with as few as 10 labeled samples, matching the performance of impractical transfer learning baselines.
该研究针对现有数据驱动动态安全评估方法存在的两大问题：需大量标注数据且需为每个故障情景单独训练模型，导致泛化能力差。提出一种基于表格的基座模型（TFM），通过上下文学习一次性评估多个故障情景，无需再训练或调参。在IEEE 68节点系统上的实验表明，单个TFM仅用每情景120个标注样本即可达到90%的平均Macro F1分数，比传统方法少两个数量级；且当使用电气距离坐标时，仅需10个新情景标注样本即可媲美全标注的迁移学习最优模型，展现出优异的泛化能力和实用部署潜力。
The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Amir Esterhuysen, Anders Jonsson
First: 2026-05-29T13:24:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:05:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation learning is a powerful tool for spatio-temporal abstraction within reinforcement learning (RL). Two well established approaches are through the successor representation (SR) and the default representation (DR). The SR encodes states by the future trajectories they induce, capturing information flow decoupled from reward. The DR builds on this by weighting trajectories with reward, integrating credit-assignment structure into the representation. Eigenvectors of both representations have been used to support a range of downstream tasks -- including option discovery, reward shaping, transfer learning, and exploration. We introduce a structurally distinct formulation: the terminal representation (TR). The TR encodes reward-weighted trajectories similarly to the DR, but can be learned as a lower-dimensionality object, and can be used directly for the mentioned applications without eigenvector computations. Eigendecomposition also imposes the assumption of symmetric transition dynamics, which the TR can bypass. In this work we develop the theoretical foundations of the TR: its derivation, convergence of two learning algorithms, its use for zero-shot compositionality, and equivalences between alternative reward formulations. We further show the TR is embedded in the top DR eigenvector, allowing it to capture the same underlying knowledge without eigendecomposition. Additionally, we provide empirical evidence of the TR as a viable alternative to existing representations in subsidiary applications, while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习中的终端表示
表示学习是强化学习（RL）中进行时空抽象的强大工具。两种成熟的方法是后继表示（SR）和默认表示（DR）。SR通过状态诱导的未来轨迹编码状态，捕捉与奖励无关的信息流；DR在此基础上对轨迹加权奖励，将责任分配结构整合进表示中。两种表示的特征向量已被用于支持多种下游任务，包括选项发现、奖励塑造、迁移学习和探索。本文引入一种结构上不同的表示：终端表示（TR）。TR与DR类似地编码奖励加权轨迹，但可作为低维对象学习，并可直接用于前述应用，无需特征向量计算。特征分解还隐含对称转移动态的假设，而TR可绕过该假设。本文构建了TR的理论基础：其推导、两种学习算法的收敛性、其在零样本组合性中的应用，以及不同奖励形式间的等价性。此外，我们证明TR嵌入于DR的最高特征向量中，使其无需特征分解即可捕捉相同底层知识。同时，我们提供了TR在辅助应用中作为现有表示可行替代方案的实证证据，且其学习、存储和使用所需的计算开销更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and symmetry assumptions of existing RL representations like successor and default representations, this work introduces the terminal representation (TR), a structurally distinct formulation that encodes reward-weighted trajectories in a lower-dimensional space without requiring eigendecomposition. The TR is derived theoretically, with convergence proven for two learning algorithms, and shown to support downstream tasks such as option discovery and reward shaping without eigenvector computations. Empirically, the TR matches the knowledge captured by the top eigenvector of the default representation while requiring less computational overhead to learn, store, and apply, demonstrating its viability as a practical alternative.
为了解决现有强化学习表示（如后继表示和默认表示）计算开销大、依赖对称转移动态等局限，本文提出了一种结构上不同的终端表示（TR），它以更低维度编码奖励加权轨迹，无需进行特征分解。TR具有理论推导、两种学习算法的收敛性证明，支持零样本组合性，并能与不同奖励形式等价。实验表明，TR在选项发现、奖励塑造等下游任务中表现与默认表示相当，但学习、存储和使用时计算开销更低，且其本质可直接从默认表示的首特征向量中提取，无需完整分解。
Rethinking Quantum Continual Learning with Quantum Fisher Information
Authors: Yu-Chao Hsu, Yu-Cheng Lin, Tai-Yue Li, Nan-Yow Chen, En-Jui Kuo
First: 2026-07-17T15:02:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:02:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum continual learning aims to train quantum models on sequential tasks without losing previously learned knowledge. However, variational quantum classifiers (VQCs) are prone to catastrophic forgetting under nonstationary task distributions. We propose quantum elastic weight consolidation (QEWC), a quantum Fisher information (QFI)-informed regularization method for mitigating forgetting. Unlike conventional elastic weight consolidation based on classical Fisher information (CFI), which measures parameter importance through measurement-dependent output statistics, QEWC uses QFI to quantify the intrinsic sensitivity of the parameterized quantum state. This gives an information-geometric view in which important parameters are identified by the local response of the quantum state manifold. We evaluate QEWC on VQCs trained on sequential binary classification tasks, including classical image-classification and quantum phase-classification tasks. Simulations show that sequential training without regularization causes severe forgetting, while both CFI-based EWC and QFI-based QEWC improve retention of previous tasks. Mechanistic analyses further show that the two methods impose different regularization geometries: CFI acts selectively on measurement-sensitive directions, whereas QFI imposes a denser state-geometric constraint over parameter space. Under depolarizing noise, CFI values are strongly suppressed by degraded measurement statistics, while QFI preserves a more stable sensitivity structure of the noisy parameterized quantum state. These results establish QEWC as a physically motivated approach for studying and mitigating forgetting in quantum continual learning through quantum-state geometry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于量子费舍尔信息的量子持续学习新思考
量子持续学习旨在在不丢失先前学习知识的前提下，对量子模型进行序列任务训练。然而，变分量子分类器（VQCs）在非平稳任务分布下易发生灾难性遗忘。我们提出量子弹性权重固化（QEWC），一种基于量子费舍尔信息（QFI）的正则化方法，用于缓解遗忘。与传统基于经典费舍尔信息（CFI）的弹性权重固化不同，后者通过测量依赖的输出统计量衡量参数重要性，QEWC则利用QFI量化参数化量子态的内在敏感性，从而提供信息几何视角——重要参数由量子态流形的局部响应识别。我们在VQCs上评估QEWC，应用于序列二分类任务，包括经典图像分类和量子相分类任务。仿真表明，无正则化的序列训练导致严重遗忘，而基于CFI的EWC和基于QFI的QEWC均能提升先前任务的保留能力。机制分析进一步表明，两种方法施加的正则化几何结构不同：CFI仅对测量敏感方向进行选择性约束，而QFI在参数空间中施加更密集的态几何约束。在去极化噪声下，CFI值因测量统计退化而显著被抑制，而QFI则保持噪声参数化量子态更稳定的敏感性结构。这些结果确立QEWC为一种基于量子态几何的物理驱动方法，用于研究和缓解量子持续学习中的遗忘问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of catastrophic forgetting in variational quantum classifiers during sequential learning, this work introduces quantum elastic weight consolidation (QEWC), a regularization method grounded in quantum Fisher information (QFI) to preserve prior knowledge. Unlike classical Fisher information-based EWC, which relies on measurement-dependent output statistics, QEWC quantifies parameter importance via QFI, capturing the intrinsic sensitivity of the parameterized quantum state to local changes in the state manifold. Experimental results on sequential binary classification tasks—both classical image and quantum phase classification—show that unregularized training leads to severe forgetting, while both CFI-based and QFI-based EWC methods significantly improve retention. Mechanistic analysis reveals that CFI imposes selective regularization along measurement-sensitive directions, whereas QFI enforces a denser, more robust geometric constraint across parameter space. Under depolarizing noise, CFI values degrade with measurement quality, while QFI maintains stable sensitivity, demonstrating QEWC’s physical robustness and suitability for quantum continual learning.
为解决变分量子分类器在序列任务中因非平稳分布导致的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出基于量子费舍尔信息（QFI）的量子弹性权重固化（QEWC）正则化方法。与依赖测量输出统计的经典费舍尔信息（CFI）方法不同，QEWC通过量化参数化量子态的内在敏感性，从信息几何角度识别关键参数。在图像分类与量子相分类等序列二分类任务中，实验表明无正则化训练导致严重遗忘，而CFI与QFI版本的EWC均能有效提升知识保留。机制分析显示，CFI仅在测量敏感方向施加正则化，而QEWC在参数空间中施加更密集的几何约束。在退相干噪声下，CFI因测量退化而剧烈下降，而QFI保持量子态敏感结构的稳定性，凸显QEWC在量子持续学习中的物理动机与鲁棒性。
CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: Sentiment-Augmented Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active Trading -- An Alpha-Reward Approach
Authors: Andrei Neagu, Eeham Khan, Leila Kosseim
First: 2026-07-17T15:01:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:01:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the FinMMEval Lab at CLEF 2026. Recipient of a Merit Award recognizing promising approaches and well-documented submissions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab, which requires daily long, flat, or short trading decisions for Bitcoin (BTC) and Tesla (TSLA) using news and historical market data. We formulate the problem as a discrete-action Markov Decision Process and compare four deep reinforcement learning algorithms: Policy Gradient (PG), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Deep Q-Learning (DQL), and Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG). The agents use technical indicators, cyclical calendar encodings, and daily news sentiment scores produced by LLaMA 3.2 1B. To reduce overfitting and align training with the objective of outperforming buy-and-hold, we introduce an alpha reward based on excess market return and randomize episode start dates. Hyperparameters are optimized with Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, with early stopping and model selection based on validation Sharpe ratio. On the CLEF Task 3 test set, DDPG achieves the strongest overall performance. DQL was selected a priori for the live endpoint because it obtained the highest validation Sharpe ratio, with selection performed without access to the test period. For TSLA, DDPG and DQL achieve cumulative returns of 54.96% and 52.62%, respectively, compared with 16.45% for buy-and-hold. For BTC, DDPG achieves a positive return of 1.58% while buy-and-hold declines by -34.27%. The results also reveal a substantial validation-to-test generalization gap, highlighting the difficulty of transferring policies selected in bull-market conditions to a bear-market regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 任务3：基于情感增强的深度强化学习主动交易——Alpha奖励方法
本文介绍了我们在CLEF 2026 FinMMEval实验室任务3中的系统，该任务要求利用新闻和历史市场数据，对比特币（BTC）和特斯拉（TSLA）进行每日多空或平仓交易决策。我们将问题建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程，并比较了四种深度强化学习算法：策略梯度（PG）、近端策略优化（PPO）、深度Q学习（DQL）和深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）。智能体使用技术指标、周期性日历编码以及由LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的每日新闻情绪得分。为减少过拟合并使训练目标与超越买入持有策略对齐，我们引入了基于超额市场收益的Alpha奖励，并随机化了训练序列的起始日期。超参数通过Ray Tune在每种算法-资产对组合上进行180次试验优化，采用验证夏普比率进行早期停止和模型选择。在CLEF任务3测试集上，DDPG表现最佳。DQL因在验证集上获得最高夏普比率，被预先选定用于实时端点，且选择过程未接触测试期数据。对于TSLA，DDPG和DQL的累计回报分别为54.96%和52.62%，远高于买入持有策略的16.45%；对于BTC，DDPG实现正回报1.58%，而买入持有策略下跌34.27%。结果还揭示了显著的验证-测试泛化差距，凸显了在牛市条件下选择的策略难以迁移到熊市环境的困难。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptive trading strategies using sentiment-rich financial data, this work applies deep reinforcement learning to daily trading decisions for Bitcoin and Tesla. It employs four RL algorithms—Policy Gradient, PPO, DQL, and DDPG—trained on technical indicators, calendar features, and LLaMA 3.2 1B-generated news sentiment. To mitigate overfitting and align rewards with outperformance over buy-and-hold, an alpha-based reward function and randomized episode start dates are introduced. Hyperparameters are tuned via Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, selecting models based on validation Sharpe ratio. DDPG outperformed others overall, while DQL was chosen for deployment due to its highest validation Sharpe. On test data, DQL and DDPG achieved 52.62% and 54.96% cumulative returns for TSLA versus 16.45% for buy-and-hold, and DDPG delivered a 1.58% return for BTC while buy-and-hold lost 34.27%. A significant generalization gap between validation and test performance suggests difficulty in transferring models trained in bull markets to bear markets.
为解决利用新闻和市场数据对比特币与特斯拉进行每日交易决策的挑战，本研究采用深度强化学习将问题建模为离散动作马尔可夫决策过程。四种算法——策略梯度、PPO、DQL与DDPG——基于技术指标、周期日历编码及LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的新闻情绪得分进行训练，采用基于超额收益的Alpha奖励机制以对齐“超越买入持有”目标并减少过拟合。超参数通过Ray Tune在每种算法-资产组合上进行180次试验优化，模型选择依据验证夏普比率。DDPG在测试集上表现最优，而DQL因验证夏普比率最高被选为线上端口模型。结果表明，DDPG与DQL在TSLA上分别实现54.96%与52.62%的累计收益，远超买入持有策略的16.45%；BTC上DDPG实现1.58%正收益，而买入持有则下跌34.27%。但实验也揭示了验证集与测试集间显著的泛化差距，说明在牛市环境下训练的策略难以适应熊市环境。
Why do CNNs excel at feature extraction? A mathematical explanation
Authors: Vinoth Nandakumar, Arush Tagade, Tongliang Liu
First: 2023-07-03T10:41:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T15:01:23+00:00
Comments: Requires major revision, based on feedback from journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over the past decade deep learning has revolutionized the field of computer vision, with convolutional neural network models proving to be very effective for image classification benchmarks. However, a fundamental theoretical questions remain answered: why can they solve discrete image classification tasks that involve feature extraction? We address this question in this paper by introducing a novel mathematical model for image classification, based on feature extraction, that can be used to generate images resembling real-world datasets. We show that convolutional neural network classifiers can solve these image classification tasks with zero error. In our proof, we construct piecewise linear functions that detect the presence of features, and show that they can be realized by a convolutional network.
中文标题/摘要
标题：卷积神经网络为何擅长特征提取？一个数学解释
过去十年，深度学习彻底改变了计算机视觉领域，卷积神经网络模型在图像分类基准测试中表现出色。然而，一个基本的理论问题仍未解决：为何它们能解决涉及特征提取的离散图像分类任务？本文通过引入一种基于特征提取的新型数学模型来解答此问题，该模型可用于生成类似真实世界数据集的图像。我们证明，卷积神经网络分类器可零误差解决这些图像分类任务。在证明中，我们构造了分段线性函数以检测特征的存在，并表明这些函数可由卷积网络实现。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are so effective at image classification by proposing a novel mathematical framework that models feature extraction as a core component of image classification. The authors construct piecewise linear functions designed to detect specific image features, demonstrating that these functions can be exactly realized by CNNs, which thereby achieve zero error on generated datasets mimicking real-world images. Their theoretical analysis provides a rigorous explanation for CNNs’ empirical success in feature-based visual tasks, offering insight into the mathematical structure underlying their performance.
本文旨在解释卷积神经网络（CNN）为何在图像分类任务中表现出色，填补了其成功背后的理论空白。作者提出一种基于特征提取的数学模型，生成模拟真实数据集的合成图像，并证明CNN可对这些图像实现零误差分类。其证明通过构建分段线性函数来检测特定特征，表明这些函数可由卷积层实现，从而从数学上验证了CNN提取和分类特征的能力。
Constrained Hebbian Learning Supports Efficient Representational Allocation under Structural Constraints
Authors: Patrick Inoue, Florian Röhrbein, Andreas Knoblauch
First: 2026-07-17T14:59:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:59:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Introduction: Biological systems face anatomical and metabolic constraints, including costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity. These constraints favor neural codes that compress behaviorally relevant information into low-redundancy patterns. We test whether an excitatory competitive Hebbian rule can support synaptic resource allocation under such constraints and whether the resulting representations occupy a more favorable cost-performance regime than reference learning rules. Methods: Representational cost is quantified using mutual-information-based measures derived from the Variational Information Bottleneck. Experiments use fixed audiovisual embeddings from three audiovisual benchmarks (AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100) to isolate downstream associative plasticity. Hebbian learning is compared with Dense Difference Target Propagation (DDTP) and backpropagation (BP) under matched sparsity and architectural constraints. Results: Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP in the main compressed comparisons, while reaching CTI values comparable to shallow BP with nonnegative weights. Rather than uniformly improving classification performance, Hebbian learning shifts the trade-off between task-relevant information and representational cost, yielding lower CTI at comparable functional performance in several settings. Discussion: The results indicate a cost-performance trade-off rather than uniform accuracy gains. For a given level of task-relevant information, Hebbian representations retain less input information while preserving functional performance, although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets. These findings support interpreting Hebbian learning as a mechanism for synaptic resource allocation rather than as a general strategy for maximizing audiovisual classification accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受结构约束的Hebbian学习支持高效的表征分配
引言：生物系统面临解剖和代谢约束，包括昂贵的突触维护和有限的连接性。这些约束倾向于支持将行为相关信息压缩为低冗余模式的神经编码。我们检验兴奋性竞争性Hebbian规则是否能在这些约束下支持突触资源分配，以及由此产生的表征是否比参考学习规则处于更优的成本-性能平衡。 方法：使用基于变分信息瓶颈的互信息度量量化表征成本。实验采用三个视听基准（AVE、Kinetics-Sounds、VGGSound100）的固定视听嵌入，以隔离下游关联可塑性。在匹配稀疏性和架构约束下，将Hebbian学习与密集差分目标传播（DDTP）和反向传播（BP）进行比较。 结果：在主要压缩比较中，Hebbian学习的任一任务信息成本（CTI）低于稀疏BP和DDTP，且其CTI值与非负权重浅层BP相当。Hebbian学习并非均匀提升分类性能，而是调整任务相关信息与表征成本之间的权衡，在多个场景中实现功能性能相当下更低的CTI。 讨论：结果表明存在成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。在给定任务相关信息水平下，Hebbian表征保留更少的输入信息，同时保持功能性能，尽管在部分数据集上准确率略有降低。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为突触资源分配机制，而非最大化视听分类准确性的通用策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by biological constraints such as costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity, this study investigates whether excitatory competitive Hebbian learning can efficiently allocate synaptic resources while preserving functional performance. Using mutual-information-based cost measures from the Variational Information Bottleneck, the researchers compare Hebbian learning against Dense Difference Target Propagation and backpropagation under fixed sparsity and architecture. Results show Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP, while matching shallow BP’s CTI with nonnegative weights. Although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets, Hebbian learning trades off input information retention for lower cost without sacrificing performance, suggesting it functions as a resource-efficient mechanism rather than a general accuracy maximizer.
为了解决生物系统中突触维护成本高和连接性受限的问题，本研究探讨兴奋性竞争式Hebbian学习是否能在结构约束下高效分配神经表征。方法上，通过固定音频视觉嵌入和匹配稀疏性约束，将Hebbian学习与密集差分目标传播和反向传播进行比较，使用基于互信息的变分信息瓶颈度量评估表征成本。结果表明，Hebbian学习在主要压缩比较中比稀疏反向传播和DDTP具有更低的任务信息成本（CTI），同时在多个场景下保持与浅层非负权重反向传播相当的分类性能，说明其在成本-性能权衡上更优，而非普遍提升准确率。
Energy-Efficient Federated Learning via Adaptive Encoder Freezing for MRI-to-CT Conversion: A Green AI-Guided Research
Authors: Ciro Benito Raggio, Lucia Migliorelli, Nils Skupien, Mathias Krohmer Zabaleta, Oliver Blanck, Francesco Cicone, Giuseppe Lucio Cascini, Paolo Zaffino, Maria Francesca Spadea
First: 2025-11-25T14:35:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:56:59+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Learning (FL) holds the potential to advance equality in health by enabling diverse institutions to collaboratively train deep learning (DL) models, even with limited data. However, the significant resource requirements of FL often exclude centres with limited computational infrastructure, further widening existing healthcare disparities. To address this issue, we propose a Green AI-oriented adaptive layer-freezing strategy designed to reduce energy consumption and computational load while maintaining model performance. We tested our approach using different federated architectures for Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI)-to-Computed Tomography (CT) conversion. The proposed adaptive strategy optimises the federated training by selectively freezing the encoder weights based on the monitored relative difference of the encoder weights from round to round. A patience-based mechanism ensures that freezing only occurs when updates remain consistently minimal. The energy consumption and CO2eq emissions of the federation were tracked using the CodeCarbon library. Compared to equivalent non-frozen counterparts, our approach reduced training time, total energy consumption and CO2eq emissions by up to 23%. At the same time, the MRI-to-CT conversion performance was maintained, with only small variations in the Mean Absolute Error (MAE). Notably, for three out of the five evaluated architectures, no statistically significant differences were observed, while two architectures exhibited statistically significant improvements. Our work aligns with a research paradigm that promotes DL-based frameworks meeting clinical requirements while ensuring climatic, social, and economic sustainability. It lays the groundwork for novel FL evaluation frameworks, advancing privacy, equity and, more broadly, justice in AI-driven healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自适应编码器冻结实现节能联邦学习用于MRI到CT转换：绿色AI导向的研究
联邦学习（FL）有望通过使不同机构在数据有限的情况下协作训练深度学习（DL）模型，促进医疗领域的平等。然而，FL的高资源需求常排除计算基础设施有限的中心，进一步加剧医疗不平等。为解决此问题，我们提出一种面向绿色AI的自适应层冻结策略，旨在降低能耗与计算负载同时保持模型性能。我们在不同联邦架构下测试了该方法用于磁共振成像（MRI）到计算机断层扫描（CT）的转换。该自适应策略通过监控每轮编码器权重的相对差异，选择性冻结编码器权重。基于耐心机制，仅当权重更新持续微小才执行冻结。使用CodeCarbon库追踪联邦的能耗与CO2eq排放。与等效未冻结模型相比，我们的方法将训练时间、总能耗和CO2eq排放降低了最高23%，同时保持MRI到CT转换性能，仅MAE出现小幅波动。值得注意的是，在五种评估架构中，三种无统计显著差异，两种有统计显著提升。本研究契合推动满足临床需求的DL框架同时确保气候、社会与经济可持续性的研究范式，为新型FL评估框架奠定基础，促进AI驱动医疗中的隐私、公平与更广泛的正义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational demands of federated learning that exclude resource-limited healthcare institutions, this study proposes an adaptive encoder freezing strategy to reduce energy consumption and computational load without sacrificing model performance. The method selectively freezes encoder weights in federated MRI-to-CT conversion tasks based on minimal weight updates over rounds, using a patience-based mechanism to avoid unnecessary freezing. Experimental results across five architectures show up to 23% reductions in training time, energy use, and CO2eq emissions, while maintaining comparable conversion accuracy—only minor MAE variations were observed, with two architectures showing statistically significant improvements and three showing no significant difference. The approach supports Green AI principles by enabling equitable, sustainable AI deployment in healthcare.
为解决联邦学习高计算需求导致资源匮乏医疗机构被排除在外的问题，本研究提出一种自适应编码器冻结策略，通过在MRI到CT转换任务中根据权重更新幅度动态冻结编码器参数，以降低能耗和计算负担。该方法采用耐心机制确保仅在权重更新持续微小时才冻结参数。实验表明，相比未冻结基线，该方法可使训练时间、能耗及二氧化碳当量排放降低最高23%，同时保持相近的转换精度，MAE仅小幅波动。五种架构中，两种架构显著提升，其余三种无显著差异，证明该方法在医疗AI中具备公平、可持续的潜力。
Candidate Attended Dialogue State Tracking Using BERT
Authors: Junyuan Zheng, Onkar Salvi, John Chan
Venue: AAAI poster
First: 2026-07-17T14:56:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:56:55+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 4 figures. Presented at the DSTC8 workshop, AAAI-20 (poster session)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dialogue state tracking (DST) is one of the core components in task-oriented dialogue systems. At each turn in a conversation, DST estimates the user belief or dialogue state, which is used as input for downstream modules to predict system actions and generate responses. The increasingly popular dialogue system applications like Google Assistant, Siri and Alexa need to support a large number of services and APIs, resulting in growing attention to the scalability of such systems. Especially for some domains with little or no training data, the capability of transferring existing knowledge of other domains is highly desired. In this paper, we present a novel scalable framework for multi-domain dialogue state tracking. The proposed system leverages the pretrained BERT model to achieve zero-shot generalization, making it easy to quickly adapt to new domains without additional training. The performance of our model is evaluated on recently released schema-based dialogue (SGD) dataset, showing significant improvement compared to previous baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用BERT的候选对话状态跟踪
对话状态跟踪（DST）是任务导向对话系统的核心组件之一。在对话的每一轮中，DST估计用户信念或对话状态，作为下游模块预测系统动作和生成响应的输入。随着Google Assistant、Siri和Alexa等对话系统应用的日益普及，它们需要支持大量服务和API，因此此类系统的可扩展性受到越来越多关注。尤其对于训练数据稀少或缺乏的领域，跨领域迁移现有知识的能力尤为迫切。本文提出了一种新颖的多领域对话状态跟踪可扩展框架。该系统利用预训练的BERT模型实现零样本泛化，无需额外训练即可快速适应新领域。我们在最近发布的基于模式的对话（SGD）数据集上评估了模型性能，结果显著优于先前基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable dialogue state tracking across diverse domains with limited training data, this paper introduces a BERT-based framework that enables zero-shot generalization. The method leverages pretrained BERT to encode dialogue context and candidate slots, allowing the model to adapt to new domains without retraining. Experimental results on the SGD dataset demonstrate significant performance gains over prior baselines, confirming the model's effectiveness in multi-domain settings.
为应对跨多领域对话状态跟踪中训练数据稀缺的问题，本文提出一种基于BERT的零样本泛化框架。该方法利用预训练BERT编码对话历史与候选槽位，无需重新训练即可快速适配新领域。在SGD数据集上的实验表明，该模型显著优于现有基线方法，验证了迁移学习在多领域对话状态跟踪中的有效性。
Dichotomous Diffusion Policy Optimization
Authors: Ruiming Liang, Yinan Zheng, Kexin Zheng, Tianyi Tan, Jianxiong Li, Liyuan Mao, Zhihao Wang, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Jingjing Liu, Jinqiao Wang, Xianyuan Zhan
First: 2025-12-31T16:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:56:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based policies have gained growing popularity in solving a wide range of decision-making tasks due to their superior expressiveness and controllable generation during inference. However, effectively training large diffusion policies using reinforcement learning (RL) remains challenging. Existing methods either suffer from unstable training due to directly maximizing value objectives, or face computational issues due to relying on crude Gaussian likelihood approximation, which requires a large amount of sufficiently small denoising steps. In this work, we propose DIPOLE (Dichotomous diffusion Policy improvement), a novel RL algorithm designed for stable and controllable diffusion policy optimization. We begin by revisiting the KL-regularized objective in RL, which offers a desirable weighted regression objective for diffusion policy extraction, but often struggles to balance greediness and stability. We then formulate a greedified policy regularization scheme, which naturally enables decomposing the optimal policy into a pair of stably learned dichotomous policies: one aims at reward maximization, and the other focuses on reward minimization. Under such a design, optimized actions can be generated by linearly combining the scores of dichotomous policies during inference, thereby enabling flexible control over the level of greediness.Evaluations in offline and offline-to-online RL settings on ExORL and OGBench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. We also use DIPOLE to train a large vision-language-action (VLA) model for end-to-end autonomous driving (AD) and evaluate it on the large-scale real-world AD benchmark NAVSIM, highlighting its potential for complex real-world applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：二元扩散策略优化
基于扩散的策略因其在推理过程中卓越的表达能力和可控生成能力，在解决各类决策任务中日益流行。然而，使用强化学习（RL）有效训练大型扩散策略仍具挑战性。现有方法要么因直接最大化价值目标而导致训练不稳定，要么因依赖粗糙的高斯似然近似而面临计算问题，后者需要大量足够小的去噪步骤。本文提出DIPOLE（二元扩散策略改进），一种专为稳定且可控的扩散策略优化设计的新RL算法。我们首先回顾RL中的KL正则化目标，该目标为扩散策略提取提供了理想的加权回归目标，但常难以平衡贪婪性与稳定性。随后，我们提出一种贪婪策略正则化方案，自然地将最优策略分解为一对稳定学习的二元策略：一个旨在最大化奖励，另一个则聚焦于最小化奖励。在此设计下，推理时可通过线性组合二元策略的得分生成优化动作，从而灵活控制贪婪程度。我们在ExORL和OGBench上的离线及离线到在线RL评估验证了该方法的有效性。我们还使用DIPOLE训练了一个大型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型用于端到端自动驾驶（AD），并在大规模真实世界AD基准NAVSIM上进行评估，凸显其在复杂现实应用中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable and controllable training of large diffusion policies in reinforcement learning, this work introduces DIPOLE, a novel RL algorithm that decomposes optimal policies into two dichotomous components—one maximizing reward and the other minimizing it—via a greedified policy regularization scheme. This decomposition enables stable training by avoiding direct value maximization and reduces computational burden by eliminating the need for fine-grained denoising steps. Experimental results on ExORL and OGBench show DIPOLE outperforms existing methods in both offline and offline-to-online settings, and when applied to train a vision-language-action model for autonomous driving on NAVSIM, it demonstrates strong generalization to real-world scenarios.
为解决强化学习中大型扩散策略训练不稳定和难以控制的问题，本文提出DIPOLE算法，通过将策略优化分解为两个二元策略：一个最大化奖励，另一个最小化奖励。该方法基于KL正则化目标并引入贪心策略正则化方案，使策略学习更稳定，同时允许在推理时通过线性组合两个策略来灵活调节贪心程度。在ExORL和OGBench上的实验表明其优于现有方法，且在NAVSIM自动驾驶基准上训练的视觉-语言-动作模型也展现出良好的现实世界泛化能力。
Intimacy as Service, Harm as Externality: Critical Perspectives on AI Companion Platform Accountability
Authors: Dayeon Eom, Julianne Renner, Sedona Chinn
First: 2026-04-07T19:11:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:55:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper examines artificial intelligence (AI) companionship as a site where intimate relations are simultaneously produced, extracted from, and governed through datafied systems. Drawing on critical data studies and platform studies, we challenge prevailing narratives that locate harm in user psychology rather than platform architecture. Through in-depth interviews with 20 individuals who have AI companions, we address three questions: what harms do users identify, how do they make sense of those harms, and what do their accounts reveal about the perceived distribution of responsibility among users, platforms, and regulators? Participants identified design-based harms, including unsolicited content generation and safety mechanisms that stigmatized the users they intended to protect, alongside use-based harms centered on emotional dependency they could recognize but not resolve. Users deployed individualized sensemaking strategies, including self-regulation, stigma navigation, and privacy rationalization, bearing the full burden of harm mitigation without platform support. On governance, participants described an accountability vacuum in which platforms deflected blame while users articulated conditional preferences that rejected both prohibition and deregulation. The findings extend responsibilization theory by demonstrating how platform-produced vulnerability becomes self-sustaining through the interpretive labor of users who lack structural alternatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：亲密作为服务，伤害作为外部性：对AI伴侣平台问责的批判性视角
本文探讨人工智能（AI）伴侣关系如何在数据化系统中同时生成、提取并受其治理。基于批判性数据研究与平台研究，我们挑战将伤害归因于用户心理而非平台架构的主流叙事。通过对20位AI伴侣使用者的深度访谈，我们探讨三个问题：用户识别哪些伤害、如何理解这些伤害，以及他们的叙述揭示了用户、平台与监管者之间责任分配的何种认知？参与者指出设计层面的伤害，如未经请求的内容生成及安全机制反而污名化本意为保护的用户，以及使用层面的伤害，即情感依赖——他们虽能识别却无法解决。用户采用个性化意义建构策略，包括自我调节、污名应对与隐私合理化，独自承担伤害缓解的全部负担，而平台未提供支持。在治理层面，参与者描述了平台推卸责任的问责真空，用户则表达有条件偏好，既反对禁令也反对放任。研究结果拓展了责任化理论，表明平台制造的脆弱性如何通过缺乏结构性替代方案的用户解释性劳动自我维持。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates AI companion platforms as sites where intimate relationships are shaped by data-driven systems, challenging the notion that user psychological vulnerability is the primary source of harm. Through interviews with 20 users, it identifies two types of harm—design-based (e.g., inappropriate content, stigmatizing safety features) and use-based (e.g., emotional dependency)—and reveals that users develop individual strategies like self-regulation and privacy rationalization to cope, without institutional support. The study finds users bear the full burden of harm mitigation while platforms evade accountability, and regulators are absent from meaningful governance, resulting in a self-sustaining cycle of vulnerability reinforced by users’ interpretive labor.
本文探讨AI伴侣平台如何通过数据化系统塑造亲密关系，挑战将用户伤害归因于心理脆弱性的主流观点。通过对20名用户的访谈，研究发现两类伤害：设计性伤害如不当内容生成和污名化安全机制，以及使用性伤害如情感依赖。用户通过自我调节、污名应对和隐私合理化等策略应对伤害，却独自承担全部责任，平台缺乏支持。研究揭示治理真空，平台推卸责任，用户表达介于禁止与放任之间的条件性立场，拓展了责任化理论，表明平台制造的脆弱性通过用户缺乏结构性替代的解释性劳动自我强化。
Presentation, Not Mechanism: A Render Confound in Deprecation-Aware Memory Evaluation
Authors: Zhaoyang Jiang, Zhizhong Fu, Zicheng Li, Yunsoo Kim, Jiacong Mi, Xuanqi Peng, Fei Teng, Honghan Wu
First: 2026-07-17T14:55:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:55:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI systems increasingly retrieve from records that revise themselves: issue threads, encyclopedic histories, policy logs, and long conversations. The challenge is not only finding relevant evidence, but deciding which claims remain in force, which were superseded, and when to abstain. Structured memories promise to solve this with typed edges, temporal updates, and conflict status, yet evaluations often change mechanism and prompt presentation together. We study this as Evidence-State Revision, comparing flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger on 2,907 high-agreement questions from GitHub, multi-repo issue histories, Wikipedia, and DyKnow-style temporal streams. A render-matched control (same layout, deprecation disabled) reveals the central confound: when a value is changed and later restored, RevisionLedger appears to beat a flat baseline by +0.182, but almost all the gain comes from easier presentation; the fine-grained mechanism residual is indistinguishable from zero (+0.021 to +0.025 across two judge families). After presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation is the only mechanism that pays for current-state queries, beating the fine ledger by 0.084; the same query-sufficiency principle says provenance mainly needs retained invalidated evidence, not richer typing. Memory evaluations should hold render fixed, and deprecation-aware systems should deploy the coarsest retained state that covers their queries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：呈现而非机制：在支持过时信息评估中的渲染混淆
AI系统越来越多地从自我修订的记录中检索信息：问题线程、百科全书历史、政策日志和长对话。挑战不仅在于找到相关证据，还在于判断哪些主张仍然有效、哪些已被取代，以及何时应保持沉默。结构化记忆通过类型化边、时间更新和冲突状态有望解决这一问题，但评估通常同时改变机制和提示呈现方式。我们将此称为证据状态修订，比较了平铺检索、粗粒度边无效化和细粒度修订账本在来自GitHub、多仓库问题历史、维基百科和DyKnow风格时间流的2907个高共识问题上的表现。一个渲染匹配的对照组（相同布局，禁用过时信息）揭示了核心混淆：当一个值被更改后又恢复时，修订账本相比平铺基线提升+0.182，但几乎全部收益来自更易呈现的界面；细粒度机制的残差与零无异（两个评审组间+0.021至+0.025）。在控制呈现后，粗粒度无效化是唯一能为当前状态查询带来收益的机制，优于细粒度账本0.084；同一查询充分性原则表明，溯源主要需要保留的无效证据，而非更丰富的类型化。记忆评估应固定渲染方式，而支持过时信息的系统应部署覆盖其查询的最粗粒度保留状态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI systems that handle self-revising records, the paper investigates how presentation confounds affect deprecation-aware memory mechanisms. It compares three approaches—flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger—on 2,907 high-agreement questions across diverse temporal data sources. A render-matched control reveals that RevisionLedger’s apparent advantage (+0.182) stems largely from presentation, not mechanism, with its true performance residual near zero (+0.021 to +0.025). When presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation outperforms RevisionLedger by 0.084 for current-state queries, suggesting that provenance benefits from retained invalidated evidence rather than fine-grained typing. The study concludes that memory evaluations must fix render and that deprecation-aware systems should use the coarsest retained state sufficient for their queries.
为评估AI系统处理自我修订记录的能力，本研究比较了扁平检索、粗粒度边失效和细粒度RevisionLedger三种记忆机制。实验显示，RevisionLedger在未控制呈现方式时比扁平检索高出+0.182，但此优势主要源于呈现方式而非机制本身。当呈现方式固定后，粗粒度失效机制在当前状态查询中胜出0.084，而细粒度机制无显著残差收益。研究建议记忆评估应固定呈现方式以分离机制影响，且自修订系统应采用最粗粒度的保留状态以满足查询需求。
Be Tangential to Manifold: Discovering Riemannian Metric for Diffusion Models
Authors: Shinnosuke Saito, Takashi Matsubara
First: 2025-10-07T01:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:55:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models are powerful deep generative models, but unlike classical models, they lack an explicit low-dimensional latent space that parameterizes the data manifold. This absence makes it difficult to perform manifold-aware operations, such as geometrically faithful interpolation or conditional guidance that respects the learned manifold. We propose a training-free Riemannian metric on the noise space, derived from the Jacobian of the score function. The key insight is that the spectral structure of this Jacobian separates tangent and normal directions of the data manifold; our metric leverages this separation to encourage paths to stay tangential to the manifold rather than drift toward high-density regions. To validate that our metric faithfully captures the manifold geometry, we examine it from two complementary angles. First, geodesics under our metric yield perceptually more natural interpolations than existing methods on synthetic, image, and video frame datasets. Second, the tangent-normal decomposition induced by our metric prevents classifier-free guidance from deviating off the manifold, improving generation quality while preserving text-image alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贴近流形：为扩散模型发现黎曼度量
扩散模型是强大的深度生成模型，但与经典模型不同，它们缺乏显式的低维潜在空间来参数化数据流形。这种缺失使得难以执行流形感知操作，例如几何忠实的插值或尊重所学流形的条件引导。我们提出了一种无需训练的噪声空间黎曼度量，该度量源自得分函数的雅可比矩阵。关键洞见在于，该雅可比矩阵的谱结构可分离数据流形的切向和法向方向；我们的度量利用这一分离，鼓励路径保持贴近流形而非漂向高密度区域。为验证该度量能忠实捕捉流形几何，我们从两个互补角度进行考察：首先，在合成、图像和视频帧数据集上，基于该度量的测地线插值在感知上比现有方法更自然；其次，该度量诱导的切向-法向分解可防止分类器自由引导偏离流形，从而在保持文本-图像对齐的同时提升生成质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of an explicit low-dimensional latent space in diffusion models that hinders manifold-aware operations, the paper proposes a training-free Riemannian metric on the noise space derived from the score function's Jacobian. This metric exploits the Jacobian's spectral structure to distinguish tangent and normal directions of the data manifold, encouraging diffusion paths to remain tangential and avoid drifting toward high-density regions. Experimental results show that geodesics under this metric produce more natural interpolations across synthetic, image, and video datasets, and that the tangent-normal decomposition improves classifier-free guidance by preventing deviation from the manifold, enhancing generation quality while preserving text-image alignment.
为解决扩散模型缺乏显式低维潜在空间导致的流形感知操作困难问题，本文提出一种基于分数函数雅可比矩阵谱结构的无训练Riemannian度量，引导扩散路径保持与数据流形相切。实验表明，该度量下的测地线在合成、图像和视频数据集上生成更自然的插值结果，且其切-法分解能有效约束分类器自由引导，避免偏离流形，从而提升生成质量并保持文本-图像对齐。
Intimate Strangers by Design: A Uses and Gratifications Analysis of AI Companionship
Authors: Dayeon Eom, Julianne Renner
First: 2026-04-07T19:53:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:49:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Drawing on 20 qualitative interviews with users of AI companion platforms and general-purpose chatbots, this study examines what users seek from AI companionship and how gratifications develop through sustained interaction. Through abductive qualitative content analysis, we find that familiar Uses and Gratifications categories, including emotional release, self-expression, social presence, and identity affirmation, are generated through interpersonalized affordances: availability, memory, personalization, and responsiveness interpreted as relational qualities. The study extends the Uses and Gratifications framework by showing that AI companionship gratifications are relationally produced and recursively reorganized as users' needs and expectations change over time.

This study investigates why users engage with AI companionship platforms by analyzing 20 qualitative interviews, revealing that gratifications such as emotional release and identity affirmation emerge not from inherent AI features but from interpersonalized affordances like availability and personalization, which users interpret as relational qualities. Through abductive qualitative content analysis, the research extends the Uses and Gratifications framework by demonstrating that these gratifications are dynamically restructured over time as users’ needs evolve, highlighting the recursive, user-driven nature of AI companionship.
