PIXIE: A Zero-Shot texture-invariant 6D pose estimation framework for unseen objects with assembly defects
Authors: Leon Jungemeyer, Alejandro Magaña, Gautham Mohan, Matthias Karl, Daniel Werdehausen
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-07-17T14:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:48:43+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted for publication in IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. The final published version will be available via IEEE Xplore
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
6D pose estimation remains a key challenge in robotics and computer vision, particularly in industrial environments. The deployment of currently available data-driven methods is often limited by resource-intensive data pipelines, reliance on textured 3D models, and sensitivity to geometric deviations caused by damages or assembly defects. We present PIXIE, a zero-shot framework that estimates the 6D pose of an object from an RGB image using only an untextured 3D model. Synthetic depth and normal maps are rendered from sampled reference viewpoints and matched to the query image via a pretrained cross-modality feature matcher. Matched keypoints are back-projected to obtain 2D--3D correspondences for PnP-based pose estimation. Relying exclusively on geometry makes the method inherently robust to lighting and texture variation, while correspondence filtering handles geometric deviations between the model and physical object. We evaluate on widely-used public benchmarks, reporting state-of-the-art results on texture-less objects without object-specific training, and introduce a novel dataset with assembly defects, texture variations, and occlusion to demonstrate real-world applicability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PIXIE：一种零样本、纹理不变的6D姿态估计框架，适用于存在装配缺陷的未知物体
6D姿态估计在机器人和计算机视觉领域，尤其是在工业环境中，仍是一个关键挑战。当前数据驱动方法的部署常受限于资源密集型数据管道、对纹理化3D模型的依赖，以及对因损坏或装配缺陷导致的几何偏差的敏感性。我们提出PIXIE，一种零样本框架，仅使用无纹理3D模型，从RGB图像中估计物体的6D姿态。通过采样参考视角渲染合成深度与法线图，并借助预训练的跨模态特征匹配器与查询图像匹配。匹配的关键点反投影以获得2D-3D对应关系，用于PnP姿态估计。该方法仅依赖几何信息，因而天然对光照和纹理变化鲁棒；对应关系过滤则处理模型与实物间的几何偏差。我们在广泛使用的公开基准上评估，报告了无需物体特定训练即在无纹理物体上达到最先进结果，并引入一个包含装配缺陷、纹理变化和遮挡的新型数据集，以展示其实际应用价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing 6D pose estimation methods in industrial settings—such as dependency on textured 3D models and sensitivity to object defects—PIXIE introduces a zero-shot framework that estimates 6D pose from RGB images using only untextured 3D models. It renders synthetic depth and normal maps from sampled viewpoints, matches them to the query image via a pretrained cross-modality feature matcher, and uses back-projected keypoints for PnP-based pose estimation. Experimental results show state-of-the-art performance on texture-less objects without object-specific training, and the method demonstrates robustness to assembly defects, texture variations, and occlusion on a newly introduced benchmark.
为解决现有6D姿态估计方法在工业场景中对纹理模型依赖强、易受装配缺陷影响的问题，PIXIE提出一种零样本框架，仅利用无纹理3D模型从RGB图像中估计6D姿态。该方法通过渲染参考视角的合成深度与法线图，利用预训练跨模态特征匹配器与查询图像对齐关键点，并反向投影用于PnP姿态估计。实验表明，该方法在无纹理物体上无需特定训练即达到当前最优性能，并在新引入的含装配缺陷、纹理变化与遮挡数据集上展现出对现实场景的鲁棒性。
DPNeXt: A Lightweight Multi-Scale Feature Fusion Framework for Efficient ViT-Based Multi-Task Dense Prediction
Authors: Jehun Kang, Jungha Wang, Youngjun Hwang, David Hyunchul Shim
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-17T14:46:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:46:39+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures. Accepted to the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-Task Learning (MTL) in robotics perception systems supports comprehensive 3D spatial scene understanding by integrating semantic segmentation and depth estimation. While Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) are increasingly adopted as robust feature encoders, existing decoding strategies present a critical bottleneck. To address this, we propose DPNeXt, a streamlined multi-scale feature fusion decoder and efficient alternative to the standard Dense Prediction Transformer (DPT). DPNeXt uses dual depthwise separable inverted bottlenecks to improve frozen VFM utilization through fusion-centric decoding and independent task modularization. To further mitigate negative inductive transfer between tasks, we introduce the Multi-Task Boundary Guidance (MTBG) strategy. Unlike prior boundary-aware methods that add fusion modules or gating, MTBG applies symmetric boundary-focused supervision to encourage geometric consistency without extra annotation or inference cost. Experiments on Cityscapes show that DPNeXt-S outperforms prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) MTL models, while DPNeXt-B further improves the overall performance and achieves the best results among the compared methods. On NYUv2, DPNeXt-B also achieves the best semantic segmentation and depth estimation results among the compared methods while requiring substantially fewer trainable parameters than prior large-scale MTL models. Compared with the standard DPT, DPNeXt-S reduces trainable parameters by 78.6% and achieves the fastest inference speed among the compared models on resource-constrained laptop hardware. The source code, model checkpoints, and a demo video will be made available at https://github.com/kangjehun/DPNeXt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DPNeXt：一种轻量级多尺度特征融合框架，用于高效ViT基础的多任务密集预测
机器人感知系统中的多任务学习（MTL）通过整合语义分割与深度估计，支持全面的3D空间场景理解。尽管视觉基础模型（VFMs）正日益被用作稳健的特征编码器，但现有解码策略存在关键瓶颈。为此，我们提出DPNeXt，一种简化版的多尺度特征融合解码器，也是标准密集预测Transformer（DPT）的高效替代方案。DPNeXt采用双深度可分离倒置瓶颈结构，通过以融合为中心的解码和独立任务模块化，提升冻结VFM的利用率。为进一步缓解任务间的负向归纳迁移，我们引入多任务边界引导（MTBG）策略。与以往边界感知方法需添加融合模块或门控机制不同，MTBG通过对称的边界聚焦监督，鼓励几何一致性，且无需额外标注或推理开销。在Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，DPNeXt-S优于现有最先进（SOTA）MTL模型，而DPNeXt-B进一步提升整体性能，达到对比方法中的最佳结果。在NYUv2数据集上，DPNeXt-B在语义分割与深度估计两项任务中均取得对比方法中的最佳结果，同时所需可训练参数显著少于以往大型MTL模型。与标准DPT相比，DPNeXt-S减少78.6%的可训练参数，并在资源受限的笔记本硬件上实现对比模型中最快的推理速度。源代码、模型检查点及演示视频将在https://github.com/kangjehun/DPNeXt公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient multi-task dense prediction in robotics perception, this paper introduces DPNeXt, a lightweight decoder framework that replaces the standard Dense Prediction Transformer with a multi-scale feature fusion architecture using dual depthwise separable inverted bottlenecks. This design enhances frozen VFM utilization and enables independent task modularization. To reduce negative inductive transfer, it employs Multi-Task Boundary Guidance (MTBG), which applies symmetric boundary supervision without extra annotations or inference overhead. Experimental results on Cityscapes and NYUv2 show that DPNeXt-S outperforms prior SOTA MTL models, while DPNeXt-B achieves the best overall performance and parameter efficiency, reducing trainable parameters by 78.6% compared to DPT and delivering the fastest inference speed on laptops.
为提升机器人感知中的多任务密集预测效率，本文提出DPNeXt，一种轻量级解码框架，旨在优化视觉基础模型的特征利用。该框架采用双深度可分离倒置瓶颈结构实现多尺度特征融合与任务独立模块化，并引入多任务边界引导策略，通过对称边界监督强化几何一致性，无需额外标注或推理开销。在Cityscapes与NYUv2数据集上的实验表明，DPNeXt-B在语义分割与深度估计两项任务上均达到当前最优性能，且参数量远低于以往大型多任务模型；DPNeXt-S相较标准DPT参数量减少78.6%，并在资源受限笔记本上实现最快推理速度。
AI Watermark Evidence Fails Forensic Readiness: An Empirical Evaluation
Authors: Saifur Rahman Tamim, Amir Labib Khan
Venue: AAAI
First: 2026-07-17T14:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:45:53+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures. A version of this paper was submitted to the AAAI/ACM Conference on AI, Ethics, and Society (AIES) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Governments are increasingly mandating that LLM-generated content carry watermarks. The EU AI Act calls for markings that are "sufficiently reliable and robust." California's SB 942 requires disclosure that is "permanent or extraordinarily difficult to remove." Both mandates rest on an untested assumption: that watermark detection yields evidence reliable enough for courts. This paper tests that assumption directly. We evaluate three representative LLM watermarking methods -- KGW, Unigram, and the MarkLLM implementation of SynthID-Text -- against the Daubert admissibility criteria and the NIST SP 800-86 digital forensic process. To structure this evaluation, we propose a Forensic Readiness Score (FRS) framework with 12 criteria, three mandatory gates, and a 60-point scoring system. We focus on meaning-preserving paraphrase as the attack vector, since it is both legally realistic and difficult to dismiss as evidence tampering. The results raise serious evidentiary concerns. Out of 846 valid paraphrase runs across 15 diverse prompts per method, every single initially-detected KGW and Unigram text lost its watermark after paraphrasing -- 100% conditional removal. SynthID fared only slightly better at 98.3%. Even before any attack, false-negative rates were already high: 70% for KGW, 83% for Unigram, 80% for SynthID. The SynthID configuration also flagged 5.4% of paraphrased human-written controls as AI-generated and showed an 18.6% paradox rate, with 80% of its own pristine watermarked output landing in the uncertainty deadband. None of the three methods satisfy more than two of five Daubert factors. We also find that the FRS point-based scoring system, despite working as designed, cannot fully capture forensic uselessness -- a limitation worth noting for future framework design. These configurations, as tested, do not meet the evidentiary bar that courts require.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI水印证据未能满足法证准备性：实证评估
各国政府正越来越多地要求由大语言模型生成的内容必须带有水印。欧盟《人工智能法案》要求水印“足够可靠且稳健”，加州SB 942则要求披露信息“永久或极难移除”。这两项规定均基于一个未经验证的假设：即水印检测结果足以作为法庭证据。本文直接检验该假设。 我们评估了三种代表性LLM水印方法——KGW、Unigram及SynthID-Text的MarkLLM实现——依据Daubert可采信标准与NIST SP 800-86数字取证流程。为结构化评估，我们提出一套“法证准备性评分（FRS）”框架，包含12项标准、三个强制关卡及60分评分体系。我们聚焦于语义保持型改写作为攻击向量，因其在法律上现实且难以被驳斥为证据篡改。 结果引发严重证据疑虑：在每种方法下，针对15个多样化提示进行846次有效改写测试中，所有最初被检测出的KGW与Unigram文本在改写后均丢失水印——100%条件移除。SynthID表现稍好，为98.3%。即使在未受攻击前，假阴性率已很高：KGW为70%，Unigram为83%，SynthID为80%。SynthID配置还误将5.4%的人类撰写控制文本标记为AI生成，并出现18.6%的悖论率，其中80%的自身原始水印输出落入不确定性区间。三种方法均未满足Daubert五项标准中的两项以上。我们亦发现，尽管FRS点数评分体系按设计运行，仍无法完全捕捉法证无用性——此局限性值得未来框架设计时注意。 经测试，这些配置未能达到法庭所需的证据门槛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by legal mandates requiring reliable AI watermarks for LLM-generated content, this study empirically evaluates three watermarking methods—KGW, Unigram, and SynthID-Text—against forensic standards including Daubert admissibility and NIST SP 800-86. Using a Forensic Readiness Score framework with 12 criteria, the researchers tested watermark persistence under meaning-preserving paraphrase attacks, a realistic legal scenario. Results show all KGW and Unigram watermarks vanished after paraphrasing (100% conditional removal), SynthID fared slightly better at 98.3% removal rate. False negatives were high even before attacks: 70% for KGW, 83% for Unigram, 80% for SynthID. SynthID also misclassified human text and exhibited a paradox rate, with 80% of its own watermarked outputs falling into uncertainty. None of the methods met more than two of five Daubert factors, and the scoring system failed to fully capture forensic irrelevance, indicating current watermarking techniques are not court-admissible.
鉴于政府对LLM生成内容需加水印的法律要求，本文实证评估了三种代表性水印方法（KGW、Unigram、SynthID-Text）在Daubert可采性标准和NIST SP 800-86数字取证流程下的可靠性。研究采用包含12项标准的“取证就绪评分”框架，以语义保留改写攻击作为测试向量（法律上现实且难以被认定为篡改）。结果显示，所有KGW和Unigram水印在改写后均被移除（100%条件移除），SynthID仅略好于98.3%。在未攻击前，假阴性率已很高：KGW为70%，Unigram为83%，SynthID为80%。SynthID还误判5.4%的人类文本为AI生成，且出现18.6%的悖论率，其中80%的自身水印文本落入不确定性区间。三种方法均未满足五个Daubert标准中的两个以上，且评分框架虽按设计运行，却未能完全捕捉其取证无效性——表明这些水印未达到法庭证据标准。
Beyond Unfolding: 60x Faster One-Stage Unmixing for Closely-Spaced Infrared Small Targets
Authors: Ximeng Zhai, Zheng Wang, Yaohong Chen, Hao Wang, Ming-Ming Cheng, Yimian Dai
First: 2026-07-17T14:42:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-17T14:42:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Due to the optical diffraction limit and long imaging distances, Closely-Spaced Infrared Small Targets (CSIST) typically exhibit energy overlap, manifesting as indistinguishable blobs in infrared images. This ambiguity invalidates the one-to-one mapping assumption of traditional detection, thereby necessitating a paradigm shift towards CSIST Unmixing, which decomposes these blobs into discrete sub-targets. However, the dominant paradigm deep unfolding networks are shackled by the high latency and structural inflexibility intrinsic to their repetitively iterative architecture. To this end, we propose the Fast One-stage CSIST Unmixing Scheme (FOCUS), a one-stage lightweight paradigm which demonstrates that deep unfolding is not necessary. Motivated by the key observation that image super-resolution (SR) and CSIST Unmixing share an isomorphic degradation model, our insight is that it is possible to achieve a paradigm shift from image SR to CSIST Unmixing via completely transforming the label space, loss functions, and evaluation criteria. Specifically, to avoid entangling geometric recovery with artifact suppression, FOCUS adopts a single pass mapping with an internal coarse-to-fine flow that progressively refines target localization from coarse spatial distributions to finer sub-pixel precision. While sparsity regularization suppresses background clutter, it also attenuates target intensities. To compensate for this attenuation of valid signals, flux conservation is introduced as a competing constraint that restores signal energy back to target centers. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first attempt to address this task via a lightweight one-stage framework without the DUN paradigm. Experiments demonstrate that our method matches or surpasses the state-of-the-art unfolding approaches in both localization and unmixing accuracy, while boosting the inference speed by 60x.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越展开：面向紧密间距红外小目标的60倍加速单阶段解混方法
由于光学衍射极限和长成像距离，紧密间距红外小目标（CSIST）常出现能量重叠，在红外图像中表现为难以区分的斑块。这种模糊性破坏了传统检测方法所依赖的一对一映射假设，因而亟需转向CSIST解混范式，将这些斑块分解为离散子目标。然而，主流的深度展开网络受限于其重复迭代架构固有的高延迟与结构僵化。为此，我们提出轻量级单阶段CSIST解混方案（FOCUS），证明深度展开并非必要。受图像超分辨率（SR）与CSIST解混共享同构退化模型的关键观察启发，我们提出通过完全转换标签空间、损失函数与评估标准，实现从图像SR到CSIST解混的范式转变。具体而言，为避免几何恢复与伪影抑制相互纠缠，FOCUS采用单次映射结合内部粗到精的渐进式流程，逐步从粗空间分布精化至亚像素级目标定位。虽然稀疏正则化可抑制背景杂波，但也削弱目标强度。为补偿有效信号衰减，引入通量守恒作为竞争性约束，将信号能量恢复至目标中心。据我们所知，本工作是首次尝试在无需DUN范式的情况下，通过轻量级单阶段框架解决该任务。实验表明，我们的方法在定位与解混精度上达到或超越当前最优展开方法，同时推理速度提升60倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of energy overlap in infrared images of closely-spaced small targets, which breaks traditional detection assumptions, this work proposes FOCUS, a one-stage lightweight framework that replaces slow iterative deep unfolding networks. By recognizing the isomorphic degradation model between image super-resolution and CSIST unmixing, FOCUS redefines the label space, loss functions, and evaluation metrics to enable direct unmixing without iterative refinement. It employs a coarse-to-fine internal flow for spatial localization and introduces flux conservation to counteract signal attenuation from sparsity regularization. Experimental results show FOCUS achieves performance comparable or superior to state-of-the-art unfolding methods while accelerating inference by 60x.
为了解决红外图像中近距离小目标因能量重叠导致的传统检测失效问题，本文提出FOCUS，一种无需深度展开网络的单阶段轻量级解混框架。通过发现图像超分辨率与目标解混在退化模型上的同构性，FOCUS重构标签空间、损失函数与评估标准，实现直接子目标分解。其采用由粗到细的内部流进行空间精化，并引入能量守恒约束以补偿稀疏正则化导致的目标信号衰减。实验表明，FOCUS在定位与解混精度上不逊于当前最优展开方法，推理速度提升60倍。
